### [1] [Primordial or Post-Inflationary: Distinguishing the Origin of Scale-Dependent Features in the CMB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08585)
*Matteo Braglia,Wenzer Qin,Xingang Chen,Tracy R. Slatyer*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文研究宇宙微波背景（Cosmic Microwave Background, CMB）各向异性中“原初特征”与“后暴胀特征”两类超越标准模型信号的区分度，发现二者之间不存在显著简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）物理无论源于原初时期还是暴胀后时期，都可能在CMB各向异性中留下尺度依赖信号，需判断这两大类信号能否被相互混淆。

Method: 原初特征限于“尖锐型（sharp）”与“共振型（resonant）”；后暴胀特征取两类定性不同的模型——奇异能量注入（exotic energy injection）与早期暗能量（early dark energy, EDE）。通过主成分分析（principal component analysis）与全似然分析（full likelihood analysis）评估简并程度。

Result: 原初特征可在功率谱中留下频率与跑动（running）近乎任意的振荡印记，而后暴胀模型产生的图样定性不同，通常无法再现原初特征振荡的典型跑动；仅用最主要主成分即显示无明显简并，全似然分析中单独或组合模型的后期分布（posterior）也无显著变化。

Conclusion: 两类信号在所考察范围内可区分，且所用方法可推广至更真实的信号或其他类型的信号。

Abstract: Physics beyond the Standard Model, whether of primordial or post-inflationary origin, can leave scale-dependent signals in the anisotropies of the Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB). In this work, we study how distinguishable these broad classes of signals are and quantify their level of degeneracy. In this initial investigation, for primordial features, we limit ourselves to those of the ``sharp'' and ``resonant'' kinds; for post-inflationary features, we consider two qualitatively distinct models: exotic energy injection, which deposits energy into the primordial plasma and modifies the global thermodynamic history, and early dark energy (EDE), which affects the CMB through the background expansion and gravitational perturbations. By studying representative examples, we find that while primordial features can leave oscillatory signatures in the CMB power spectrum with essentially arbitrary frequency and running, the post-inflationary models considered here produce qualitatively different patterns that cannot generically reproduce the characteristic running of primordial feature oscillations. We verify these findings first with a principal component analysis, and show using only the most significant principal components that there are no significant degeneracies between the primordial and post-inflationary features considered here, and secondly with a full likelihood analysis, which shows that the resulting posteriors do not change significantly when considering individual classes of models or combinations of primordial/post-inflationary features. While the two post-inflationary models probe distinct physical mechanisms and both are implemented in a simplified, phenomenological fashion, the methods used in this work can be generalized to include more realistic signals or other classes of signals.

</details>


### [2] [Primordial non-Gaussianity as a large-scale cosmic-web bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09362)
*Kilian Peraza Vera,Francisco-Shu Kitaura,Francesco Sinigaglia*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文将局域型原初非高斯性引发的尺度依赖偏置（scale-dependent bias）重新表述为一种大尺度宇宙网环境偏置（cosmic-web environmental bias），通过 Hessian 场定义的四种大尺度环境权重再现了示踪偏置的 k^{-2} 上升，并可对无 PNG 的暗晕星表进行后处理注入等效 f_NL 信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初非高斯性（primordial non-Gaussianity）是研究暴胀物理（inflation）的独特探针，但其大尺度结构信号出现在最大、受宇宙方差（cosmic variance）限制最强的尺度上，难以测量；标准图景下的局域型信号被处理为各向同性的偏置修正。

Method: 受 Kitaura 与 Sinigaglia 的谱宇宙网层级（spectral cosmic-web hierarchy）启发，定义 Hessian 场 H_ij(x)=∂_i∂_j∇^{-4}δ(x)，其迹等于 ∇^{-2}δ，携带与 Dalal 等尺度依赖偏置项相同的 k^{-2} 增强；对 H_ij 对角化得到四种大尺度宇宙网环境并各赋一个权重，利用 AbacusPNG 局域 PNG N 体模拟（f_NL=-100,-30,30,100）进行检验。

Result: 四种环境权重能够再现示踪偏置在大尺度上 k^{-2} 形式的上升；此外，不含 PNG 的既有暗晕星表经后处理可被赋予等效的 f_NL 特征。

Conclusion: 该宇宙网响应不仅可用于模拟生成，还启发了一种基于示踪体在大尺度环境中占据情况相对匹配的高斯参考态变化的观测统计量，可作为传统低 k 功率谱（power spectrum）的补充。

Abstract: Primordial non-Gaussianity is a unique probe of the physics of inflation, but its large-scale-structure signature is difficult to measure because it appears on the largest, most cosmic-variance-limited modes. The standard view of local-type primordial non-Gaussianity is isotropic: it induces a scale-dependent correction to tracer bias, $Δb(k)\propto f_{\rm NL}k^{-2}$, on large scales. Here we show that the same large-scale response can be reformulated as an anisotropic, nonlocal environmental-bias effect. Motivated by the spectral cosmic-web hierarchy of Kitaura and Sinigaglia, we define a Hessian field $\mathcal{H}_{ij}(\bf{x})\equiv\partial_i\partial_j\nabla^{-4}δ(\bf{x})$, whose trace obeys $\mathrm{Tr}\mathcal{H}=\nabla^{-2}δ$ and therefore carries the same $k^{-2}$ enhancement that appears in the Dalal et al. scale-dependent-bias term. Diagonalizing $\mathcal{H}_{ij}$ defines four large-scale web environments, analogous to knots, filaments, sheets, and voids, although operating on scales much larger than the conventional nonlinear cosmic web. We assign one weight to each environment and test the construction with the AbacusPNG suite of local-PNG $N$-body simulations, using $f_{\rm NL}=\{-100,-30,30,100\}$. We show that these four weights reproduce the large-scale $k^{-2}$ rise of the tracer bias. We further show that existing halo catalogs without PNG can be post-processed to imprint an effective $f_{\rm NL}$ signature. Beyond mock generation, the resulting cosmic-web response motivates an observational statistic based on changes in tracer occupation of large-scale environments relative to a matched Gaussian reference, complementary to the conventional low-$k$ power spectrum.

</details>


### [3] [Does DESI point towards transient cosmic acceleration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09743)
*Muhammad Ahmad,Saddam Hussain,Ayodeji Ibitoye,Yi'an Zhao,Ming Zhang,Lang Cui*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究一种围绕 w=-1 振荡的暗能量状态方程，其含正比于 e 折数立方的源项，导致高红移振幅偏向 w<-1、未来偏向 w>-1，从而给出"瞬时加速"宇宙。结合 Planck、DESI BAO 和三类超新星数据做贝叶斯证据比较，该模型相对 ΛCDM 证据不显著（Pantheon+ 与 Union3 不确定，DES-Dovekie 弱证据）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究暗能量状态方程（equation of state）偏离宇宙学常数 w=-1 的振荡行为及其对宇宙膨胀历史的影响，并检验此类动力学暗能量（dynamical dark energy）模型相对标准 ΛCDM 模型的观测支持程度。

Method: 构建含源项的振荡状态方程，源项正比于 e 折数（number of e-folds）的立方；将该模型与 BellDE、CPL 及平坦 ΛCDM 模型对比，联合 Planck、DESI BAO 与 Pantheon+、Union3、DES-Dovekie 三类超新星（supernova）数据拟合，并以对数证据（log-evidence）进行贝叶斯比较。

Result: 动力学暗能量模型给出 H0 约 66.0-67.84 km/s/Mpc、Ωm 约 0.3067-0.3271，与基准模型一致，仅 Union3 组合下 Ωm 轻微上移。BellDE 证据相对最强（Pantheon+ 弱证据，Union3 与 DES-Dovekie 中等证据）；含源振荡模型对 Pantheon+ 与 Union3 为不确定证据，对 DES-Dovekie 为弱证据。

Conclusion: 源项使振荡振幅在高红移偏向 w<-1、未来偏向 w>-1，导致暗能量密度在未来稀释、哈勃参数（Hubble parameter）渐近趋于零，当前加速膨胀是瞬时的，宇宙最终趋于减速并渐近进入静态而非收缩。观测数据尚不足以显著区分该振荡模型与 ΛCDM。

Abstract: We investigate an oscillatory equation of state for dark energy that oscillates around the mean value $-1$ and includes a source term proportional to the cube of the number of e-folds. Due to this source term, the oscillation amplitude shifts towards the $w_{\rm de}<-1$ regime at higher redshifts, whereas in the future, the source term drives the oscillation amplitude towards the $w_{\rm de}>-1$ regime. As the equation of state enters the $w_{\rm de}>-1$ regime in the future, the dark energy density dilutes, causing the Hubble parameter to decrease asymptotically towards zero, so that the present acceleration is transient: the Universe transitions to decelerated expansion and approaches a static state as $a\to\infty$, without ever contracting. We compare this equation of state with BellDE, CPL and flat $Λ$CDM. We confront the model with a combination of datasets, including Planck, DESI BAO and three different supernova compilations: Pantheon+, Union3 and DES-Dovekie. We compare the log-evidence of each model with that of $Λ$CDM. The dynamical dark energy models yield $H_0$ values in the range $66.0^{+0.80}_{-0.79}$--$67.84^{+0.69}_{-0.74}\,{\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$, with matter density values in the range $Ω_{\rm m}=0.3067^{+0.0073}_{-0.0072}$--$0.3271^{+0.0083}_{-0.0080}$ across all the combinations. These values are consistent with those of the baseline model, apart from a mild upward shift in $Ω_{\rm m}$ for the Union3 combination. Among all the models, BellDE yields comparatively stronger Bayesian evidence than the other models, with weak evidence for the Pantheon+ compilation and moderate evidence for the Union3 and DES-Dovekie compilations. The sourced oscillatory model yields inconclusive evidence for Pantheon + and Union3, while weak evidence for DES-Dovekie relative to $Λ$CDM.

</details>

### [4] [On the Volume Conjectures for Dehn-filled $3$-manifolds along fundamental shadow link complements with big volume](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08517)
*Yunpeng Meng,Baojun Wu,Tian Yang,Yuxuan Yang*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 该文证明了在体积足够接近基本影子链环补空间（fundamental shadow link complement）体积的条件下，通过整系数 Dehn 填充（integral Dehn-filling）得到的闭可定向双曲三维流形满足体积猜想（Volume Conjecture）与渐近展开猜想（Asymptotic Expansion Conjecture），并借助扩展量子 6j 符号（extended quantum 6j-symbols）及其弱反射对称性（Weak Reflection Symmetry）完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体积猜想与渐近展开猜想是量子拓扑（quantum topology）中的核心问题，已有结果多针对特定链环或流形；本文旨在将结论推广到由基本影子链环补空间经整系数 Dehn 填充获得的闭可定向双曲三维流形，以及在体积接近条件下经 change-of-pair 操作得到的配对。

Method: 证明的关键是引入扩展量子 6j 符号（extended quantum 6j-symbols）及其满足的弱反射对称性（Weak Reflection Symmetry）；该对称性通过与 b-6j 符号（b-6j symbols）建立联系而得到，所用工具为实 Gevrey-1 估计（real Gevrey-1 estimate）与截断 Borel-Laplace 变换（truncated Borel-Laplace transform）。

Result: 在体积足够接近补空间体积的假设下，证明了对应闭可定向双曲三维流形的体积猜想与渐近展开猜想；更一般地，对基本影子链环补空间上经 change-of-pair 操作得到的配对，在相应双曲锥流形（hyperbolic cone manifold）体积足够接近补空间体积时，所猜想的渐近展开成立。

Conclusion: 结果为体积猜想与渐近展开猜想提供了一类新的、基于基本影子链环与 Dehn 填充的成立范围，并表明扩展量子 6j 符号及其弱反射对称性可作为研究量子不变量（quantum invariants）渐近行为的有力工具。

Abstract: We prove the Volume Conjecture and the Asymptotic Expansion Conjecture for closed oriented hyperbolic $3$-manifolds obtained by integral Dehn-fillings along a fundamental shadow link complement, provided that their volumes are sufficiently close to that of the complement. More generally, we show that the conjectured asymptotic expansions hold for pairs obtained by change-of-pair operations on a fundamental shadow link complement whenever the volume of the corresponding hyperbolic cone manifold is sufficiently close to that of the complement. A key ingredient in the proof is the introduction of extended quantum $6j$-symbols and a Weak Reflection Symmetry satisfied by them. The Weak Reflection Symmetry is established by relating the extended quantum $6j$-symbols to the $b$-$6j$ symbols through a real Gevrey-$1$ estimate and a truncated Borel-Laplace transform.

</details>

### [5] [The Koto II experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08514)
*Silvia Martellotti*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: KOTO II 是 J-PARC 提出的下一代实验，目标在 2030 年代将 \(K_L \rightarrow \pi^0\nu\bar\nu\) 分支比测量灵敏度提升至 \(10^{-12}\) 以下，逼近标准模型预言值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: \(K_L \rightarrow \pi^0\nu\bar\nu\) 在标准模型（Standard Model）中高度压低，预言分支比为 \(3\times10^{-11}\)、理论不确定度仅 2%，对新物理（new physics）极为敏感，因此是味物理（flavour physics）的黄金模式（golden mode）。

Method: J-PARC 的 KOTO 实验利用 2021 年采集的数据搜寻该衰变并给出分支比上限；KOTO II 为 2024 年提出的下一代实验方案，旨在进一步提升测量灵敏度。

Result: KOTO 基于 2021 年数据给出迄今最严格的上限：90% 置信水平（confidence level）下分支比上限为 \(2.2\times10^{-9}\)。

Conclusion: KOTO II 计划于 2030 年代实现低于 \(10^{-12}\) 的分支比测量灵敏度，从而深入检验标准模型并探索新物理。

Abstract: $K_L \rightarrow π^0ν\barν$ is a golden mode for flavour physics: it is highly suppressed in the Standard Model, with a predicted branching ratio of $3 \times 10^{-11}$ and a theoretical uncertainty of just 2\%, and is therefore extremely sensitive to new physics. The KOTO experiment at J-PARC is searching for this decay. Using the data taken in 2021, it has set the most stringent upper limit to date of $2.2 \times 10^{-9}$ on the branching ratio at 90\% C.L. We present KOTO II, a next-generation experiment at J-PARC proposed in 2024, which aims to measure the $K_L \rightarrow π^0ν\barν$ branching ratio with a sensitivity below $10^{-12}$ in the 2030s.

</details>


### [6] [GeoRead: Geometry-Aware Transformer for Continuous AC-LGAD Readout Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08694)
*Lixing Wang,Leena Diehl,Spencer Ellis,Marcos Fernández García,Gabriele Giacomini,Vagelis Gkougkousis,Ben Kilminster,Jingyu Luo,Anna Macchiolo,Yajna Mahabeer,Yevhenii Padniuk,Daria Senina,Samantha Sunnarborg,Jessica Tang,Alessandro Tricoli,Don C. Wong,Gaetano Barone*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: GeoRead：一种时空 Transformer，利用相对焊盘位置注意力与时间抖动/事件混合增广，实现 AC-LGAD 的多事件连续位置预测及不确定性估计，中位位置误差达 5.8 μm（凸包内）与 8.2 μm（全扫描区）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AC 耦合低增益雪崩二极管（AC-coupled Low-Gain Avalanche Diodes, AC-LGADs）的信号传播难以解析求解，故需数据驱动的机器学习重建；但现有方法假设固定读出焊盘布局，且仅从孤立、时间对齐的波形窗口中预测单一命中，限制了对不同传感器设计的灵活性与连续读出场景（到达时间未知、多命中重叠）的适用性。

Method: 提出 GeoRead——一种时空 Transformer（spatial-temporal Transformer），将焊盘相对位置（relative pad positions）融入注意力机制，输出具有预测不确定性（predictive uncertainties）的时间分辨命中位置估计；并采用时间抖动（time-jitter）与加性事件混合（additive event-mixing）增广，以单事件测量合成时间失配与重叠波形进行训练。

Result: 基于 500 μm 间距 AC-LGAD 的四通道瞬态电流技术（transient-current-technique, TCT）数据，在 190 V 下，读出焊盘中心凸包（convex hull）内中位位置误差为 5.8 μm，全扫描区域为 8.2 μm；模型在时间偏移下保持相近精度，并可在无预设单事件窗口条件下实现叠加事件的时间分辨重建。

Conclusion: 结果表明，所提方法为将基于波形的位置重建推广至连续读出（continuous readout）与灵活传感器几何结构（flexible sensor geometries）提供了基础。

Abstract: AC-coupled Low-Gain Avalanche Diodes (AC-LGADs) are promising silicon sensor technologies for high-precision four-dimensional tracking. Position reconstruction in AC-LGADs relies on correlations among waveforms induced on multiple readout pads, where analytically solving the signal propagation becomes difficult, motivating data-based machine learning reconstruction. Existing machine learning approaches generally assume a fixed readout pad layout and predict one hit from an isolated, temporally aligned waveform window. These assumptions limit their flexibility across sensor designs and applicability to continuous readout where signal arrival times are not known in advance and multiple hits might overlap in the same waveforms. We introduce GeoRead, a spatial-temporal Transformer enabling multi-event continuous position prediction and uncertainty estimation. We incorporate relative pad positions into attention and produces time-resolved hit position estimates with predictive uncertainties. Time-jitter and additive event-mixing augmentations are used to train the model on temporally misaligned and overlapping waveforms synthesized from single-event measurements. Using four-channel transient-current-technique (TCT) data of a 500\mum-pitch AC-LGAD, GeoRead achieves median position errors of 5.8\mum within the convex hull of the readout-pad centers and 8.2\mum over the full scanned region at 190\volt. The model maintains comparable accuracy under temporal shifts and demonstrates time-resolved reconstruction of superposed events, without predefined per-event window. These results provide a basis for extending waveform-based position reconstruction toward continuous readout and flexible sensor geometries.

</details>


### [7] [Kinematic Reconstruction of Top-Quark Pairs Using Analytical and Machine-Learning Methods with CMS Open Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09797)
*V. Aushev,M. Spitsyn,R. Yermolenko,O. Zenaiev*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文针对双轻子（dilepton）$t\bar{t}$ 衰变道中因两个中微子（neutrino）不可探测导致的重建困难，在CMS开放数据（CMS Open Data）蒙特卡罗样本上比较现有运动学重建方法，提出改进的LKRv2与LKRv3，并开发基于残差多层感知机（residual multilayer perceptron）与条件归一化流（conditional normalizing flow）的两种机器学习（machine-learning）方法，显著提升重建分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双轻子 $t\bar{t}$ 衰变道存在两个未被探测的中微子（neutrino），使顶夸克对（top-quark-pair）运动学重建本质上不适定，现有CMS运动学重建方法的分辨率受限，需系统评估并改进。

Method: 基于7 TeV质心能量（centre-of-mass energy）的CMS开放数据 $t\bar{t}$ 蒙特卡罗样本，研究CMS现有运动学重建方法；通过对进入重建的喷注-轻子配对（jet-lepton pairing）施加更严格条件得到LKRv2，并利用双轻子与中微子对系统间的运动学相似性得到LKRv3；另构建残差多层感知机与条件归一化流两种机器学习方案。

Result: LKRv3相对基线LKR将 $t\bar{t}$ 不变质量（invariant mass）分辨率提升约7%，横向动量（transverse momentum）与快度（rapidity）提升较小；机器学习方法相对LKRv3将不变质量、横向动量与快度分辨率分别提升约30%、20%与15%，在产生阈值（production threshold）附近不变质量分辨率达30 GeV。

Conclusion: 专门的生成器层面（generator-level）研究与生成器到探测器（generator-to-detector）迁移检验表明，机器学习方法学到的是对 $ν\barν$ 近似的真实物理驱动修正，而非仅利用探测器效应；机器学习在双轻子 $t\bar{t}$ 重建上潜力显著，而中微子对近似（neutrino-pair approximation）被识别为可达分辨率的重要限制。

Abstract: The reconstruction of the top-quark-pair ($t\bar{t}$) kinematics in the dilepton $t\bar{t}$ decay channel is challenging due to the presence of two undetected neutrinos. In this work, existing kinematic reconstruction methods used by the CMS Collaboration are studied using a Monte Carlo sample with $t\bar{t}$ events from CMS Open Data at a centre-of-mass energy of 7 TeV. An improved version of the loose kinematic reconstruction, LKRv2, is developed by imposing more stringent requirements on the jet-lepton pairing entering the reconstruction. A further modification, LKRv3, exploits the kinematic similarity between the dilepton and neutrino-pair systems and improves the resolution of the reconstructed $t\bar{t}$ invariant mass relative to the baseline LKR by $7\%$, with smaller improvements for the transverse momentum and rapidity of $t\bar{t}$. In addition, two machine-learning (ML) based approaches are developed using a residual multilayer perceptron and a conditional normalizing flow. Relative to LKRv3, they improve the resolution of the invariant mass, transverse momentum and rapidity by approximately $30\%$, $20\%$ and $15\%$, respectively. In particular, a resolution of 30 GeV is achieved for the reconstructed invariant mass of $t\bar{t}$ near the production threshold. A dedicated generator-level study and a generator-to-detector transfer test demonstrate that the ML approaches learn a genuine physics-driven correction to the $ν\barν$ approximation, rather than merely exploiting detector-specific effects. These results demonstrate the strong potential of ML-based approaches for improving the reconstruction of dilepton $t\bar{t}$ events, while identifying the neutrino-pair approximation as an important limitation on the achievable resolution.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔法（magic，又称nonstabilizerness）是通用量子计算（universal quantum computation）所依赖的量子资源，其在高能与核物理中的应用几乎只使用SRE，但魔法并无唯一度量，因此需要考察不同度量是否给出一致的物理结论。

Conclusion: 该差异可归因于基于优化的度量的非解析结构（non-analytic structure），因此从魔法极值化（extremizing magic）导出的物理结论可能依赖于度量选择。

Abstract: Magic, or nonstabilizerness, is the quantum resource behind universal quantum computation. Its applications in high-energy and nuclear physics rely almost exclusively on the stabilizer Rényi entropy (SRE), although magic has no unique measure. For two qubits, we compare the SRE with three measures defined through optimizations over stabilizer states: the min- and max-relative entropies of magic and the logarithmic free robustness. We determine the maximal-magic states of all four measures, with exact proofs, and find that they depend on the measure. In the determination of the weak mixing angle from minimal magic in Møller scattering, the min-relative entropy gives an energy-dependent value that differs from that of the SRE, whereas the max-relative entropy and the logarithmic free robustness give $\sin^2θ_W=1/4$ at all energies. We trace this difference to the non-analytic structure of the optimization-based measures. Physical conclusions drawn from extremizing magic can thus depend on the measure.

Abstract: Perfect wave transfer (PWT) reproduces spatial reflection at a common finite time under a fixed Hamiltonian. In the single-charge model, the first two notions coincide. Global transfer additionally requires rephasing of the compact zero mode. This spectral condition is also sufficient when the Robin boundary data induced by the physical profile are included. Its transfer parameters are precisely the reduced fractions $r=p/q$ with odd numerator and denominator, with shortest transfer time $pL/v_0$. Occupation- and charge-constrained error estimates quantify robustness, while an exact homogeneous-chain calculation explains the $J^{2/3}$ resolved-band scale also observed in the engineered channels. These results provide a spectral design method and finite-device benchmarks for quadratic quantum channels. An interacting microscopic realization remains open.

The interface between gravity, described by general relativity, and quantum mechanics, which describes the microscopic world, is of particular interest. From a theory standpoint, these two major descriptions of nature have not been combined in a satisfactory manner. Technology is gradually becoming mature enough such that probing gravity at the microscopic scale, where quantum mechanics and gravity potentially meet, is becoming accessible. </details>

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) are a hallmark of non-Hermitian physics, giving rise to extreme sensitivity and enhanced dynamic response. That is not necessarily the case in the quantum regime, where this kind of behavior around EPs, to a large extent, remains elusive. In fact, the interplay between dissipation and quantum fluctuations makes the complete Lindbladian evolution quite different from effective non-Hermitian Hamiltonian descriptions. In this context, we investigate the signatures of the Liouvillian EPs on spectral and dynamic response by considering properly normalized long-time averages and imbalances. Even though our approach is generic, we focus on two paradigmatic models-a lossy Bose-Hubbard lattice and a dissipative two-spin system, where we investigate the role of quantum jumps and interactions in the Liouvillian spectrum, in terms of complex and structured pseudospectra. Our results demonstrate how non-Hermitian phenomena emerge in realistic quantum lattices, providing a bridge between classical EP physics and experimentally accessible open quantum systems.

  <summary>Details</summary>

However, existing proposals for time crystals in open quantum systems require an external supply of thermodynamic resources, such as work or energetic coherence. Whether coupling to an environment, without any such resource supply, can induce a time crystal absent in the isolated system remains open. Here, we answer this question systematically. We first construct an explicit model and prove that coupling to an environment can activate a time crystal absent in the isolated system, even without such resource supply. We consider Lindblad master equations with (i) time-independent Hamiltonian and jump operators, (ii) time-translation covariance, and (iii) detailed balance---conditions that hold in standard derivations unless thermodynamic resources are supplied externally. Our model satisfies these conditions, and the activation occurs in a stationary state of both the isolated and open systems. We give several implementations of this dynamics with a zero-temperature Gibbs environment, a time-independent, energy-conserving interaction, and only short-range Hamiltonians, one of which exactly reproduces the time-crystalline periodicity. We then prove a no-go theorem: under (i)--(iii), activation can arise only from an environment-induced Hamiltonian term, not from dissipation. Our results show that time crystals can be activated in open systems without external resource supply and identify the physical requirements on the system and environment for this activation through our general no-go theorem.

</details>

Conclusion: ET论证在简并态叠加下失效，非热偏差可归因于模哈密顿量的相互作用项；作者推测，真实费米系统趋衡过程中的初始条件可能被编码于模哈密顿量的相互作用项及由此产生的费米子关联中。

In effect, the ET may be thought of as providing an independent route to establishing the Fermi-Dirac (FD) distribution for free fermions. We revisit this argument for fermions belonging to a superposition of degenerate eigenstates. Specifically, we show that the modular hamiltonian corresponding to a superposition of degenerate eigenstates in a 1-d free fermion lattice system is interacting, and therefore the reduced density matrix for the subsystem is not gaussian. </details>

Abstract: When does a quantum particle arrive at a detector? The temporal distributions of itinerant quantum particles have been debated since the birth of quantum mechanics. In support of the theory, on superconducting quantum hardware, we measure arrival-time distributions for single and multiple interacting particles in one- and two-dimensional circuits. The measured distributions resolve both regimes and agree closely with quantum-trajectory theory. The crossover persists in interacting many-particle systems: the optimal monitoring point is preserved while many-body interference reshapes the distributions.   <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该形式化框架可用于超越纠缠的量子关联（quantum correlations beyond entanglement）表征与封闭量子系统热化（thermalization in closed quantum systems）等问题。

In addition we derive some information theory quantities such as relative entropy, conditional entropy, mutual information and Rényi entropy. We then compare the results with Wehrl entropy for the 1D harmonic oscillator, for pure and mixed states. We conclude the the work discussing potential applications of this new formalism, ranging from quantum correlations beyond entanglement to thermalization in closed quantum systems.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a novel relation connecting the light-quark-mass derivative of the topological susceptibility in quantum chromodynamics (QCD) to the connected correlation between the squared topological charge and the integrated light-quark scalar density. Applying this relation to $(2+1)$-flavor lattice QCD at $m_π\simeq220\,\mathrm{MeV}$ over temperatures ranging from well below to well above the chiral crossover, we find that the response rises rapidly through the crossover from approximately unity toward two. This evolution corresponds to a change from nearly linear to nearly quadratic dependence of the topological susceptibility on the light-quark mass. Using the chiral response as the exponent of a local power law in the light-quark mass, we determine its effective amplitude from the topological susceptibility. This amplitude exhibits a maximum near the crossover temperature, reflecting the competition between the decreasing topological susceptibility and the increasing chiral response. These results reveal a thermal reorganization of the quark-mass dependence of QCD topology beyond the suppression of topological fluctuations alone.

Abstract: Let $N$ be a natural tensor which vanishes at a metric $g\_0$. By diffeomorphism invariance, $DN(g\_0)^*$ takes values in divergence-free symmetric $2$-tensors, and in TT tensors if moreover $DN(g\_0)$ kills conformal directions. Such operators are local potentials, which we call machines: they produce smooth compactly supported tensors with the required property and, on closed manifolds, all of them up to a finite-dimensional space when their symbol is injective. On Einstein manifolds of every dimension, the linearised Bach tensor gives the operator $P$ of an earlier paper, which factorises on TT tensors into the massless and partially massless spin-$2$ operators; we also construct machines on Bach-flat, conformally flat and Ricci-parallel metrics. Replacing the diffeomorphisms of $M$ by those of the vacuum development, we obtain potentials, with a Fredholm property, for the linearised Einstein constraint equations on arbitrary spacelike hypersurfaces of Minkowski, de Sitter and anti-de Sitter spacetimes.

Abstract: The evolution of electron correlation in the superconducting state of the mixed-crystal series $λ$-(BETS)$_2$GaBr$_x$Cl$_{4-x}$ was investigated by magnetic and transport measurements. Br concentrations evaluated by electron-probe microanalysis agree well with single-crystal X-ray diffraction refinement. Demagnetization-corrected susceptibility and magnetization of crystals with $x_{c(rf)}$ = 0.22, 0.24, 0.42, and 0.63 all show a diamagnetic superconducting transition. The lower critical field $H_{c1}$, determined from the deviation from the Meissner response, shows a dome-like maximum at $x_{c(rf)}$ = 0.24. The upper critical field $H_{c2}$, obtained from the first high-field magnetoresistance measurements in this system, decreases with increasing Br concentration up to $x_{c(rf)}$ = 0.42, establishing the high-field superconducting boundary on the chemical-pressure side. Combining these results with reported transport data under chemical and physical pressure, we propose a pressure-temperature phase diagram for $λ$-(BETS)$_2$GaCl$_4$. Its double-dome-like structure has a local minimum near x = 0, where a quantum-critical point was proposed at P $\approx$ 0.13 GPa, separating a low-pressure regime of superconductivity coexisting with magnetism and an unconventional metallic state from a higher-pressure regime with conventional metallic behavior. A low-temperature resistivity upturn, previously reported only under physical pressure, is also found under chemical pressure, suggesting that spin-density-wave fluctuations may extend continuously across both pressure axes. The phase diagram overlaps with that of $λ$-(STF)$_2$GaCl$_4$ under physical pressure, indicating that chemical substitution, external pressure, and molecular substitution can be unified in a single bandwidth-controlled phase diagram for $λ$-type molecular superconductors.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导涨落与量子干涉效应（弱反局域化 WAL / 弱局域化 WL）均可在超导薄膜转变区影响磁电导，二者孰为主导及如何区分尚需澄清，本文以此为出发点考察 BSCCO 薄膜中的竞争关系。

Method: 对高温超导体 Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8+δ（BSCCO）薄膜开展磁输运（magnetotransport）测量，并结合非超导对照样品进行比对；采用 Hikami–Larkin–Nagaoka（HLN）框架拟合 WAL 类信号，并分析接近 BKT 转变温度 T_BKT 处的关联特征及霍尔测量结果。

Result: 在整个转变区，磁电导由超导涨落主导；在远高于 Tc 且无 SF 的狭窄温区，磁电导尖峰可由 HLN 框架描述为 WAL 类特征；靠近 T_BKT 时出现与 Berezinskii–Kosterlitz–Thouless（BKT）关联一致的信号；非超导对照样品无 WAL 类特征，霍尔测量显示主导载流子响应发生符号反转。

Conclusion: 狭窄温区的 WAL 类特征仅在超导涨落共存时出现，表明转变区物理由配对驱动的集体行为而非单粒子量子干涉所决定；霍尔响应的符号反转提示超导起始伴随横向输运的显著重构。

Abstract: Superconducting fluctuations (SF), arising from Cooper-pair-driven interactions and electron-electron interactions within the transition regime, can dominate the magnetoconductance (MC) of superconducting thin films near their transition temperature. In some systems, this fluctuation-dominated regime is preceded by a narrow window in which quantum interference effects are manifested as weak antilocalization (WAL) or weak localization (WL). Here, we report magnetotransport measurements on thin films of the high-temperature superconductor Bi$_2$Sr$_2$CaCu$_2$O$_{8+δ}$ (BSCCO) that establish SF as the dominant mechanism governing MC across the transition regime. In the narrow temperature window well above $T_c$ where SF does not exist, a cusp in MC can be attributed to WAL-like signature well described by the Hikami--Larkin--Nagaoka (HLN) framework. Closer to the transition ($T_{BKT}$), we observe signatures consistent with Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless (BKT) correlations, underscoring that the dominant physics is set by collective, pairing-driven behavior rather than single-particle quantum interference. Measurements on non-superconducting control samples show no WAL-like features, indicating that the WAL over narrow temperature window, emerges only in conjunction with superconducting fluctuations. Hall measurements further reveal a concomitant sign reversal of the dominant carrier response, pointing to a substantial reorganization of transverse transport coincident with the onset of superconductivity. Together, these observations establish that magnetotransport in BSCCO thin film.

### [26] [Energy- and momentum-resolved Cooper-pair emission from a cuprate superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09857)
*Henry Amir,Jack Zwettler,Ka Ho Wong,Genda Gu,Rafael M. Fernandes,Jorge Noronha,Dirk K. Morr,Fahad Mahmood*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用双电子角分辨光电子能谱（2e-ARPES）直接观测到最优掺杂铜氧化物超导体中的库珀对（Cooper pair）发射，并在超导转变温度以下发现伴随集体激发散射的异常联合能量展宽行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导源于库珀配对（Cooper pairing），但传统光电子能谱（photoemission）仅探测单个电子而非电子对，缺乏对配对直接且动量分辨的探测手段。

Method: 采用双电子角分辨光电子能谱（two-electron angle-resolved photoemission, 2e-ARPES）对最优掺杂铜氧化物（cuprate）超导体进行测量，考察零对结合能与零对动量处的双电子发射信号及其联合能量分布随温度的演化。

Result: 在超导转变温度以下观测到零对结合能、零对动量处显著的双电子发射峰，并在正常态消失；同时发现联合能量分布随温度降低沿相对能量轴（relative-energy axis）明显展宽，而总能宽几乎不变，该现象无法由仪器分辨率、静态无序或热展宽解释。

Conclusion: 2e-ARPES可同时作为库珀对发射与超导态多体动力学（many-body dynamics）的探针；异常展宽指向涉及超导体集体激发（collective excitations）的散射过程。

Abstract: Superconductivity arises from Cooper pairing, but conventional photoemission probes electrons individually rather than as pairs. Here we report the observation of Cooper-pair emission from an optimally doped cuprate superconductor using two-electron angle-resolved photoemission (2e-ARPES). Below the superconducting transition, we observe a distinct two-electron emission peak at zero pair binding energy and zero pair momentum that disappears in the normal state, revealing the characteristic fingerprint of a Cooper pair. Unexpectedly, the joint energy distribution broadens markedly along the relative-energy axis as temperature decreases, while the total energy width remains nearly unchanged. This anomalous behavior cannot be explained by instrumental resolution, static disorder or thermal broadening, and instead points to scattering processes involving collective excitations of the superconductor. Our results establish 2e-ARPES as a probe of both Cooper-pair emission and the many-body dynamics of the superconducting state.

</details>

### [27] [Observing Transverse Momentum Broadening from TEECs in Heavy Ion Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08579)
*João Barata,Hai Tao Li,Andrey V. Sadofyev,João M. Silva*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究重离子碰撞双喷注事件中横向能量-能量关联量（TEEC）的方位角依赖，借助真空TMD因子化结合NLL$'$重求和与一阶介质修正，发现经典动量展宽压低背对背峰，辐射修正进一步压低峰并增强大角度退关联，介质响应则使能量缓慢分布于整个方位角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 背对背区域的TEEC可分辨$φ=π$附近的能量流，对介质诱导的动量展宽（momentum broadening）及介质响应敏感，因此可作为分离末态效应、理解喷注在夸克-胶子等离子体中传播的探针。

Method: 考虑色单态硬过程中产生的夸克-反夸克对在均匀背景介质中传播；以真空TMD因子化公式为起点，将NLL$'$重求和与强耦合一阶的介质修正相结合；并额外以平均能量流的形式加入每个喷注引起的介质响应。

Result: 在所研究参数下，经典动量展宽降低背对背峰而其余分布几乎不变；辐射修正显著进一步压低峰值并增强较大角度的退关联；介质响应将沉积能量散布到整个方位角，使TEEC贡献在背对背区变化缓慢、峰值处较小并随角度增大而上升。

Conclusion: 背对背TEEC对动量展宽、辐射修正与介质响应具有不同且可分辨的响应模式，表明该观测量可作为刻画介质诱导末态效应的有效工具。

Abstract: We study the azimuthal dependence of transverse energy-energy correlators (TEECs) in dijet events in heavy-ion collisions. In the back-to-back region this observable resolves the energy flow around $φ=π$ and is sensitive to medium-induced momentum broadening and the response of the medium. To isolate final-state effects, we consider a quark-antiquark pair produced in a colour-singlet hard process and propagating through a homogeneous background medium. Starting from the vacuum TMD factorization formula, we combine NLL$'$ resummation with medium modifications at first order in the strong coupling. For the parameters studied, (classical) momentum broadening lowers the back-to-back peak and leaves the distribution elsewhere nearly unchanged, while radiative corrections significantly suppress the peak further and enhance larger angular decorrelations. We additionally include the medium response sourced by each jet, as a mean energy flow. This spreads the deposited energy over the whole azimuth, so that the contribution to the TEEC varies slowly across the back-to-back region, is small at the peak and grows toward larger angles.

</details>

*Yue Su,Ying-Bo He,Xiao-Yun Wang,Xiao-Hai Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文计算了B_s^0衰变中伴随η产生三个自旋为1的D_s^*介子分子态候选者的分支比，在截断参数2≤α≤5范围内最大中心值可达10^{-6}至10^{-5}量级，提示未来实验可能探测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于利用B_s^0衰变伴随η产生的过程，为D_{s0}^*(2317)\bar D_s^*等分子态候选者提供结构与产生机制的互补探针。

Method: 方法上构建介子圈重散射振幅（meson-loop rescattering amplitudes），其由因子化弱源（factorized weak sources）与重强子手征相互作用（heavy-hadron chiral interactions）组成；成分耦合（constituent couplings）通过阈依赖的成分关系（threshold-dependent compositeness relations）估计，并将输入不确定性传播至预测速率。

Result: 结果给出两种相对符号下的分支比（branching fractions）；在截断范围2≤α≤5内，较大的中心分支比达数个10^{-6}至数个10^{-5}。

Conclusion: 结论认为这些速率在未来实验测量中具有潜在可及性（potential accessibility），并纳入相反C宇称伙伴假设（opposite-C-parity partner hypotheses）及其继承基准参数作为补充。

Abstract: We study the production of spin-one $D_{s0}^*(2317)\bar D_s^*$, $D_{s1}(2460)\bar D_s^*$, and $D_{s1}(2536)\bar D_s^*$ molecular candidates in $B_s^0$ decays with an accompanying $η$, providing a complementary probe of their structure and production mechanisms. The meson-loop rescattering amplitudes are constructed from factorized weak sources and heavy-hadron chiral interactions. Constituent couplings are estimated using threshold-dependent compositeness relations, and the associated input uncertainties are propagated to the predicted rates. Opposite-$C$-parity partner hypotheses are included with inherited benchmark parameters. We calculate the branching fractions for two relative signs. Over the cutoff range $2\leqα\leq5$, the larger central branching fractions reach several $10^{-6}$ to several $10^{-5}$, suggesting potential accessibility in future experimental measurements.

</details>


### [29] [An interrelation of three neutrino mixing angles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09478)
*Zhi-zhong Xing*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出三个中微子混合角（neutrino mixing angles）之间的新关系式 θ23 = (θ12 + θ13)/2 + π/8，并据此预言 θ23 = 43.46^{+0.51°}_{-0.52°}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索中微子混合角之间可能存在的内在关联，以获得可在实验上检验的唯象学预言。

Method: 采用一个简单的唯象学假设（ansatz）推导该角度关系，并借用最新的 JUNO 与 Daya Bay 精确数据作为输入；附带给出轻子味混合矩阵（lepton flavor mixing matrix）按单一小参数的新的展开形式。

Result: 由所提关系预言 θ23 = 43.46^{+0.51°}_{-0.52°}，该关系在唯象学上受青睐且可被实验检验。

Conclusion: 该角度关系为中微子混合角之间的关联提供了一种新的可能解释，其预言可通过实验检验；单一小参数展开可作为副产物用于轻子味混合矩阵的描述。

Abstract: We conjecture a novel interrelation of three neutrino mixing angles: $θ^{}_{23} = \left(θ^{}_{12} + θ^{}_{13}\right)/2 + π/8$, which predicts $θ^{}_{23} = 43.46^{+0.51^\circ}_{-0.52^\circ}$ with inputs of the latest JUNO and Daya Bay precision data. A simple ansatz is proposed to derive this phenomenologically favored and experimentally testable angle relation, and present a new expansion of the lepton flavor mixing matrix in powers of a single small parameter as a by-product.

</details>


### [30] [Restoration of rotational symmetry in light-front dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09193)
*V. A. Karmanov,E. A. Kupriyanova*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文讨论光前动力学（light-front dynamics）中因截断（truncation）导致的旋转对称性破坏问题，并提出以特定系数叠加不同角动量投影态的方式恢复物理解，且通过与Bethe-Salpeter方程投影结果比较加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光前动力学框架下求解束缚态（bound state）问题时，用有限Fock分量（Fock components）近似态矢量会造成结合能（binding energies）依赖于角动量在z轴上的投影，即所谓旋转对称性破坏（violation of rotational symmetry），需要给出合理的物理解释与处理方案。

Method: 作者论证物理态是这些分裂态（split states）以某些明确定义的系数构成的叠加（superposition）；作为检验，另求解Bethe-Salpeter方程并将其解投影到光前（light-front）上，得到不受旋转不变性破坏影响的波函数，再与叠加形式的解进行比较。

Result: 与Bethe-Salpeter方程投影所得波函数的比较表明，叠加形式的解不仅能较好地描述结合能，波函数（wave functions）本身也能被分裂态的叠加相当好地描述。

Conclusion: 光前动力学中截断引起的角动量投影依赖性可由分裂态的适当叠加予以消除，该叠加方案对结合能与波函数均给出与Bethe-Salpeter投影结果相符的描述。

Abstract: Solving the bound state problem in the framework of the light-front dynamics (with the state vector defined in the light-front plane t+z=0), one finds that after truncation - representing approximately the state vector by a finite number of the Fock components, the binding energies become to depend on projection of the angular momentum on the z-axes. This is so called violation of rotational symmetry in light-front dynamics. It is shown that the physical solution is the superposition of these states with some well defined coefficients. For a test, we also solve the Bethe-Salpeter equation and projecting its solution on the light-front, we find the wave functions which do not suffer from violation of the rotation invariance. Comparison with them of solution in the form of superposition shows that not only binding energies but also the wave functions themselves are described by the superpositions of the split states fairly well.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出基于柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络（Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks, KANs）的组件归因方法（component attribution methods），用于探测粒子Transformer（Particle Transformer, ParT）中多头注意力（Multi-Head Attention, MHA）等机制是否真正学到了与喷注（jet）物理描述量对应的判别性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验高能物理（Experimental High Energy Physics, HEP）已采用Transformer架构进行喷注识别，ParT在注意力机制中显式注入粒子关联信息并达到最优精度，但其有效性背后的可解释性（interpretability）仍然有限。

Method: 提出利用KANs进行组件归因的方法，将模型内部组件的作用直接回溯到喷注的物理描述量（physical descriptors）上，以此探测MHA或ParT架构等机制是否将这些物理量学习为判别性特征。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数值结果；所述贡献为提供一种可归因到物理描述量的分析工具，用于检验相关机制对判别性特征的学习情况。

Conclusion: 该工作为高能物理中Transformer类喷注识别模型提供了面向物理描述量的可解释性分析路径，有助于判断MHA与ParT架构的有效性来源。

Abstract: Experimental High Energy Physics (HEP) has adopted the transformer architecture for identification of jets, particularly the state-of-the-art Particle Transformer (ParT) which injects explicit information about particle correlations into the attention mechanism. While able to achieve state-of-the-art results in accuracy, interpretability to its effectiveness has been limited. In this paper we propose component attribution methods using Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) that directly tie back to the physical descriptors of jets, to probe whether mechanisms such as Multi-Head Attention (MHA), or the ParT architecture, are able to learn these as discriminating features.

</details>


### [32] [Deep Learning Theory through the Lens of Statistical Physics: A Topical Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09138)
*Wei Huang,Youjin Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述以统计物理（statistical physics）视角统一梳理深度学习理论进展，将表征学习（representation learning）视为高维系统中的集体现象（collective phenomenon），并强调各近似方法的适用范围与竞争叙事之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型（foundation models）与深度学习的成功远超对大规模神经网络如何学习与泛化的理论理解，需要一种能描述高维、非线性、强耦合系统的语言，统计物理正提供此类概念与方法。

Method: 采用统计物理式思维，从相与临界性（criticality）、自由能（free-energy）视角等概念，到核方法与高斯过程极限（Gaussian-process limits）、平均场与动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory）、随机矩阵工具（random-matrix tools）及Fokker-Planck形式等方法的综述性整合。

Result: 综述了宽网络的平均场分析（mean-field analyses of wide networks）、损失景观（loss landscapes）的谱与几何视角、将随机梯度训练视为动力学过程，以及组织过参数化（overparameterization）、泛化与表征变化等现象的相变类比（phase-transition analogies）。

Conclusion: 这些视角构成统一物理框架，将表征学习视为高维系统中的集体现象，衔接梯度优化的确定性与随机性机制，并阐明各近似解释什么、何时成立及竞争叙事如何关联或分歧。

Abstract: The recent success of foundation models and deep learning has far outpaced our theoretical understanding of how large-scale neural networks learn and generalize. Statistical physics provides a natural language for describing such high-dimensional, nonlinear, and strongly coupled systems, offering concepts such as mean-field theory, phase transitions, energy landscapes, and stochastic dynamics. In this topical review, we adopt a statistical-physics way of thinking -- from concepts including phases and criticality, and free-energy viewpoints, to methods such as kernel and Gaussian-process limits, mean-field and dynamical mean-field theories, random-matrix tools, and Fokker-Planck formalisms, and to a mindset emphasizing universality, scaling, and tractable limits with explicit validity regimes. We survey recent progress on understanding modern deep learning through this lens, including mean-field analyses of wide networks, spectral and geometric perspectives on loss landscapes, stochastic-gradient-based training viewed as a dynamical process, and phase-transition analogies that organize phenomena such as overparameterization, generalization, and representation change. Our goal is to synthesize these threads into a coherent framework that clarifies what each approximation explains, when it is expected to hold, and how apparently competing narratives relate or diverge. Together, these perspectives form a unified physical framework that views representation learning as a collective phenomenon in high-dimensional systems, bridging deterministic and stochastic regimes of gradient-based optimization.

</details>

Abstract: Many non-equilibrium systems, such as expanding plasmas, evolve by equations that depend explicitly on time and interpolate between autonomous early-time (UV) and late-time (IR) limits. After time is compactified, fixed points lie on invariant boundary fibers, where the Conley index is one-sided and blind to the UV. For time dependence that admits a $C^1$ compactification, we reflect the extended phase space across these fibers. Both sheets of the double run from the UV to the IR, and the UV index reappears, suspended by one degree. Extending one sheet slightly beyond both fibers gives an explicit UV-to-IR transport map between the Conley indices of the limit systems, and its non-vanishing forces connecting solutions. If one block with a fixed strict entrance/exit decomposition serves every frozen system, this map is an isomorphism at every admissible rate. If the block also has nonzero relative homology, the isolated invariant sets of the two limits are connected at every rate. In the Gubser flow of Israel-Stewart theory, the separatrix between two classes of initial data is a transversal connection at every finite relaxation scale $c$, with splitting $Δ(c)\propto1/c$. In the Bjorken flow at the lowest moment truncation, we prove existence and uniqueness of the attractor solution without regularization. For the three-dimensional Gubser system we construct the global Morse decomposition. In the one-loop flow of the $(4+ε)$-dimensional $O(N)$ model with cubic anisotropy, a shift of $N$ through $N=4$ has vanishing transport map, but a driven connection and a nonzero connecting homomorphism detect it at every rate.

Abstract: We study the focusing vector nonlinear Schrödinger equation, or Manakov system, for a pair of coupled initial conditions with compactly supported, concave parabolic density profiles and phases depending quadratically on the spatial coordinate. For arbitrary initial chirps in the two fields, we provide closed-form expressions for their evolution, and an explicit expression for the time of the gradient catastrophe, if it occurs, and we classify the effect of the coupling on the catastrophe. However, coupling can also force the catastrophe in fields that would otherwise relax if decoupled. For identical initial supports, and in the presence of dispersion, we numerically show that the same dispersionless criterion determines, in the semiclassical regime, whether the fully dispersive solution relaxes or develops a rogue wave in each field, whose onset time is accurately predicted by the corresponding dispersionless catastrophe time, in direct analogy with the scalar case. Method: 在有限应变二次梯度弹性框架中赋予材料表面独立本构能；在适当矫顽性（coercivity）与关于二次梯度的凸性假设下证明极小元存在性；推导有限变形构型下的增量小扰动（small-on-large）列式，并用于轴向拉伸软圆柱的弹性毛细珠化（elastocapillary beading）分析；基于C1连续等几何分析（isogeometric analysis）在开源有限元代码OOFEM中实现。

To investigate these effects, we derive an incremental small-on-large formulation in the finitely deformed configuration and apply it to the elastocapillary beading of an axially stretched soft cylinder. For an infinite cylinder and the parameters considered, increasing the second-gradient modulus delays bifurcation and shifts the critical mode towards longer wavelengths. Above a finite threshold of this modulus, the critical stretch saturates in the long-wave limit. The model is implemented in the open-source finite element code OOFEM using C1-continuous isogeometric analysis. Finite-cylinder simulations yield onset stretches in close agreement with the analytical predictions. Result: 数值实验验证了收敛性，并在具有数百万条边的网络上展示了强扩展性（strong scaling）与弱扩展性（weak scaling）。应用示例包括：光子网络（photonic networks）中的局域光学共振（localized optical resonances）、矿物置换过程中演化孔隙网络（evolving pore networks）内优先通道的形成，以及周期网络上 KdV 方程（Korteweg–de Vries）的非线性色散波（nonlinear dispersive waves）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一、通用且可大规模并行的物理网络建模与求解框架，能够兼容异质网络结构与多种物理结点条件，并在光学、地球化学与非线性波动等跨领域问题中展示了适用性。

Abstract: Transport, reaction and wave propagation on physical networks depend on dynamics along edges and interactions at junctions. We present a flexible framework for modeling physical networks at scale. A network calculus expresses edge dynamics and vertex contributions as fields over the whole network. Differential operators satisfy integration by parts-identities that connect local dynamics to global conservation laws, and explicit vertex constraints allow a variety of physical junction conditions. We implement this formalism using the open-source Dedalus spectral framework. This accommodates heterogeneous edge lengths, material properties and spectral resolutions. Initial-value, boundary-value and eigenvalue problems share a parallel solution method that eliminates internal unknowns independently on subgraphs and couples their solutions through shared boundary vertex interfaces. We verify numerical convergence and demonstrate strong and weak scaling on networks with millions of edges. We present applications to localized optical resonances in photonic networks, preferential channel formation during mineral replacement in evolving pore networks, and nonlinear dispersive waves for KdV on a periodic network.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速冷却的液体会在结晶前脱离平衡形成玻璃，但玻璃态仍可能后续析晶，这一非平衡析晶路径的机制尚需在统一理论框架下厘清。

Conclusion: 析晶可视为玻璃态的一条失玻璃化路径，其速率与形貌受冻结能量、局部稳定性涨落及逾渗结构共同控制，RFOT框架可统一描述从经典成核到纳米晶化的转变及相关生长异常。

Abstract: If a liquid is cooled rapidly, it may fall out of equilibrium before it can crystallize. It may, however, crystallize later from the nonequilibrium glassy state. Using the Random First-Order Transition (RFOT) theory of glasses, we discuss a variety of mechanisms of crystallization as a route to devitrification. Ordinary nucleation is sped up by the frozen in energy of the glass relative to the liquid. At sufficiently low temperatures, a mechanism of nanonucleation appears, producing nanometer scale crystal inclusions in the glass. Nanocrystallization depends strongly on the local fluctuations in stability, and the transition from classical nucleation and growth to nanocrystallization occurs dominantly through rare, less stable glassy regions that percolate through space, producing an additional dramatic speedup in the crystal nucleation and growth rates. Similar fluctuations in local stability also explain anomalies of crystal growth in deeply supercooled systems.

</details>

Abstract: In this contribution, we review existing multivalent models of Influenza A Virus (IAV) systems to study virion dynamics and design of multivalent entry blockers. We review existing biochemical estimations of the dissociation constants and catalytic activities of the two virion ligands [hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA)], which can be used to develop quantitative theories and experiments. Multivalent models are statistical mechanics frameworks for studying the simultaneous interaction of multiple reversible ligand-receptor bonds. They quantify the effects of both combinatorial entropy terms and single-molecule ligand-receptor affinities. Multivalent frameworks led to the rationale of superselectivity, the ability of probes to sense specific concentrations of conjugated receptors. Multivalent models guided the design of antiviral nanomedicines constituted by polymeric scaffolds conjugated with small molecules such as sialic acid receptors targeting the ligands on the surface of the virion. Importantly, neuraminidase is an enzyme that limits the number of host-guest bonds through receptor desialylation. Like active particles, neuraminidase activity enhances virion motility in the extracellular medium. Modeling the dynamics of Influenza A virions requires developing out-of-equilibrium multivalent models.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模量平台长期被视为均聚物熔体中拓扑缠结的标志，并被用于判定半柔性共轭聚合物（semiflexible conjugated polymers）的缠结行为；本文质疑这一判据在共轭聚合物中的唯一性。

Conclusion: 模量平台本身不能唯一判定共轭聚合物中的拓扑缠结；链间缔合的强度与寿命（strength and lifetime of interchain association）可作为分子设计参数，将骨架堆积（backbone packing）与应力松弛、黏弹性质（viscoelastic properties）及可加工性联系起来，补充其在电子结构与电荷传输（charge transport）中的既有作用。

Abstract: The modulus plateau is widely regarded as a hallmark of topological entanglement in homopolymer melts and has been used to identify entanglement in semiflexible conjugated polymers. Using regiorandom poly(3-alkylthiophenes) as a model system, we show that their plateau elasticity relaxes through an Arrhenius process decoupled from segmental and terminal dynamics, reorganizes persistently under mechanical perturbation, and varies systematically with side-chain length. These behaviors are hard to reconcile with a purely topological-entanglement picture and instead support a transient network dominated by reversible interchain associations, consistent with π-π interactions between conjugated backbone segments. The results thus demonstrate that plateau elasticity alone does not uniquely identify topological entanglement in conjugated polymers. More broadly, they identify the strength and lifetime of interchain association as molecular design parameters linking backbone packing to stress relaxation, viscoelastic properties, and processability in conjugated polymers, complementing their established role in electronic structure and charge transport.

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: CaloCHILL 是一种在学习的潜在空间（latent space）中生成量能器簇射（calorimeter shower）的生成模型，先生成紧凑摘要再展开为全分辨率点云，在高粒度下兼顾速度与精度。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量能器簇射模拟（detector simulation）是探测器模拟中开销最大的部分，而高粒度量能器（highly granular calorimeter）中单次强子簇射可产生数千个能量沉积，使逐点生成方式代价更高。

Method: 模型分三阶段：先用流匹配模型（flow-matching model）确定各量能器层的击中数（hits）与最早击中时间，再用平均流模型（mean-flow model）生成潜在摘要，最后由变分自编码器（variational autoencoder, VAE）的解码器展开为完整点云，并沿簇射轴使用稀疏注意力（sparse attention）控制压缩开销。

Conclusion: 在潜在空间中生成簇射摘要、末端再展开的策略可显著加速高粒度量能器模拟，同时保持或提升物理观测量的还原精度。

Abstract: Calorimeter showers are the most expensive part of detector simulation, and highly granular calorimeters, in which a single hadronic shower leaves thousands of energy deposits, make them more expensive still. We present CaloCHILL (Calorimeter Hits In Learned Latents), a generative model that does not produce these deposits one by one: instead it generates a compact summary of the shower in a learned latent space and expands it to full resolution only at the end. The complete shower, including the arrival time of every hit, is built in three stages: a flow-matching model first sets the number of hits and the earliest hit time in each calorimeter layer, a mean-flow model then generates the latent summary, and the decompressor of a variational autoencoder turns it into the full point cloud, giving every hit its position, energy and time. Sparse attention along the shower axis keeps this compression affordable for showers with thousands of hits. On positive-pion showers in the calorimeter of the International Large Detector, a proposed detector for the FCC-ee, CaloCHILL reproduces the main shower observables, including their time structure. This new model is 600 times faster than its predecessor, while improving on all accuracy metrics considered.

</details>

Abstract: PIONEER is an approved experiment at the Paul Scherrer Institut (PSI) in Villigen, Switzerland that aims to measure the charged-pion branching ratio to electrons vs. muons with an accuracy of 0.01%. This is an order of magnitude improvement in precision compared to its predecessors and approaches the accuracy of the standard model prediction. The key elements of PIONEER are a high resolution calorimeter built concentrically around the high granularity active target (ATAR). The ATAR is composed of a stack of 48 low gain avalanche strip detectors (LGADs) to stop the beam pions and provide detailed information of the event topology. This will include precise timing (in the order of 200ps), spatial information (to an accuracy of about 60 micro meter) and pulse height information with a high dynamic range to separate pion stops from muons and MIPs. To avoid affecting the energy measurement of the positrons, the readout electronics is placed outside the acceptance angle of the calorimeter. It is composed of a fast amplifier and a fast sampling chip digitizing the LGAD wave form for real time analysis. The paper describes the PIONEER-experiment with focus on the ATAR and its readout. The status of the development and activities in 2026 will be reported.

</details>

Conclusion: PICO-40L 的 RSU 概念验证将为目前在 SNOLAB 建造的最终一代探测器 PICO-500 的运行配置提供指导。

Abstract: The PICO collaboration has designed and constructed a novel bubble chamber to search for evidence of Weakly Interacting Massive Particles, a leading dark matter candidate. The PICO-40L detector is the successor to PICO-60, and uses a fully redesigned system for pressurization of the active volume after a bubble nucleation event. Pressure in PICO-40L is transferred to the target fluid through a set of two nested synthetic quartz vessels connected by bellows below the active volume. This is in contrast to the design of earlier detectors in which the pressure was transferred through a buffer fluid atop the target fluid, within a single synthetic quartz vessel connected to a bellows above the active volume. Removing the buffer fluid removes the potential for backgrounds arising from the interface between the two fluids, and moving the bellows below the target fluid suppresses potential backgrounds from particulates originating from the bellows. The newly designed detector concept of PICO-40L is referred to as the Right-Side-Up (RSU) design and requires a dedicated thermal system to keep the lower region of the detector cold while the upper region is stably superheated. The main goal of PICO-40L is to establish the performance characteristics of the RSU detector concept, which will inform the operating configuration of the final generation PICO dark matter detector, PICO-500, which is currently under construction at SNOLAB. This paper reviews the design, installation, and performance of the PICO-40L detector.

</details>

Abstract: We study Liouville transforms for the differential equation $Ju'+qu=wf$ on the real interval $(a,b)$ where $J$ is a constant, invertible, skew-hermitian matrix and $q$ and $w$ are matrices whose entries are distributions of order $0$ with $q$ Hermitian and $w$ non-negative. In particular, we examine their use in the treatment of quasi-regular endpoints and their impact on the Weyl-Titchmarsh $\mathbb M$-function associated with the differential equation.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在所述条件下，极限测度支撑于有限多条解析弧（analytic arcs），其对数势低于平均测度者，差值为四个显式作用量中最小者的两倍；对Im V非单调情形给出条件性推广，并在圆形势弧情形验证了几何切换条件，仅余非共振（non-resonance）假设。

Abstract: Freezing the coefficients of a banded locally Toeplitz matrix gives a family of Toeplitz eigenvalue measures. Their average can fill a region of the complex plane even when the eigenvalues of the original matrices appear to follow curves. We conjecture that, when the above average has a sufficiently regular planar density, the limiting eigenvalue measure is its mother body. We prove it for a class of finite Dirichlet discrete Schrödinger operators with complex potential $V$ and a common real coefficient $α>0$ on the two adjacent diagonals. When $(\Im V)'>0$ and $V$ admits a suitable holomorphic extension, the limiting measure is supported on finitely many analytic arcs. Its logarithmic potential falls below that of the averaged measure by twice the least of four explicit actions. We obtain these actions from a four-term asymptotic expansion of the Dirichlet determinant. When $\Im V$ is not monotone, an exact transfer recursion gives a conditional extension. For a circular potential arc we verify the geometric switching condition, leaving non-resonance as the remaining hypothesis.

</details>

Conclusion: 常见兵形并非孤立类别，而是连通结构景观（connected structural landscape）中的不同区域；该方法补充传统开局分类，揭示异名开局的相似性并提供定量比较基础。

Abstract: Chess openings naturally separate into slow and fast degrees of freedom: pawns evolve through irreversible moves, whereas pieces can be rapidly reorganized within the resulting structure. We exploit this separation to construct a quantitative morphology of opening space. From a catalogue of 3810 named variations, 1749 reach a common depth of $N=10$ plies, corresponding to 619 distinct positions and only 435 joint pawn structures. We characterize these positions through observables measuring pawn commitment and spatial organization, blockade and tension, and piece activity and centralization. Their partial independence reveals a multidimensional opening space and in order to explore its structural organization, we construct a network whose nodes are distinct White pawn structures, connected when a single pawn move or capture transforms one into another. Remarkably, 139 of the 149 observed structures belong to a single connected component containing $98\%$ of all positions. This network exhibits a statistically significant modular organization, with eight communities whose representative structures correspond to familiar pawn formations. Rather than forming isolated categories, these formations emerge as distinct regions of a connected structural landscape. Our approach thus complements the traditional opening taxonomy by revealing similarities between differently named openings and providing a quantitative basis for comparing the positions they produce.

</details>

Abstract: We present a general proof of different power-law decays versus distance of the eigenvalues of two-point covariance matrices in critical lattice quantum models, allowing extraction of multiple scaling dimensions. Using quantum Monte Carlo simulations to compute covariance matrices in different symmetry sectors, we resolve the expected relevant and marginal primary operators as well as some descendants. Abstract: The zero-magnetic-field metal-insulator transition in strongly correlated two-dimensional electron systems was experimentally discovered three decades ago, thereby triggering extensive experimental and theoretical investigations. Prior to this discovery, the occurrence of such a transition --- as well as the very existence of a metallic phase in two dimensions --- was regarded as forbidden within the framework of the one-parameter scaling theory of localization. In this work, we provide a comprehensive review of the transport and thermodynamic properties of two-dimensional electron and hole systems in which carrier-carrier interactions constitute the dominant energy scale. We present and critically analyze both experimental observations and theoretical developments accumulated over the past thirty years.

</details>

Abstract: The sign problem in path integrals severely limits the application of quantum Monte Carlo methods to strongly correlated systems, whereas the Lefschetz-thimble method offers a rigorous framework to mitigate this issue. In this paper, we establish a numerical framework based on the Lefschetz thimbles for the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model. By introducing continuous auxiliary fields via the Hubbard-Stratonovich transformation, the partition function is formulated as a high-dimensional complex integral. We employ the complex Langevin equation to explore the saddle-point distribution of the effective action in the complex plane. Monte Carlo integration is then performed to compute the partition function and Green functions. Our numerical results exhibit excellent agreement with high-temperature expansions, infrared-limit solutions, and determinantal quantum Monte Carlo (DQMC) simulations, confirming the validity of our framework. Furthermore, we demonstrate that our complex Langevin search successfully captures the relevant dominant saddle points governing the SYK model in the large-N limit. Our work deepens the understanding of the sign problem in the SYK model and provides a viable non-perturbative avenue for addressing sign problems in more complex strongly correlated systems.

</details>

Abstract: We extend the scalability of variational infinite two-dimensional tensor network methods with application to investigate frustrated magnetism of the square-lattice $J_1$-$J_2$ model and kagome-lattice Heisenberg model. With no obstruction beyond this limit, our method obtains direct estimates of order parameters. For the square-lattice $J_1$-$J_2$ model, we determine isotropic plaquette order in the valence-bond solid region. For the model near $J_2/J_1=0.5$, and the kagome lattice Heisenberg model, our data indicate linear-dispersion gapless states.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子—反中微子湮灭同时受广义相对论输运（general-relativistic transport）与中微子味转换影响，但二者能否独立处理取决于引力如何加权不同味族的中微子群体，此前缺乏系统检验。

Result: 均匀味转换且重轻子中微子发射可忽略时，味转换修正与时空几何无关；味依赖发射区重叠时该可分离性近似成立：相较相同局部速度的平直空间盘，引力使沉积能量增强1.4–5倍，而相对味转换效应变化多数温度剖面下不超过6%，快速自转黑洞情形下随盘变化的转换可使其升至22%。当重轻子中微子来自与电子味发射区分离的内环时，相对论弯曲与能量移动优先增强涉及内环中微子的湮灭，相对论转换因子最高达平直空间的2.4倍；某一构型中味均分在平直空间使沉积降低54%，在Kerr时空反使沉积提高11%。

Abstract: Neutrino-antineutrino annihilation above black-hole accretion discs is shaped both by general-relativistic transport and by neutrino flavour conversion. Whether these effects can be treated independently depends on how gravity weights the different flavour populations. We investigate this interplay using backward ray tracing in Schwarzschild and Kerr spacetimes, combined with an exact angular-moment formulation of the annihilation kernel that we validate against the analytic spherical solution and published disc calculations. We first establish that flavour-conversion corrections are independent of the spacetime geometry when conversion is uniform and the initial heavy-lepton neutrino emission is negligible. For uniform conversion, this separability remains approximately valid when the flavour-dependent emitting regions overlap: compared with the same discs in flat space, moving with the same local velocities, gravity enhances the deposited energy by factors of 1.4-5, while changing the relative conversion effect by no more than 6% for most temperature profiles considered; conversion that varies across the disc raises this to 22% around a rapidly spinning hole. A qualitatively different regime emerges when heavy-lepton neutrinos originate from an inner ring separated from the electron-flavour emitting regions. Relativistic bending and energy shifts preferentially enhance annihilations involving inner-ring neutrinos, producing relativistic conversion factors up to 2.4 times the flat-space ones. In one configuration, flavour equipartition reduces the deposition by 54% in flat space but increases it by 11% in Kerr spacetime. These results identify the spatial arrangement of flavour-dependent neutrinospheres as a key determinant, in the models considered, of whether flavour conversion and relativistic transport can be treated separately.

</details>

*D. Castillo,D. Frycz,J. Menéndez*

TL;DR: 本文检验了近期提出的"轻子与核波函数因子化失效"方案能否解释镓异常（gallium anomaly），通过核壳模型（nuclear shell model, NSM）计算跃迁密度并构造因子化与非因子化截面，结果显示两者一致，仍比实验值高约20%。因此，使用现实核跃迁密度并不能解决镓异常。

Abstract: The gallium anomaly, a 20% deficit in the measured $ν_e$ captures on $^{71}$Ga, remains unexplained. A recent work [Phys. Rev. Lett. 137, 131805 (2026), arXiv:2512.20560] has proposed a solution based on the breakdown of the factorization of lepton and nuclear wavefunctions that underlies the prediction of the cross section, combined with ad hoc Gamow-Teller nuclear transition densities. We test this proposed solution with transition densities calculated within the nuclear shell model (NSM), normalized to the $^{71}$Ge electron-capture half-life and convolved with Dirac-Hartree-Fock-Slater electron wavefunctions. We explore several NSM interactions, bare and effective Gamow-Teller operators, as well as the impact of the valence space. In all cases, we obtain remarkably consistent NSM transition densities. Our factorized and nonfactorized cross sections agree, remaining 20% higher than the measured value. In addition, we use NSM single-nucleon transition densities as ansatz and find that solving the anomaly requires fine-tuned densities, with percent-level cancellations and an overall scale orders of magnitude larger than the calculated NSM ones. We conclude that the gallium anomaly is not solved when using realistic nuclear transition densities.

Abstract: We present new developments in the mathematical foundations and numerical simulations of the causal and stable first-order relativistic viscous hydrodynamic theory by Bemfica, Disconzi, Noronha, and Kovtun (BDNK) in general spacetimes. We develop two independent flux-conservative algorithms for arbitrary 3+1-dimensional spacetime metrics. Both are hyperbolic, causal, and well-posed, pass convergence tests, and reproduce known results. The first uses a new first-order reduction with mathematically preserved constraints, whose numerical violations can be handled with cleaning fields (the resulting coupled system is proven hyperbolic and well-posed). The second uses a new mixed-order formulation, whose solutions are proven strictly equivalent to the original BDNK system, without cleaning fields. Our proofs rely on a new general theorem providing sufficient conditions for transforming a mixed-order system with arbitrary-order equations into a strictly hyperbolic system, strengthening a classical result by Choquet Bruhat for weak hyperbolicity. We introduce a new practical prescription for selecting admissible hydrodynamic frames for a broad class of equations of state, including nonconformal ones relevant to heavy-ion collisions and astrophysics. Our simulations capture viscous shock broadening and suppression of Kelvin--Helmholtz vortices, reproduce Bjorken and Gubser flows, and study departures from stationary ideal-fluid configurations in Michel accretion and rotating tori on fixed Schwarzschild and Kerr backgrounds. For heavy-ion collisions, we perform boost invariant simulations with a lattice-QCD-based equation of state and fluctuating TRENTo initial conditions, comparing BDNK with a minimal Israel--Stewart theory. The descriptions agree at small shear viscosity but can differ substantially at larger viscosities, particularly for early initialization with rapid longitudinal expansion.

The formalism is applied to representative nonlinear RMF parameterization sets to investigate the model dependence of baryon number fluctuations over a wide range of temperatures and baryon chemical potentials. All the considered RMF parameterizations reproduce the overall qualitative beam-energy dependence of the cumulant ratios. The observed skewness and kurtosis within the RMF models satisfy the experimental values. Specifically, the FSU-family parameterizations exhibit relatively small deviations from the measured heavy-ion observables i.e. cumulant ratios.

</details>

Method: 以微观Glauber型动力学为出发点，经空间平均场近似得到连续场方程；采用线性稳定性（linear stability）与弱非线性分析（weakly nonlinear analysis），在Hopf分岔附近导出复Ginzburg–Landau方程（complex Ginzburg–Landau equation）；并基于一维行波前（traveling fronts）的微扰理论（perturbation theory）进行半解析计算，最后与离散Monte Carlo模拟对照。

Result: 耦合强度（coupling strength）增大会诱导Hopf分岔；空间均匀极限下出现极限环振荡（limit-cycle oscillations）与有序态，其间存在由\(\mathbb{Z}_3\)对称异宿分岔（heteroclinic bifurcation）界定的双稳区；二维情形可维持稳定旋转螺旋波，并半解析预测圆形波前的曲率依赖传播速度及螺旋波旋转角速度与螺距。

Abstract: We investigate the spatiotemporal dynamics of a three-state active Potts model with a cyclic transition bias. Starting from microscopic Glauber-type dynamics, we derive macroscopic continuum field equations through a spatial mean-field approximation. Linear stability and weakly nonlinear analyses reveal that increasing the coupling strength induces a Hopf bifurcation, near which the critical amplitude dynamics is described by the complex Ginzburg--Landau equation. In the spatially homogeneous limit, the system exhibits limit-cycle oscillations and ordered states, with an intermediate regime of bistability bounded by a $\mathbb{Z}_3$-symmetric heteroclinic bifurcation. In two dimensions, the system sustains stable rotating spiral waves. Using perturbation theory based on one-dimensional traveling fronts, we semianalytically predict the curvature-dependent propagation velocity of circular fronts, as well as the rotational angular velocity and pitch of spiral waves. Finally, we compare the continuum model with discrete Monte Carlo simulations to clarify the role of thermal fluctuations and evaluate the validity of the mean-field approximation.

### [61] [Violent relaxation redux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09531)
*Michael L. Nastac,Yonadav Barry Ginat,Robert J. Ewart,Michael Barnes,Alexander A. Schekochihin*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 无碰撞自引力系统（collisionless self-gravitating systems）的剧烈弛豫（violent relaxation）被认为可实现Lynden-Bell最大熵平衡，其底层机制是引力湍流（gravitational turbulence）；相空间体积守恒（Casimir不变量）仅在比动力学时间对数倍长的时间尺度上被破坏，使系统通过一连串瞬时Lynden-Bell平衡"绝热"弛豫至最小能量、最大熵的基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无碰撞自引力系统（如并合星系）长期被视为统计力学（statistical mechanics）原理的例外：混沌多体系统应弛豫到最大熵态，但受相空间体积严格守恒（等价于无穷多无碰撞Casimir不变量）约束的Lynden-Bell理论似乎并不能很好描述这类系统。

Method: 提出剧烈弛豫确实产生Lynden-Bell最大熵平衡，且二者由引力湍流（gravitational turbulence）支撑——大尺度引力涨落驱动分布函数的混沌相空间混合；推导该湍流的普适小尺度（亚Jeans尺度，sub-Jeans）能谱，并用一维N体模拟（N-body simulations）加以验证。

Result: 相空间体积守恒的破坏发生在仅比动力学（剧烈弛豫）时间对数倍长（随粒子数N）的时间尺度上；这种时间尺度分离使系统能够经过一系列Lynden-Bell平衡"绝热"弛豫，每个平衡对应Casimir不变量的瞬时值，长时间下趋近于"基态"——Casimir已调整至系统能量取最小可能值的分布。

Conclusion: 系统具有朝向最小能量、最大熵状态的普适倾向；该图像与一维N体模拟结果一致，并被假设可解释星系与暗物质晕（dark-matter halos）的普适平衡态。

Abstract: Collisionless self-gravitating systems, such as merging galaxies, have long seemed an exception to the statistical-mechanical principle that chaotic many-body systems relax to maximum-entropy states: Lynden-Bell's statistical mechanics of their "violent relaxation," constrained by exact conservation of phase-space volume -- equivalently, of infinitely many collisionless "Casimir" invariants -- has appeared not to be a good description. We propose that violent relaxation does give rise to Lynden-Bell's maximum-entropy equilibria, but that both are underpinned by gravitational turbulence, in which large-scale gravitational fluctuations drive chaotic phase-space mixing of the particles' distribution function. We derive the universal small-scale (sub-Jeans) spectra of this turbulence and show that it leads to phase-space-volume conservation being broken on a timescale only logarithmically longer, in the particle number N, than the dynamical (violent-relaxation) time. This timescale separation lets the system relax "adiabatically" through a sequence of Lynden-Bell equilibria, each corresponding to instantaneous values of the Casimirs. At long times, it approaches an equilibrium close to its "ground state": a distribution whose Casimirs have adjusted so that the system's energy is now its minimum possible energy. Thus, the system's universal tendency is toward a minimum-energy, maximum-entropy state. We corroborate this picture with 1D N-body simulations and hypothesize that it can explain the universal equilibria of galaxies and dark-matter halos.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在检验五重态模型在幽灵穿越（phantom crossing）情形下与最新DESI及高红移观测的相容性，并量化其宇宙学参数及耦合强度。

Abstract: The quintom model consisting of quintessence and phantom scalar fields with linear coupling to matter is explored and constrained by updated DESI and supernovae type Ia~(SNIa) observational data. The matter interaction is assumed only between phantom and matter and the quintessence self potential is in an exponential form. The quintom evolution shows a class of solutions with phantom crossing scenario consistent with the current DESI data. From low redshift observations, we place constraints on the model with BAO data from DESI and distance measurements from SNIa Pantheon+ samples and SH0Es. For high-redshift constraints, we use CMB priors from Planck and found that the quintom model gives the best-fit Hubble parameter $H_{0}=65.780~(68.736)$~km s$^{-1}$ Mpc$^{-1}$ for DESI+CMB+SNIa~(+SH0ES) respectively. The marginalized statistical constrained values are $H_{0}=66.21\pm 0.84~(68.49^{+0.46}_{-0.40})$~km s$^{-1}$ Mpc$^{-1}$ for DESI+CMB+SNIa~(+SH0ES) respectively. Using the BBN prior for high-redshift constraints, the corresponding best-fit values for DESI+BBN+SNIa~(+SH0ES) constraints are $H_{0}=63.999~(71.962)$~km s$^{-1}$ Mpc$^{-1}$ and the marginalized statistical values for DESI+BBN+SNIa~(+SH0ES) constraints are $H_{0}=61.6^{+6.6}_{-3.0}~(71.86\pm 0.88)$~km s$^{-1}$ Mpc$^{-1}$ respectively. Generally, adding SH0ES significantly raises the best-fit and marginalized value of $H_{0}$ for all datasets. In comparison to $Λ$CDM, the interacting quintom model gives lower values of $σ_{8}=0.74-0.79$ for combination of datasets with CMB prior. Lastly, the fifth-force constraint from local scale solar system Cassini probe is estimated, we find the constraint on the phantom-matter coupling $|δ|<3.3\times 10^{-3}$.

</details>

*Alessandro Santini,Diganta Bandopadhyay,Jonathan Gair*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出 $\texttt{Phentax}$，即波形模型 $\texttt{IMRPhenomTHM}$ 的 $\texttt{JAX}$ 重实现，旨在为 LISA 全局拟合（global fit）提供快速、可批处理且可微分（differentiable）的波形计算工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA 预计将探测数千个白矮星双星、极端质量比旋近（EMRIs）、早期旋近的恒星级黑洞双星以及 $10^4$-$10^7 M_\odot$ 的 massive black hole binaries（MBHBs），需对全部源参数进行联合推断，而数十万量级的波形模板评估计算代价高昂。

Method: 采用 $\texttt{JAX}$ 对时域唯象波形模型 $\texttt{IMRPhenomTHM}$（面向近似等质量双黑洞并包含高阶模式（higher-order modes）效应）进行重新实现，得到 $\texttt{Phentax}$。

Abstract: The upcoming Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) is expected to be sensitive to thousands of white dwarf (WD) binaries in our galaxy, extreme mass ratio inspirals (EMRIs) of stellar-mass compact objects into massive black holes, stellar-mass black hole binaries (SOBHBs) in their early inspiral phase, and, crucially, massive black hole binaries (MBHBs) with masses in the range of $10^4$-$10^7 M_\odot$. The presence of such a large number of signals requires joint inference of all parameters across all sources present in the data, an approach referred to as "global fit". This requires evaluating hundreds of thousands of waveform templates, which can be computationally expensive. To address this challenge, we present $\texttt{Phentax}$, a $\texttt{JAX}$ re-implementation of the $\texttt{IMRPhenomTHM}$ waveform model. This is a state-of-the-art time-domain phenomenological waveform model for comparable-mass binary black holes that includes the effects of higher-order modes. Given this, $\texttt{IMRPhenomTHM}$ is one of the models currently used to simulate and analyze MBHB signals in LISA. With $\texttt{Phentax}$, we provide a fast, easy-to-batch, and differentiable implementation of this waveform model to accelerate the development of LISA global fit algorithms.

</details>

*Chen Liang*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 径向激发（radially excited）的迷你玻色子星在静态轴对称微扰下存在零模，并由此分岔为链状与环状物质分布的构型；但自相互作用如何影响这些零模此前尚不清楚。

Method: 针对带单个径向节点的球对称玻色子星，研究其 $\ell=2$ 零模随自相互作用的变化，并构建分岔产生的链与环构型；比较两类模型：具有排斥性四次自相互作用的 massive 玻色子星，以及具有 Q-ball 型势的 solitonic 玻色子星。

Result: 对 massive 玻色子星，在所考察的全部四次耦合取值下零模均存在；随耦合增大，零模移入候选稳定分支，使其无法对 $\ell=2$ 微扰完全稳定。对 solitonic 玻色子星，分岔强烈依赖引力耦合：耦合减小时单一球对称星族可出现多个零模，链与环交替出现；低于临界耦合时零模消失。平直时空中环状 Q-ball 仍存在而链状构型不存在。

Conclusion: 自相互作用与引力耦合共同决定零模的存在性及链/环分岔结构；链状构型的形成需要引力参与，而环状构型可在平直时空中作为静态 Q-ball 独立存在。

For massive boson stars, the zero mode persists for all values of the quartic coupling we considered. As the coupling grows, it moves onto the candidate stable branch, so that this branch cannot be entirely stable against $\ell=2$ perturbations, even though sufficiently strong self-interactions are known to quench the radial instability of these stars. For solitonic boson stars, the bifurcations depend strongly on the gravitational coupling. As it decreases, a single family of spherical stars can have multiple zero modes, at which chains and rings alternate, and below a critical coupling the zero modes disappear. </details>

Method: 采用两步流水线（two step pipeline）：第一步为搜索算法（search algorithm），在模拟LISA数据中检测潜在瞬变；第二步为机器学习模型（machine learning model），将检测到的瞬变分类为引力波或仪器毛刺。
