Conclusion: 该流水线展现了用于精确快速预警与辅助全局拟合的潜在价值；论文亦讨论了训练中遇到的挑战，包括当前框架的局限性。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) is a space-based gravitational wave detector scheduled for launch in the 2030s by the European Space Agency. Operating in the 0.1 mHz to 0.1 Hz band, LISA will observe a variety of astrophysical sources including but not limited to supermassive black hole mergers, extreme mass ratio inspirals, and white dwarf binaries. LISA will yield new insights, including supermassive black hole formation, new tests of general relativity, population studies of white dwarfs and others. Like all interferometric gravitational wave detectors, LISA data is expected to contain non-astrophysical transients, commonly referred to as glitches. We introduce a two step pipeline that consists of a search algorithm for detecting potential transients in simulated LISA data followed by a machine learning model that classifies the detected transients as gravitational waves or instrumental glitches. This is important for MBHBs with very high masses (around $10^6\ M_{\odot}$) which are transients that could be morphologically similar to LISA glitches in the data. Initial results demonstrate a promising ability to classify detected events with an accuracy close to $98\%$. The pipeline also shows the ability to analyze a month of data in under 2 minutes on a laptop. This shows an eventual utility of our pipeline for enabling accurate rapid alerts from low latency searches. It could also support model selection in global-fit analyses by providing an initial summary of the location and type of individual signals. This paper also discusses challenges encountered during training, including limitations of the current framework.

</details>


### [66] [Landauer bound in a regular Bardeen black hole: extremality and evaporation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08948)
*J. Andrade,J. O. Cedeño,Jiří Svozilík,Tayyab Naseer,Faisal Javed,M. Zeeshan Gul*

Main category: gr-qc

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文针对极端黑洞（extremal black hole）霍金温度（Hawking temperature）趋于零是否意味着信息可在无能量转移下被擦除这一问题，结合热力学 Landauer 原理（Landauer principle）与黑洞物理的张力展开检验。

Method: 采用规则 Bardeen 几何（regular Bardeen geometry）中的双能级量子系统（two-level quantum system）作为量子存储（quantum memory），与外腔（exterior cavity）中受限的标量玻色场耦合；在共振单模（resonant single-mode）下给出重置协议，并在多模（multimode）计算中保留反向旋转项（counter-rotating terms），同时对热态储库计算 Reeb–Wolf 平衡；另将受困场与无约束场（unconfined scalar field）的近极端光度（luminosity）行为作对比。

Result: 对最大混合态存储，得到下界 Q_∞ ≥ Δ_{R,∞}(1/2 − ε)，Δ_{R,∞} 为储库能隙、ε 为重置误差；单模共振协议在不需外部控制做功下可达该下界，能量由存储本身提供。多模计算显示在脉冲参数不变时，越接近极端化残余重置误差越大；热态储库的 Reeb–Wolf 分析中，相对熵（relative entropy）项折合能量后可有有限低温极限，且这些数值制备均比霍金温度更热。无约束场近极端光度为 P_∞ ∝ y^4，y = r_H/|g| − √2，准静态能耗假设给出 y ∝ t^{-1/2}，但并不构成稳定遗迹（stable remnant）存在的证据。

Conclusion: 就所考察的特定重置操作而言，霍金温度趋于零并不迫使被转移的热量（transferred heat）趋于零；外腔几何使场的基态激发能保持有限，从而维持了信息擦除的能量代价。

Abstract: Does the vanishing Hawking temperature of an extremal black hole imply that information can be erased without energy transfer? We investigate this question in the regular Bardeen geometry using a two-level quantum system, representing a quantum memory, coupled to a scalar bosonic field confined within an exterior cavity. The Hawking temperature tends to zero at extremality, as does the corresponding thermal Landauer scale. The field in an exterior cavity behaves differently, provided both walls remain away from the horizon, its lowest excitation energy stays finite. For a maximally mixed memory coupled to a reservoir initially in its unique ground state, we find $Q_\infty\geqΔ_{R,\infty}(1/2-ε)$, with $Δ_{R,\infty}$ the reservoir gap and $ε$ the reset error. For a resonant single-mode interaction, we give a reset protocol that attains the bound with no work supplied by the external control. In this example, the memory itself supplies the energy gained by the field. In the multimode calculation, counter-rotating terms are retained. Keeping the pulse parameters unchanged, we find a larger residual reset error closer to extremality. For reservoirs prepared thermally, we evaluate the Reeb--Wolf balance and find that the relative-entropy contribution, expressed in energy units, can have a finite low-temperature limit. These numerical preparations are externally imposed and remain hotter than the Hawking temperature. For comparison, an unconfined scalar field has near-extremal luminosity $P_\infty\propto y^4$, with $y=r_H/|g|-\sqrt2$. Assuming a particular quasistatic energy-loss prescription gives $y\propto t^{-1/2}$; this does not establish the existence of a stable remnant. For this particular reset operation, vanishing Hawking temperature therefore does not force the transferred heat to vanish.

</details>


### [67] [Equatorial Periodic Orbits and Gravitational Wave Signatures in Weyl Geometric Gravity Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08952)
*Dhruba Jyoti Gogoi,Himanshu Chaudhary,Tiberiu Harko*

TL;DR: 本文研究Weyl几何引力（Weyl geometric gravity）中球对称黑洞解的类时测地线性质及其在极端质量比旋近（EMRI）系统中的引力波辐射，表明非度规性（nonmetricity）会在轨道拓扑与波形上留下可观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weyl几何引力黑洞解相较Schwarzschild解在径向坐标上多出线性与二次修正项，需要明确这些修正所体现的非度规性如何影响强场中质量测试粒子的动力学，并评估其可观测性。

Method: 通过引入辅助标量场并在Ricci标量与标量场非最小耦合下，将理论重构为Riemann几何中的标量-矢量-张量（scalar-vector-tensor）理论；假设Weyl矢量仅有径向分量，得到含三个积分常数的真空球对称黑洞型精确解，并分析其类时测地线（timelike geodesics）、周期轨道拓扑结构及EMRI引力波发射。

Result: 非度规性的存在对紧致EMRI系统的轨道拓扑与引力波波形均产生显著且可测量的印记，表现为独特的时间调制与振幅调制（temporal and amplitude modulations）。

Conclusion: 这些时间与振幅上的独特调制为在宇宙尺度上检验非度规性提供了有前景的观测途径（observational avenue）。

Abstract: Weyl geometric gravity theory is a gravitational theory with Lagrangian density constructed from the square of the Weyl curvature scalar and the strength of the Weyl vector. The theory can be reformulated as a scalar - vector - tensor theory in the Riemann geometry by introducing an auxiliary scalar field, in the presence of a non - minimal coupling between the Ricci scalar and the scalar field. Assuming that the Weyl vector has only a radial component, an exact spherically symmetric black hole type solution of the field equations in the vacuum can be obtained. The solution depends on three arbitrary integration constants. As compared to the case of the Schwarzschild black hole, the Weyl geometric gravity black hole solution contains two new terms, linear and quadratic, in the radial coordinate. It is the main goal of the present work to investigate astrophysical properties of the Weyl geometric black holes by considering the behavior of the timelike geodesics, which allows to explore how the geometric nonmetricity influences the strong-field dynamics of massive test particles as well as the topological structure of the periodic orbits. The resulting gravitational wave emission in extreme mass-ratio inspiral (EMRI) systems is also considered. Our results show that the presence of nonmetricity in Weyl geometric gravity leaves distinct and measurable signatures on both the orbital topology and the emitted gravitational waveforms of compact EMRI systems. These unique temporal and amplitude modulations provide a promising observational avenue for testing the presence of nonmetricity on cosmic scales.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非最小耦合（non-minimal coupling）的 \(f(R,\lmatt)\) 引力在弱场极限下的表现，以考察曲率与物质的耦合如何共同改变几何与流体性质。

Method: 对围绕闵可夫斯基（Minkowski）几何背景的度规做弱场展开，并采用准牛顿近似，解析求解支配引力扰动与物质变量的耦合方程组；物质侧取能量密度围绕常数参考值扰动。

Conclusion: 在所考察的弱场区域内，曲率与物质的非最小耦合同时修饰了时空几何与理想流体的热力学量。

Abstract: We study the weak-field regime of $f(R,\lmatt)$ gravity for a static and spherically symmetric configuration described by a perfect fluid, whose energy density is perturbed about a constant reference value. By means of a weak-field limit expansion about a Minkowski geometric background and under a quasi-Newtonian approximation, we derive and analytically solve the coupled system that governs the gravitational perturbations and the matter variables.
  The solution contains the long-range Newtonian contribution and Yukawa-type corrections in the temporal and radial components of the metric. In the matter sector, the energy density, the internal energy, and the pressure acquire radial contributions, with a non-zero constant background rest-mass energy density and vanishing background internal energy and pressure. The results show how the non-minimal coupling between curvature and matter jointly modifies the geometry and the fluid in the considered regime.

</details>

Abstract: Following a proposal that the thermodynamic aspects of general relativity can be found in the evolution of the past light cones of arbitrary worldlines, we generalize the Bondi-Sachs formalism to these surfaces. The observer may accelerate and rotate their tetrad, the spacetime may contain matter and a cosmological constant, and no symmetry is assumed. We impose regularity conditions at the vertex of each light cone and along the polar axis, including a general axial condition that allows for shear. Given a suitable prescription for the stresses of matter, the Einstein field equations can then be integrated hierarchically from data on a single light cone, and the regularity at the vertex fixes all constants of integration. The solution reveals a universal property: to first order in the observer's proper time, the evolution of the metric and of the four-momentum crossing the light cones is independent of the specific behavior of matter. As an application, we derive, for an observer immersed in matter, an analogue of the integral mass formula of black hole mechanics.

</details>


### [70] [The Weyl Tensor for Gravitational-Wave Causal Sets: Thickness of Geodesic Analogs and Geodesic Deviation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09056)
*Nauman Ibrahim,Luca Bombelli*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究在平面引力波时空中撒入因果集（causal set），并探讨利用测地线偏离（geodesic deviation）的离散类比来提取外尔张量（Weyl tensor）分量的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果集理论（causal set theory）假设最小尺度下时空为偏序集，并应能在离散尺度之上重现连续几何的全部特征；本文旨在探索如何从这种离散结构中探测曲率。

Method: 将因果集撒入线性化平面引力波（linearized plane gravitational wave）时空；以一对元素间所有最长链（longest chains）的集合作为二者间类时测地线（timelike geodesic）的离散类比，并通过不同维闵可夫斯基时空（Minkowski spacetime）的数值模拟考察该集合的展宽。

Result: 数值模拟显示，最长链集合的展宽或“厚度”（thickness）随链长增加而增大；在理想元间空间距离估计器与振幅 $h_0=10^{-2}$ 的假设下，至少需要 $N_{\mathrm{total}} \gtrsim \mathcal{O}(10^8)$ 个元素才能克服厚度噪声并探测到曲率。

Conclusion: 该元素数量超出当前约 $\mathcal{O}(10^6)$ 的可模拟规模；后续工作或可从撒入的因果集中提取更多信息，从而降低对 $N_{\mathrm{total}}$ 的估计需求。

Abstract: Causal set theory proposes that spacetime is replaced by a partially ordered set (a directed acyclic graph) at the smallest scales. This graph is postulated to produce all the features of continuous geometry above some discreteness scale. We develop an approach to sprinkling causal sets into a linearized plane gravitational wave spacetime, and report on the feasibility of using the analog of geodesic deviation to find a component of the Weyl tensor for such causal sets. We take the collection of all longest chains between a pair of elements to be the discrete analog of the timelike geodesic between them and find through numerical simulations in Minkowski spacetime of various dimensions that the spread or "thickness" of the collection increases with chain length. We estimate, assuming an ideal element-to-element spatial distance estimator and a wave of amplitude $h_0 = 10^{-2}$, that at least $N_{\mathrm{total}} \gtrsim \mathcal{O}(10^8)$ elements are needed to overcome the noise introduced by the thickness and detect the curvature. This is beyond the $\mathcal{O}(10^6)$ elements we can currently simulate. Future work may show ways of extracting more information from the sprinkled causal set and hence lowering the estimate for $N_{\mathrm{total}}$.

</details>

Method: 将云的占据数与二维、轨道平均的Fokker–Planck方程（Fokker–Planck equation）耦合，纳入恒星弛豫（stellar relaxation）、俘获、轨道反作用以及云与恒星轨道的引力波（gravitational-wave, GW）辐射；对24个模型进行演化，黑洞质量M=10^5–10^7 M_⊙，耦合0.04≤α≤0.40。

Conclusion: 恒星电离是超辐射饱和后玻色子云耗散的重要通道，其效率依赖于云的空间延展与恒星动力学补充；致密云则以引力波辐射耗散为主。

Abstract: Stellar perturbations can ionize a superradiant boson cloud while transferring energy and angular momentum from the perturbing orbits. We evolve 24 models with black hole masses $M=10^5$--$10^7M_{\odot}$ and couplings $0.04\leqα\leq0.40$. Stars with semimajor axes of a few Bohr radii, $r_c=M/α^2$, dominate ionization. Stellar replenishment from the outer cluster sustains ionization over hundreds of Myr, particularly for extended clouds. Of the 24 models, 21 lose at least $99\%$ of their initial cloud mass. *Abdullah Guvendi,Omar Mustafa*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在厘清光学度规本身、其反射双层几何完备化与外部给定规范场三者的构造边界，并检验洛伦兹违反径向因子对零测地线传播及连续体类比量子散射谱的影响。

We further separate fixed-$\ell$ from fixed-$Λ_e$ parameter protocols and audit the optical effective-mass degeneracy using Lorentzian curvature invariants, timelike geodesics, and a massive Klein--Gordon probe. Method: 从完整辅助场方程（auxiliary-field equations）出发，指出 Dirac–Kähler 物质方程包含一个标量方程与一个两形式相容性条件（two-form consistency condition），后者对径向标量自动满足；随后推导适用于一般有反作用（backreacted）静态球对称几何的精确积分恒等式，并给出 GfE 真空几何的微扰解以定位曲率显著区域。

Result: 在采样标量与径向辅助场本征值为正、且 m²+ξR 非负的假设下，正则且无穷远趋于零的标量解必须恒为零，故在正则恒等连通分支（identity-connected branch）上不存在 m²≥0 的最小耦合静态标量毛发；进一步估计表明，即使 m²+ξR 变为负值也不足以产生正则静态零模（zero mode），仍存在一段有限非最小耦合区间无法从真空解分岔出新的分支。

Conclusion: GfE 的纯标量扇区对静态标量毛发具有强约束：符号判据（sign argument）能排除一大类构型，且非最小耦合的规避空间受限于有限区间，说明该框架下静态标量毛发的存在条件相当苛刻。

Abstract: We investigate static, spherically symmetric black holes in Gravity from Entropy (GfE), focusing on the sector in which the bosonic form-space field is a pure scalar. The latter is automatically satisfied by a radial scalar. We derive an exact integral identity, valid for a general backreacted static spherical geometry, and prove that a regular scalar approaching zero at infinity must vanish if the sampled scalar and radial auxiliary-field eigenvalues are positive and the effective mass squared $m^2+ξR$ is nonnegative. In particular, this excludes minimally coupled static scalar hair with $m^2\geq0$ on the regular identity-connected branch. We also present the perturbative GfE vacuum geometry and use its curvature to identify the region in which nonminimal coupling can evade the sign argument. A second, sharper estimate shows that making $m^2+ξR$ negative is not by itself sufficient to produce a regular static zero mode: a finite interval of nonminimal couplings still admits no branch bifurcating from the vacuum solution.

</details>

<details>

Result: 渐近对称性生成时间重参数化（time reparametrizations）代数；当规范参数依赖Schwarzian模时，等距条件（isometry condition）选取定态KdV层级（stationary KdV hierarchy），且该层级亦由扰动系数上的真空Einstein方程独立施加。欧氏区中，每一模各自独立产生对近极端熵（near-extremal entropy）的奇次幂修正序列（tower of odd-power corrections）。

Conclusion: 结果表明广义Schwarzian热力学的KdV层级结构与熵修正可由四维近极端Kerr的精确扰动自然给出，两类模分别独立贡献奇次幂熵修正，为近极端黑洞热力学与四维引力扰动之间建立了直接对应。

Abstract: We derive the generalized Schwarzian thermodynamics of near-extremal Kerr directly from four-dimensional gravitational perturbations of the near-horizon geometry. Both perturbations are smooth solutions of the vacuum Einstein equations reduced to the Jackiw--Teitelboim equation for the dual scalar, without conical singularities. We study their asymptotic symmetries and show that they generate an algebra of time reparametrizations; requiring the gauge parameters to depend on the Schwarzian mode, the isometry condition selects the stationary KdV hierarchy. The same hierarchy is independently enforced by the vacuum Einstein equations on the perturbation coefficient. In the Euclidean regime, we evaluate the Hamiltonian action and show that each mode independently generates a tower of odd-power corrections to the near-extremal entropy.

</details>

Conclusion: 结论是：非对易效应对表面引力的改变会导致高频发射谱的压低，从而使非对易启发式 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞的粒子产生谱显著区别于经典情形。

Abstract: By considering a massless Klein-Gordon field and the moving mirror model, we calculate particle production for a noncommutative-inspired Reissner-Nordström black hole sourced by smeared distributions of mass and charge. ### [77] [Fortuitous Cancellation of Competing Effects in the Free Energy of Water Autoionization: Long-Range Electrostatics, System Size, and Nuclear Quantum Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08874)
*Samuel Varner,Yinan Xu,Yezhi Jin,Gustavo R. Pérez-Lemus,Pablo F. Zubieta Rico,Zhiying Yi,Joan M. Montes de Oca,Paul F. Nealey,Seth B. Darling,Zhen-Gang Wang,Juan J. de Pablo*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作结合机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）与谱自适应偏置力增强采样，构建从电中性水到溶剂分离离子对（SSIP）的完整自由能面，揭示水自电离的热力学受长程静电、体系尺寸、温度与核量子效应竞争性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水的自电离（autoionization）平衡常数约10^-14，是酸碱平衡与生命化学的基础，但形成并分离离子的自由能代价的分子起源仍不清楚；以往研究受限于难以对水反应性进行足够精细的建模。

Method: 将机器学习原子间势（machine learning interatomic potentials, MLIPs）与谱自适应偏置力（spectral adaptive biasing force）增强采样相结合，把质子转移与“水线”（water wire）构型与自由能景观联系起来，并考察长程静电、体系尺寸、温度与核量子运动的影响。

Result: 自由能面显示过渡态中质子在三分子水线的第二、三分子间共享；接触离子对（contact ion pair）不稳定，SSIP仅弱亚稳，故求pK_w需采样更长水线；长程静电使3 Å以外离子分离的自由能升高，且力误差相近的模型在该区域给出显著不同的自由能曲线；增大体系尺寸降低计算得到的pK_w，核量子效应使其更接近实验并再现D₂O相对H₂O更高的pK_w。

Conclusion: 研究为解释有限元胞中带电与极性基团反应扩散的MLIP模拟热力学结果建立了基准，并说明竞争效应间的相互抵消可使模拟在遗漏若干物理贡献时仍给出接近实验的电离常数。

Abstract: Few molecular processes are more fundamental to chemistry than the autoionization of water, whose equilibrium constant, $K_w \approx 10^{-14}$, underlies acid-base equilibria and the chemistry of life. Yet the molecular origins of the free energy cost of forming and separating ions remain incompletely understood. Efforts to explain it have been limited by the difficulty of modeling water reactivity in sufficient detail. Here we combine machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) with spectral adaptive biasing force enhanced sampling to connect proton transfer and water wire configurations to the underlying free energy landscape. We identify competing influences of long-range electrostatics, system size, temperature, and nuclear quantum motion on the predicted ionization thermodynamics. The free energy surface extends from neutral water to ions separated beyond the solvent-separated ion pair (SSIP). It reveals a transition state in which a proton is shared between the second and third molecules of a three-membered water wire. The contact ion pair is unstable and the SSIP only weakly metastable, so determining pK$_w$ requires sampling longer wires. Long-range electrostatics raise the free energy at ion separations beyond $3\,\mathrm{\mathring{A}}$. Models with comparable force errors give substantially different free energy profiles in this region. Increasing system size lowers the calculated pK$_w$, while nuclear quantum effects bring it closer to experiment and reproduce the higher pK$_w$ of D$_2$O relative to H$_2$O. These results establish a baseline for interpreting thermodynamic results from MLIP simulations in which charged and polar groups react and diffuse in finite cells. Our analysis also explains how cancellation among competing effects can yield ionization constants close to experiment even when simulations omit several physical contributions.

</details>


### [78] [Increased Crystal Nucleation in the Glassy State of a Polymer/Water Mixture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09456)
*Mika Kobayashi,Shingo Kobayashi,Kazuyoshi Shiga,Hiroaki Yamada,Masaru Tanaka*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该摘要报道在生物相容性聚合物（poly(2-methoxyethyl acrylate)，PMEA）/水混合体系中，成核（nucleation）在玻璃化转变温度 $T_g$ 以下异常增强，并在约 $T_g-10$ K 处出现有效成核速率最大值，挑战经典成核理论（classical nucleation theory）的传统图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典成核理论认为晶体成核需要足够过饱和度（supersaturation），而低温下分子迁移率（molecular mobility）降低会抑制成核，导致成核速率最大值通常位于 $T_g$ 之上。作者旨在检验这一传统图像在特定体系中的适用性，并解释冷却与加热之间结晶行为的显著不对称。

Method: 以生物相容性聚合物 PMEA/水混合物为实验对象，考察 $T_g$ 以下的成核行为；结合量热实验（calorimetric experiments）分析水合 PMEA 中水的冷结晶（cold crystallization）对温度历史的依赖性。

Result: 实验发现 $T_g$ 以下晶核（nuclei）数量显著增加，有效成核速率在约 $T_g-10$ K 处呈现不寻常的最大值；量热实验显示水合 PMEA 中水的冷结晶具有强烈的温度历史依赖性，且该现象可由所观察到的成核行为解释。

Conclusion: 作者提出，$T_g$ 以下成核的异常增强是联系高玻璃形成能力（glass-forming ability）与加热时冷结晶的关键机制，可解释冷却与加热间结晶行为的显著不对称；并推测其他玻璃形成液体（glass-forming liquids）的冷结晶也可能源于 $T_g$ 以下隐藏的成核速率最大值。

Abstract: Within classical nucleation theory, crystal nucleation requires sufficient supersaturation, but is suppressed at low temperatures due to reduced molecular mobility, leading to a maximum nucleation rate typically above the glass transition temperature $T_g$. Here, we experimentally show that this conventional picture breaks down in a biocompatible polymer poly(2-methoxyethyl acrylate)(PMEA)/water mixture. We find that the number of nuclei increases dramatically below $T_g$, with the effective nucleation rate exhibiting an unusual maximum at approximately $T_g-10$ K. Calorimetric experiments reveal a strong temperature-history dependence of cold crystallization of water in hydrated PMEA, which is explained by the observed nucleation behavior. We propose that the unusual enhancement of nucleation below $T_g$ provides a key mechanism linking high glass-forming ability and cold crystallization upon heating, thereby explaining the striking asymmetry in crystallization behavior between cooling and heating. Our findings suggest that a hidden nucleation-rate maximum below $T_g$ may also underlie cold crystallization in other glass-forming liquids.

</details>


### [79] [Propagating the Hierarchical Equations of Motion using the Multi-Layer Multi-Configurational Time-Dependent Hartree method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09637)
*Robin L. Grether,Samuel L. Rudge,Martin Mäck,Riley J. Preston,Uwe Manthe,Michael Thoss*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将分层运动方程（HEOM）的双空间表述与多组态含时Hartree方法（MCTDH）结合，实现费米型HEOM的高效传播，并在Holstein型及强振动耦合纳米结模型中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HEOM是开放量子系统（open quantum systems）时间演化的数值精确方法，但计算代价随系统规模急剧增长；已有张量列（tensor-train）与张量网络（tensor-network）方法被用于拓展其适用范围，仍需更高效的传播方案处理更大、更真实的体系。

Method: 基于已有的HEOM双空间（twin-space）表述，采用多组态含时Hartree方法（multi-configurational time-dependent Hartree method, MCTDH）对费米型HEOM（fermionic HEOM）进行高效时间传播，构建HEOM+MCTDH方法。

Result: 以Holstein型纳米结（Holstein-type nanojunction）模型的电子输运（electron transport）计算作为基准测试；并进一步应用于包含强分子-电极耦合（molecule-electrode coupling）的复杂振动耦合纳米结（vibronic coupling nanojunction）模型。

Conclusion: HEOM+MCTDH可有效扩展HEOM的适用规模，并首次使强振动耦合纳米结模型的完全量子（fully quantum）模拟成为可行。

Abstract: The hierarchical equations of motion (HEOM) are a powerful, numerically exact approach for simulating the time evolution of open quantum systems. Recently, several tensor-train- and tensor-network-based approaches have been suggested and realized to extend the range of applicability of the HEOM approach to larger and more realistic systems. In this paper, we build upon the existing twin-space formulation of the HEOM and present a novel approach to propagate the fermionic HEOM efficiently using the multi-configurational time-dependent Hartree method (MCTDH). We benchmark the resulting HEOM+MCTDH method with electron transport calculations for a Holstein-type nanojunction model. Furthermore, we demonstrate the capabilities of the HEOM+MCTDH method by applying it to a more complex vibronic coupling nanojunction model with strong molecule-electrode coupling for which fully quantum simulations have not been feasible previously.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多丝电化学金属化忆阻器（electrochemical metallization memristor）建模依赖多参数微观模拟或难以对应物理量的唯象规律，缺乏参数量少且具有物理意义的解析描述。

Result: 模型重现双极响应（含电压控制负微分电阻，negative differential resistance），并在不重新拟合的情况下预测单极扫描（unipolar sweep）及脉冲响应幅度（其中出现状态记忆）；第二个样品亦由同一模型描述。真空中器件不开关且无负微分电阻，残余高压电流符合离子跳跃导电（ionic hopping conduction）。

Conclusion: 该平均场模型以少量具物理意义的参数统一描述多丝忆阻器的双极与单极行为，并支持吸附水提供对反应、真空下离子跳跃导电的物理解释；但该判断仅基于摘要所述单一材料体系与两个样品。

Abstract: Memristors based on electrochemical metallization with many filaments are usually modeled by microscopic simulations with many parameters or by phenomenological laws whose parameters are hard to relate to physical quantities. We present an analytical mean-field model for a population of Ag filaments confined in the pores of a nanoporous matrix, built on Ohm's law, Faraday's law and a power-based rupture criterion. The filaments have a truncated normal distribution of cross-sections and rupture leaving lognormally distributed gaps whose median grows with the dissipated power. Gap closing shifts the distribution of squared gaps rigidly, and the model therefore admits closed-form expressions for the switching voltage, the read current, the rupture branch and the high-voltage current. We apply it to Ag/nanoporous anodic alumina/Al structures fabricated from industrial-grade aluminum, with a 2.4$μ$m alumina layer, and measured under ambient conditions. The parameters were fitted to the bipolar sweeps, with the gap dispersion taken from the temporal shape of the read after a pulse. They reproduce the bipolar response, including voltage-controlled negative differential resistance, and predict without refitting the unipolar sweeps and the amplitude of the pulse response, where state memory appears. A second sample, with a larger contact deposited chemically, is described by the same model. In vacuum the devices neither switch nor show negative differential resistance, as expected if adsorbed water supplies the counter reaction; the residual high-voltage current follows ionic hopping conduction.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在 PbTe 生长与 Pb 沉积之间引入关键制备步骤：破真空前用 Te 帽层（Te capping layer）覆盖纳米线顶面以支持非原位加工；随后去除掩膜并制备阴影墙（shadow walls）；将芯片重新装入生长腔后通过热分解（thermal decomposition）去除 Te 保护层，再沉积 Pb 薄膜。

Result: 尽管中间经历非原位处理，仍可获得具有原位等效质量（in-situ-equivalent quality）的洁净 PbTe-Pb 界面，并实现从 PbTe 纳米线延伸至衬底的连续 Pb 薄膜。

Conclusion: 该技术可支撑复杂设计的先进 PbTe-Pb 量子器件（quantum devices）开发，为实现拓扑量子比特（topological qubits）提供了工艺路径。

Abstract: We report recent progress in the growth and fabrication of advanced PbTe-Pb Majorana devices for three-terminal correlation experiments. A critical prerequisite for these devices is a continuous Pb film spanning from the PbTe nanowire to the substrate. In previous studies, PbTe nanowires were spatially separated from the substrate mask, making them unable to fulfill this requirement. To address this issue, we have developed a new growth and fabrication approach. Key fabrication steps are introduced between the PbTe growth and Pb deposition. The top facet of the PbTe nanowire is covered with a Te capping layer before breaking the ultra-high vacuum, enabling ex situ processing for subsequent steps. The mask is then removed, followed by the fabrication of shadow walls. After reloading the chip into the growth chamber, the Te protection layer is removed through thermal decomposition, and the Pb film is subsequently deposited. Notably, a pristine PbTe-Pb interface with an in-situ-equivalent quality can be achieved despite the ex-situ processing. Our technique facilitates the develop of advanced PbTe-Pb quantum devices with complex designs, paving the way toward the realization of topological qubits.

</details>


### [82] [Coulomb screening of the orbital inverse Faraday effect in finite-size diffusive conductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09068)
*A. A. Kopasov,A. S. Mel'nikov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文基于含自洽静电场的玻尔兹曼方程，建立有限尺寸扩散导体中逆法拉第效应的动理学理论，表明库仑屏蔽会将二阶直流响应的峰值移向更高驱动频率并压低峰值幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有对逆法拉第效应（inverse Faraday effect）的描述多采用非自洽近似，忽略库仑屏蔽（Coulomb screening）对有限尺寸扩散导体中光致直流磁化的影响，其定量预测的适用边界尚不明确。

Method: 从玻尔兹曼方程（Boltzmann equation）出发并耦合自洽静电场，构建二维圆盘在圆偏振交流电场驱动下的动理学理论；一阶电荷调制与感生电场通过库仑相互作用耦合，自洽一阶解给出二阶直流环流的非线性源；径向问题采用角谐展开（angular-harmonic expansion）与切比雪夫配点法（Chebyshev collocation method）数值求解。

Result: 非自洽近似下，当辐射频率对应的扩散长度（diffusion length）与样品半径相当时，二阶直流响应随频率出现极大值；引入自洽屏蔽后该极大值向更高驱动频率移动且峰值幅度被抑制，该现象在面积积分环流与感生磁矩上一致出现。

Conclusion: 结果厘清了逆法拉第效应非自洽描述的局限，并表明库仑自洽性（Coulomb self-consistency）对有限尺寸扩散导体中光致直流磁化（optically induced dc magnetization）的定量预测至关重要。

Abstract: We study the effects of Coulomb screening on the second-order dc response in finite-size diffusive conductors. Starting from the Boltzmann equation supplemented by the self-consistent electrostatic field, we develop a kinetic theory of the inverse Faraday effect for a two-dimensional disk driven by a circularly polarized ac electric field. In this formulation, the first-order charge modulation and the induced electric field are coupled through the Coulomb interaction, and the self-consistent first-order solution determines the nonlinear source for the second-order dc circulating current responsible for the induced magnetic moment. The radial problem is solved numerically using an angular-harmonic expansion and the Chebyshev collocation method. It is shown that in the non-self-consistent approximation the frequency dependence of the second-order dc response has a maximum when the diffusion length associated with the radiation frequency becomes comparable to the sample radius. This behavior is found to be strongly modified by self-consistent screening. We demonstrate that Coulomb screening shifts the maximum of the dc response to a higher drive frequency and suppresses the peak amplitude. This behavior is observed consistently for the area-integrated circulating current and the induced magnetic moment. The obtained results clarify the limitations of the non-self-consistent description of the inverse Faraday effect and show that Coulomb self-consistency is crucial for quantitative predictions of optically induced dc magnetization in finite-size diffusive conductors.

</details>

TL;DR: 在S/S堆垛构型的对称双层Janus WSeS中观测到热稳定的增强三子（trion）发光；反平行内建电场在界面形成强电子限域，低温下三子发光占主导，且强电子-声子耦合使其在高温下保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Janus过渡金属硫族化合物（transition metal dichalcogenides）的镜面对称性破缺为研究自旋-谷-声子（spin-valley-phonon）相互作用介导的多体效应提供了新平台，而强电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）需要强有效偶极矩与纳米尺度电荷限域（charge confinement）的精确匹配。

Method: 采用S/S堆垛构型的对称双层Janus WSeS结构，利用其向内指向的反平行内建电场（antiparallel built-in electric fields）在界面处实现强电子限域，并通过界面构型调控（interfacial architecture tailoring）研究其发光特性。

Result: 反平行内建电场在界面诱导强电子限域效应，使低温下三子发光占主导；与光学声子A_1^2能量（51 meV）共振的巨大电子-声子耦合强度（3.9）促成了该三子发光在高温下的热稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过调控Janus WSeS同质双层（homobilayer）的界面结构，该研究克服了传统半导体体系固有的热退相干（thermal decoherence）限制，为室温多体相互作用研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Janus transition metal dichalcogenides with broken mirror symmetry provide a novel platform for exploring many-body effects mediated by spin-valley-phonon interactions. Strong electron-phonon coupling demands a precise match between a strong effective dipole moment and nanometer-scale charge confinement. Here, we report thermally robust and enhanced trion emission in a symmetric bilayer WSeS with an S/S stacking configuration. The inward-pointing antiparallel built-in electric fields induce a strong electron confinement effect at the interface, driving dominant trion emission at cryogenic temperatures. Furthermore, a massive electron-phonon coupling strength (3.9) resonant with the optical phonon A_1^2 energy (51 meV) contributes to the thermal stability of this trion emission at elevated temperatures. By tailoring the interfacial architecture in Janus WSeS homobilayers, this study overcomes the thermal decoherence limits inherent to traditional semiconductor systems, offering novel insights into room-temperature many-body interactions.

</details>


### [84] [Disentangling Spin and Orbital Contributions in the Search for Altermagnetic Terahertz Conversion in RuO2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09098)
*Jiahao Liu,Yuxiao Mo,Tongyang Guan,Wenhui Fang,Yongwei Cui,Wentao Qin,Zhe Yuan,Lishu Zhang,Zhensheng Tao,Yizheng Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用太赫兹发射光谱首次实验区分了RuO2异质结中自旋流与轨道流到电荷的转换通道，发现以Fe为注入层时无逆交错磁自旋劈裂效应证据，观测到的各向异性可由近界面环境调制的各向异性逆自旋霍尔响应解释，而轨道-电荷转换在RuO2中基本各向同性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金红石RuO2被视为交错磁体（altermagnet）的重要候选材料，但其已报道的电荷-角动量转换的微观起源尚未厘清；近期理论与实验提示轨道输运（orbital transport）可能占主导，导致自旋介导与轨道介导的转换之间存在根本性歧义。

Method: 采用太赫兹发射光谱（terahertz emission spectroscopy）对RuO2异质结进行实验，通过选用Fe、Ni和坡莫合金（permalloy）作为不同注入层来区分自旋流与轨道流的注入通道，并在受控自旋流注入条件下考察各向异性响应。

Result: Fe被鉴定为以自旋流注入为主，Ni与坡莫合金则以轨道流注入为主，表明轨道对超快太赫兹发射有显著贡献；在受控自旋流注入下未发现逆交错磁自旋劈裂效应（inverse altermagnetic spin-splitting effect）的证据，观测到的各向异性与受近界面环境强烈制约的各向异性逆自旋霍尔响应（inverse spin Hall response）一致；轨道流注入下各向异性明显更弱，说明RuO2中轨道-电荷转换基本为各向同性。

Conclusion: 轨道输运与界面调制的自旋霍尔转换是RuO2太赫兹发射的关键机制；在将各向异性归因于交错磁性之前，须先区分自旋、轨道与界面三类贡献。

Abstract: Rutile RuO2 has emerged as a prominent candidate altermagnet, yet the microscopic origin of its reported charge-angular-momentum conversion remains unsettled. Recent theoretical and experimental advances have highlighted a potentially dominant role of orbital transport, raising a fundamental ambiguity between spin- and orbital-mediated conversion in RuO2. Here, we report the first experimental disentanglement of the spin- and orbital-to-charge conversion channels in RuO2 heterostructures using terahertz emission spectroscopy. We identify Fe as a predominantly spin-current injector, whereas Ni and permalloy predominantly inject orbital currents, revealing a substantial orbital contribution to ultrafast terahertz emission. Under controlled spin-current injection, we find no evidence for inverse altermagnetic spin-splitting effect. Instead, the observed anisotropy is consistent with an anisotropic inverse spin Hall response that is strongly conditioned by the near-interface environment. Under orbital-current injection, the anisotropy is markedly weaker, indicating that orbital-to-charge conversion is largely isotropic in RuO2. Our results establish orbital transport and interface-conditioned spin Hall conversion as key to terahertz emission from RuO2, underscoring the need to disentangle spin, orbital, and interfacial contributions before assigning anisotropy to altermagnetism.

</details>


### [85] [Momentum-space engineering in van der Waals heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09285)
*Xiao Li,Astrid Weston,James G. McHugh,Michael D. Thompson,Matthew Watson,Yunze Gao,Nicholas Clark,Laxman Nagi Reddy,Samuel Magorrian,Wendong Wang,Zhiyuan Wang,Sofia Konyzheva,Francisco Selles,Amy Carl,Katarzyna J. Salamon,Alex Summerfield,Nicholas Hine,Andre K. Geim,Neil R. Wilson,Jonathan R. Prance,Andrey V. Kretinin,Vladimir I. Falko,Roman Gorbachev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出动量空间“Umklapp”能带工程（Umklapp band engineering）这一互补性策略，通过匹配两层高度失配二维晶体的带边倒空间位置实现共振层间杂化，并在扭转石墨烯/MoS₂体系中实验验证了额外狄拉克型谱特征的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二十年来石墨烯（graphene）中狄拉克费米子（Dirac fermions）的调控主要依赖实空间超晶格（real-space superlattices）的微带工程（miniband engineering），该路线存在局限；因此需要一种互补的、基于动量空间的能带调控途径。

Method: 采用动量空间Umklapp能带工程：通过布拉格矢量（Bragg vectors）的平移，使两个相邻且高度失配（highly incommensurate）二维晶体的带边倒空间位置重合，产生共振层间杂化（resonant interlayer hybridisation）。实验上采用单层石墨烯/MoS₂，通过扭转使MoS₂的Q谷态经Umklapp与石墨烯狄拉克锥（Dirac cones）达到能量-动量共振。

Result: 杂化产生了清晰的额外狄拉克型谱特征，由特征性朗道能级扇（Landau-level fan）与舒勃尼科夫-德哈斯振荡（Shubnikov-de Haas oscillations）所证实，对应贝里相位π（Berry phase π）电子。该新拓扑谱特征形成自旋-谷锁定的Ising体系，对小扭转角变化稳健，并可通过外部位移场（displacement field）进一步调控。

Conclusion: Umklapp能带工程为调控范德华异质结（van der Waals heterostructures）的电子结构提供了实用策略，将量子材料的设计空间拓展至莫尔超晶格（moiré superlattices）与弱近邻效应（weak proximity effects）之外。

Abstract: Twenty years of graphene research have established Dirac fermions as a versatile platform for exploring quantum topological phenomena. Yet control over graphene's low-energy spectrum mostly follows the route of miniband engineering using real-space superlattices. Here we introduce a complementary approach based on momentum-space 'Umklapp' band engineering. This is realised by matching reciprocal space positions of band edges of two adjacent highly incommensurate 2D crystals to coincide upon a boost from their Bragg vectors, creating resonant interlayer hybridisation that qualitatively reconstructs their spectra. To demonstrate this approach experimentally, we use monolayer graphene on MoS$_2$, twisted such that MoS$_2$ Q-valley states are brought by Umklapp into energy-momentum resonance with graphene's Dirac cones. The ensuing hybridization results in the formation of a clear additional Dirac-like spectral feature, identified by a distinct Landau-level fan and Shubnikov-de Haas oscillations characteristic to Berry phase $π$ electrons. These new topological spectral features create a spin-valley locked Ising system which is robust against small twist-angle variations that can be further tuned by an external displacement field. More broadly, Umklapp band engineering offers a practical strategy for tailoring the electronic structure in van der Waals heterostructures, expanding the design space of quantum materials beyond moiré superlattices and weak proximity effects.

</details>


### [86] [Josephson current along the domain walls in Weyl fully compensated ferrimagnet Ti$_2$MnAl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09547)
*V. D. Esin,N. N. Orlova,A. R. Banschikova,Yu. S. Barash,D. N. Borisenko,N. N. Kolesnikov,E. V. Deviatov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在Nb-Ti₂MnAl-Nb平面邻近结中观察到约2 μm间距的约瑟夫森效应，磁场方向不同时临界电流各向异性显著：平行场呈Fraunhofer型抑制，垂直场则表现为非平凡分布。作者据此推断超电流由拓扑补偿铁磁体Ti₂MnAl畴壁处的 chiral 表面态承载，并以小薄片磁化数据佐证畴壁的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探测拓扑补偿铁磁体（fully compensated ferrimagnet）Ti₂MnAl中可能存在的畴壁手性表面态（chiral surface states）及其对超导电流输运的作用，需实验验证此类拓扑态能否作为长程超电流通道。

Method: 制备超导铌（Nb）电极与Ti₂MnAl单晶薄片构成的平面约瑟夫森邻近器件（proximity devices），测量2 μm电极间距下的临界电流随磁场的变化 I_c(B)，比较平行与垂直两种磁场取向下的行为，并辅以小尺寸Ti₂MnAl薄片的磁化（magnetization）测量。

Result: 平行磁场下I_c(B)呈标准Fraunhofer型抑制；垂直磁场下I_c(B)定性不同，表明结平面内约瑟夫森电流分布非平凡。此外观察到长程超电流（long-range supercurrent）及I_c(B)中的快速非周期涨落，磁化数据支持小薄片中存在畴壁。

Conclusion: 综合各向异性、长程超电流与非周期涨落，推断约瑟夫森电流由沿畴壁（domain walls）出现的手性表面态输运，畴壁判断得到磁化数据支持；该结果为拓扑补偿铁磁体中畴壁手性态承载超电流提供了实验证据。

Abstract: We experimentally investigate Josephson current in planar Nb-Ti$_2$MnAl-Nb proximity devices, which are formed as junctions between superconducting niobium leads and single crystal flakes of the Weyl fully compensated ferrimagnet Ti$_2$MnAl. We demonstrate well developed Josephson effect for 2~$μ$m distance between the niobium contacts on the Ti$_2$MnAl surface. The measured critical current dependence on the magnetic field $I_c(B)$ shows strong anisotropy for two magnetic field orientations in respect to the Nb-Ti$_2$MnAl-Nb junction plane. For the parallel magnetic field, one can see standard Fraunhofer-like $I_c(B)$ suppression pattern. In contrast, $I_c(B)$ is qualitatively different in normal magnetic field, indicating non-trivial distribution of the Josephson current within the junction plane. From this anisotropy, long-range supercurrent and fast aperiodic fluctuations in $I_c(B)$ we conclude that Josephson current is transferred by the chiral surface states, which emerge along the domain walls in topological compensated ferrimagnet Ti$_2$MnAl. The conclusion on the domain walls is supported by magnetization data for small Ti$_2$MnAl flakes.

</details>


### [87] [Quantum Interference Control of Coupled Atomic Spins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09578)
*Sergio Santiago Gomez,Gustavo Mauricio Meneses Ancalle,Daniel Domínguez,María José Sánchez,Alejandro Ferrón*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于朗道-齐纳-施蒂克尔伯格-马约拉纳干涉（LZSM interferometry）的原子尺度自旋体系非共振电控方案，在MgO薄层上的Ti-Ti-Fe三聚体平台实现远程、非共振、非局域的自旋量子操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子尺度自旋体系是量子比特（qubit）的候选平台，但隧穿电子引起的退相干与操控速度受限制约其可扩展性；需要一种可电学驱动、快速且低退相干的相干控制手段。

Method: 先分析无Fe原子的Ti二聚体，通过光谱研究证明可结合外磁场面内分量、针尖磁场与偏置电压工程化避免交叉（avoided level crossings），并据此发展非共振LZSM-STM相干态控制协议；随后研究完整三聚体，利用Fe原子在远端Ti原子处产生的局域磁场实现非局域自旋控制。

Result: 束前方法得到验证；三聚体中Fe原子的局域磁场可实现远端自旋的非局域操控，该远程非共振操控减少了隧穿电子相关的不利效应，并支持短时间尺度的量子操作。

Conclusion: 量子干涉可作为原子尺度体系中电驱动、非共振、非局域自旋控制的资源，兼顾快速量子操作与退相干抑制，为可扩展的原子基量子比特架构提供可行路径。

Abstract: We propose a protocol for the non-resonant electric control of quantum states in atomic-scale spin systems using Landau-Zener-Stückelberg-Majorana (LZSM) interferometry. We focus on a Ti-Ti-Fe trimer on a thin MgO layer, a platform that has recently emerged as a promising candidate for qubit realization using electron spin resonance combined with scanning tunneling microscopy (ESR-STM). We first analyze a Ti dimer in the absence of the Fe atom and show, through a detailed spectroscopic study, that avoided level crossings can be engineered by combining the in-plane component of the external magnetic field, the tip magnetic field, and the applied bias voltage, which allows the system to be tuned electrically through these crossings. Based on this, we develop LZSM protocols for coherent state control in the dimer using non-resonant LZSM-STM. We then consider the full trimer, where the Fe atom generates a local magnetic field at the remote Ti atom, enabling non-local spin control. We show that this remote and non-resonant manipulation reduces unwanted effects associated with tunneling electrons and enables quantum operations on short timescales. Our results show that quantum interference can be used as a resource for electrically driven, non-resonant, and non-local spin control in atomic-scale systems. This approach enables fast quantum operations while mitigating decoherence, offering a promising route toward scalable atom-based qubit architectures.

</details>


### [88] [Scanning Probe Control, Quantum Dynamics, and Dispersive Readout of Suspended Carbon Nanotube Resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09581)
*Cristian Staii*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文为悬空碳纳米管（suspended carbon nanotubes, CNTs）建立统一理论框架，将原子力显微镜（AFM）针尖驱动、Duffing非线性量子化与库珀对盒（Cooper-pair box, CPB）色散读出整合于同一器件，评估相干操控与态分辨检测的可行条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 悬空CNT具有小有效质量、强静电可调性与机械非线性，适合量子纳米力学（quantum nanomechanics），但缺乏将局域驱动、非线性量子动力学与超导态分辨检测统一描述的理论框架，且AFM力梯度、针尖位置涨落与电压噪声等退相干来源的影响有待量化。

Method: 先推导CNT的线性与非线性Duffing响应，量化AFM力梯度、针尖位置涨落及电压噪声对机械稳定性、退相干（dephasing）与加热的影响；随后对Duffing振子作量子化处理，导出其Kerr非谐性并确定选择性操控单一声子跃迁的条件；最后推导CNT–CPB相互作用及其色散极限（dispersive limit）。

Result: 给出了低反作用（backaction）相干驱动的运行条件；估算热占据与主动冷却，界定54 MHz至5.37 GHz频段CNT模式的约束；表明声子数与宇称（parity）可通过Ramsey干涉测量映射至CPB，并用量子电容（quantum-capacitance）读出检测。

Conclusion: 该框架明确了局域AFM驱动、非线性量子动力学与超导态分辨检测可在单一CNT器件中协同工作的条件，为悬空CNT量子纳米力学实验提供了设计依据。

Abstract: Suspended carbon nanotubes (CNTs) offer promising opportunities for quantum nanomechanics owing to their small effective mass, strong electrostatic tunability, and mechanical nonlinearity. We develop a unified theoretical framework for coherent control and readout of a suspended CNT driven by an atomic force microscope (AFM) tip and capacitively coupled to a Cooper-pair box (CPB). We first derive the linear and nonlinear Duffing response of the CNT and quantify the effects of AFM force gradients, tip-position fluctuations, and voltage noise on mechanical stability, dephasing, and heating. These results establish operating conditions for coherent actuation with limited backaction. We then quantize the Duffing oscillator, derive its Kerr anharmonicity, and identify the requirements for selective control of individual vibrational transitions. Estimates of thermal occupation and active cooling illustrate the constraints for CNT modes spanning frequencies from 54 MHz to 5.37 GHz. Finally, we derive the CNT--CPB interaction and its dispersive limit, showing how phonon number and parity can be mapped onto the CPB through Ramsey interferometry and detected using quantum-capacitance readout. The resulting framework identifies the conditions under which localized AFM actuation, nonlinear quantum dynamics, and superconducting state-resolved detection can operate within a single CNT device.

</details>


### [89] [Local Correlations Determine the Dephasing Threshold of $\mathbb{Z}_2$ Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09668)
*Rishith Reddy,Ashutosh Dubey,Partha Sarathi,Ankur Das*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文以Kane-Mele Z₂拓扑绝缘体为平台，研究退相干（dephasing）下能带拓扑（band topology）是否存活。作者构造单粒子关联哈密顿量（correlation Hamiltonian），以其能隙闭合判定拓扑相变，并结合不变量直接计算与辅助体系的两端相干输运作为诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：在存在退相干的情形下，如何刻画与判定能带拓扑的存活（survival of band topology）。既有拓扑不变量定义于纯态/无噪声体系，噪声是否会破坏单粒子拓扑结构尚需明确的判据。

Method: 在Kane-Mele Z₂拓扑绝缘体中，构造一个单粒子哈密顿量，使其在单粒子层面给出相同的关联（identical correlation）；以该关联哈密顿量的能隙闭合（gap closing）确定拓扑到平庸的相变，并将其与纯度能隙（purity gap）相联系。诊断手段包括直接计算拓扑不变量，以及计算通过辅助关联哈密顿量的相干两端输运（coherent two-terminal transport）以观察非平庸区的量子化电导（quantized conductance）。

Result: 关联哈密顿量的能隙闭合标记拓扑—平庸相变，且该闭合与纯度能隙相关；直接计算的拓扑不变量与辅助关联哈密顿量的相干两端输运结果相互印证，非平庸区呈现量子化电导。作者明确指出，该电导并非退相干条件下的物理电导（physical conductance）。

Conclusion: 研究给出了一个可预测的局域判据（predictive local criterion），用于判断在特定噪声通道（specified noise channel）下单粒子拓扑结构的存活。该判据以关联哈密顿量的能隙闭合/纯度能隙为核心，而非依赖物理输运测量。

Abstract: In this work, we try to understand the question of how to address the survival of band topology in the presence of dephasing. We investigate this question in the Kane-Mele $\mathbb{Z}_2$ topological insulator. To determine the stability of the topological invariant we construct a single particle Hamiltonian that also provides identical correlation at the single particle level. The phase transition from topological to trivial is determined by the gap closing of this correlation Hamiltonian. This gap closing is related to purity gap. As a diagnostics we calculated the invariant directly and as a complementary diagnostic, coherent two-terminal transport through the auxiliary correlation Hamiltonian exhibits quantized conductance in the nontrivial region. This does not establish physical conductance under dephasing. Our results identify a predictive local criterion for the survival of single-particle topological structure under a specified noise channel.

</details>


### [90] [Cavity-enhanced second harmonic generation from Rydberg excitons in Cu$_2$O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09705)
*Horatio Q. X Wong,Krzysztof Sawicki,Madeleine Fisher,Stephen A. Lynch,Wolfgang Langbein,Sai Kiran Rajendran,Hamid Ohadi,Matthew P. A. Jones,Liam A. P. Gallagher*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用双共振光学微腔增强二次谐波产生（SHG）来研究Cu₂O中的里德堡激子，观察到偶宇称激子态并将腔基测量扩展至n=10，腔内SHG总增强因子为21±2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cu₂O中的里德堡激子（Rydberg excitons）为固态体系提供巨大光学非线性（optical nonlinearities）的平台，需要有效的探测与增强手段来研究并利用这些非线性。

Method: 采用二次谐波产生（second-harmonic generation, SHG）光谱，在同时增强泵浦场与二次谐波场的双共振光学微腔（doubly resonant optical microcavity）中测量Cu₂O里德堡激子，并通过改变泵浦腔失谐（pump cavity detuning）分析增强机制。

Result: 在光学腔中观察到偶宇称激子态（even-parity exciton states），将基于腔的里德堡激子测量扩展至主量子数n=10；SHG强度强烈依赖泵浦腔失谐，表明微腔主要作用是增强腔内泵浦场，共振二次谐波腔提供额外增强，总体腔内SHG增强因子为21±2。

Conclusion: 腔增强SHG光谱（cavity-enhanced SHG spectroscopy）是研究微腔中里德堡激子的有效工具，并为利用里德堡激子极化激元（Rydberg exciton polariton）体系中的光学非线性提供了路径。

Abstract: Rydberg excitons in Cu$_2$O provide a solid-state setting for creating giant optical nonlinearities. Here, we use second-harmonic generation (SHG) to study Rydberg excitons in a doubly resonant optical microcavity designed to enhance both the pump and second-harmonic fields. We observe even-parity exciton states in an optical cavity and extend cavity-based measurements of Rydberg excitons up to $n=10$. The SHG intensity depends strongly on pump cavity detuning, showing the dominant role of the microcavity is the enhancement of the intra-cavity pump field. Analysis reveals an additional enhancement from the resonant second-harmonic cavity giving an overall SHG enhancement factor of $21 \pm 2$ inside the microcavity. These results establish cavity-enhanced SHG spectroscopy as a valuable tool for studying Rydberg excitons in microcavities and provide a pathway towards exploiting optical nonlinearities in Rydberg exciton polariton systems.

</details>


### [91] [Exciton design via topological spin textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09800)
*Karim Rezouali,Selcuk Sözeri,Samir Lounis*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该理论工作建立了激子（exciton）与磁斯格明子（magnetic skyrmion）相互作用的理论框架，将电子与空穴视为具有各自自旋—轨道耦合（spin–orbit coupling）的独立组分。结果表明斯格明子拓扑可转移至激子，并借此实现对激子的俘获、光学选择定则调控以及由电流脉冲驱动的输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子（exciton）能无电荷地传输能量与信息，在光电子学（optoelectronics）中颇具吸引力但难以导引；磁斯格明子（magnetic skyrmion）则可由小电流驱动。二者的结合有望同时解决激子导引与拓扑磁比特（topological magnetic bit）与光学激发耦合的问题。

Method: 构建激子与斯格明子相互作用的理论，将激子中的电子与空穴作为独立组分处理，并分别为其引入自旋—轨道耦合（spin–orbit coupling）；在此基础上分析交换耦合（exchange coupling）与自旋—轨道劈裂（spin–orbit splitting）的相对大小，以及自旋织构（spin texture）的量子几何量（quantum metric）与拓扑荷（topological charge）的作用。

Result: 仅当交换耦合超过相关自旋—轨道劈裂时，斯格明子的拓扑才转移给激子，此时激子经历一个完整磁通量子（flux quantum），且在两谷（valley）中符号相反。自旋织构的量子度规形成环形势垒以俘获激子，拓扑荷则锁定俘获态的角动量（angular momentum），进而决定其光学选择定则（optical selection rules）。

Conclusion: 由于陷阱随斯格明子一同移动，激子可由电流脉冲输运；小尺寸斯格明子可容纳单个激子，为将拓扑磁比特与光学激发相结合的未来信息技术混合应用提供了可能。

Abstract: Excitons carry energy and information through a semiconductor without carrying charge. This makes them attractive for optoelectronics, but also hard to guide. Magnetic skyrmions, on the other hand, are moved by small electrical currents. Here we develop a theory of excitons interacting with a skyrmion in which the electron and the hole are treated as distinct constituents, each with its own spin--orbit coupling. We find that the topology of the skyrmion is transferred to the exciton only when the exchange coupling exceeds the relevant spin--orbit splitting. The exciton then experiences a full flux quantum, with opposite signs in the two valleys. The quantum metric of the spin texture creates a ring-shaped barrier that traps excitons, whereas the topological charge fixes the angular momentum of the trapped states and hence their optical selection rules. Because the trap moves with the skyrmion, excitons can be transported by current pulses, and small skyrmions accommodate a single exciton, which opens the door for potential hybrid applications in future information technology intertwining topological magnetic bits with optical excitations.

</details>


### [92] [Microscopic theory of exciton polarons in transition metal dichalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09819)
*Carlos Maciel-Escudero,Giuseppe Meneghini,Jamie M. Fitzgerald,Ermin Malic*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立描述过渡金属硫族化合物（transition metal dichalcogenides, TMDs）中激子极化子（exciton polaron）的微观模型，揭示MoSe₂单层中1s声学极化子色散存在明显折点及谱函数展宽，标志从良好定义的束缚激子极化子向激子-声子散射主导区的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极化子（polaron）中载流子被声子云包裹，会根本性重整化有效质量与迁移率；然而二维半导体中电中性激子极化子的相关研究有限，针对其谱特征与重整化性质的理论工作仍很少。

Method: 构建描述TMDs中激子极化子的微观模型，并将其应用于MoSe₂单层中的1s声学极化子；进一步引入谷间（intervalley）激子-声子相互作用进行分析。

Result: 极化子色散出现明显折点，伴随谱函数显著展宽；这些特征标志从具有重整化能量、降低群速度和增强有效质量的束缚激子极化子区，向激子-声子散射主导区的临界转变。引入谷间激子-声子相互作用后，动量暗激子（momentum-dark exciton）的能量位置强烈调制激子极化子寿命。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了确定激子极化子重整化性质的微观框架，并在二维半导体中确立了实验可及的极化子特征信号。

Abstract: Polarons are composite quasiparticles formed when charge carriers strongly couple to lattice vibrations, manifesting as carriers dressed by a surrounding cloud of phonons. Although this dressing fundamentally renormalizes the effective mass and mobility of carriers, its impact on electrically neutral exciton polarons in 2D semiconductors has received limited attention. In particular, despite the growing interest in polaronic effects in atomically thin semiconductors, only a few theoretical studies have addressed spectral signatures and renormalized properties of exciton polarons in these materials. Here, we develop a microscopic model to describe exciton polarons in transition metal dichalcogenides. Applying this model to $1s$ acoustic polarons in an MoSe$_2$ monolayer, we reveal a distinct kink in the polaron dispersion, accompanied by a pronounced broadening of its spectral function. These spectral signatures mark a critical transition from a regime of well-defined bound exciton polarons, characterized by a renormalized energy, reduced group velocity, and enhanced effective mass, to a regime dominated by exciton-phonon scattering. Furthermore, by incorporating intervalley exciton-phonon interaction, we demonstrate that the energetic position of momentum-dark excitons strongly modifies the exciton-polaron lifetime. Overall, our work provides a microscopic framework for determining renormalized properties of exciton polarons and establishes experimentally accessible polaron signatures in 2D semiconductors.

</details>


### [93] [Trajectory-Dependent Decoherence and Leakage During Shuttling of Multi-Spin Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09873)
*Yu-Ning Zhang,Aleksandr S. Mokeev,Maximilian Rimbach-Russ,David P. DiVincenzo,Viatcheslav V. Dobrovitski*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作分析了在多自旋编码逻辑量子比特（logical qubit）被时空关联的1/f噪声退相干时，不同穿梭（shuttling）协议下的保真度与泄漏，并给出可由成对退相干因子预测性能的显式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模半导体量子处理器日益依赖量子比特穿梭的容错架构，而多自旋逻辑量子比特在穿梭过程中的退相干行为尚不清楚，亟需理解其保真度与泄漏。

Method: 考虑二、三、四自旋编码，在时空关联的1/f噪声退相干条件下分析逻辑量子比特的保真度（fidelity）与泄漏（leakage），比较不同穿梭协议，并用自旋对的退相干因子解释其行为。

Result: 所有编码下，短距离时并行穿梭（parallel shuttling）抗退相干效果最佳，而长程传输时顺序穿梭（sequential shuttling）更优（具体边界取决于器件参数）；并给出保真度与泄漏的显式表达式。

Conclusion: 研究建立了穿梭轨迹、噪声关联与量子比特退相干之间的联系，实验上仅需测量若干成对退相干因子即可预测多自旋量子比特性能，为可扩展半导体量子处理器的抗噪声穿梭提供指导。

Abstract: Logical qubits encoded in collective states of multiple spins are promising building blocks for large-scale semiconductor quantum processors. As novel fault-tolerant architectures increasingly rely on qubit shuttling, understanding decoherence of the shuttled multi-spin qubits becomes crucial. We analyze fidelity and leakage of the logical qubits dephased by the spatio-temporally correlated $1/f$ noise, considering two-, three-, and four-spin encodings. We study different shuttling protocols and establish that for all encodings generally, parallel shuttling provides the best protection against decoherence at short distances, whereas sequential shuttling outperforms others for long-range transport (although the exact boundaries depend on the specific device parameters). We explain this common behavior in terms of the dephasing factors of pairs of the shuttled spins; we provide explicit expressions for fidelities and leakage, thus enabling experimentalists to predict performance of the multi-spin qubits from only several independently measured pairwise dephasing factors. Our results establish a connection between shuttling trajectories, noise correlations, and qubit decoherence, and provide guidance for achieving noise-resilient shuttling in scalable semiconductor quantum processors.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [Transformer-Based Characterization of Criticality in Directed Percolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08527)
*Bozheng Dou,Wei Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究采用监督式 Transformer 编码器（Transformer encoder）分析 (1+1) 维与 (2+1) 维键定向渗流（bond directed percolation, bond DP），实现相分类并提取临界点与临界指数；通过注意力展开（attention rollout）定义的"注意力中心"揭示临界区附近表征的系统性重组，并考察了不同初始态数据间的迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定向渗流（directed percolation, DP）是研究进入吸收态的非平衡相变（nonequilibrium phase transitions）的典范框架，但从高维时空构型中直接提取临界行为仍具挑战性，需要有效的机器学习手段从构型数据中识别临界标度信息。

Method: 使用监督式 Transformer 编码器处理 (1+1) 维和 (2+1) 维键定向渗流的时空构型，基于分类概率估计临界点与临界指数；采用注意力展开并定义注意力中心（attention center）以量化累积注意力分布的时间质心，并开展初始态（全占据态与单种子态）之间的迁移学习与微调实验。

Result: Transformer 能高精度区分活跃相与吸收相，估计的临界点与临界指数与既有文献参考值高度吻合；注意力模式与注意力中心在临界区附近系统性重组并呈现明显的有限尺寸（finite-size）依赖；两类初始态数据间存在可迁移信息，但迁移性能呈方向性不对称，且等量目标域数据下微调相较从头训练仅带来边际精度提升。

Conclusion: 监督式 Transformer 不仅能完成定向渗流的相分类，其输出还蕴含临界标度相关信息，注意力分析为理解模型的内部表征提供了可解释线索；初始态相关数据间的知识迁移存在方向性差异，微调的收益有限。

Abstract: Directed percolation (DP) provides a canonical framework for studying nonequilibrium phase transitions into absorbing states, yet extracting critical behavior directly from high-dimensional spatiotemporal configurations remains challenging. Here, we investigate (1 + 1)- and (2 + 1)-dimensional bond DP using a supervised Transformer encoder. Supervised learning results demonstrate that the Transformer can distinguish between the active and absorbing phases of directed percolation with high accuracy. The critical point and critical exponents estimated from the model's classification probabilities align closely with reference values from existing literature, indicating that the model not only achieves phase classification but also yields outputs containing information related to critical scaling behavior. To probe the model's internal representation, we employ attention rollout and define an attention center that quantifies the temporal centroid of the cumulative attention distribution. The attention patterns and the attention center reorganize systematically near the critical region and exhibit pronounced finite-size dependence. Finally, we examined the model's transferability between configurations generated from fully occupied initial states and those from single-seed initial states. The results indicate the presence of transferable information between the two types of data, though the transfer performance exhibits directional asymmetry. Furthermore, when using an equal amount of target-domain data, fine-tuning yielded only a marginal improvement in accuracy compared to training from scratch.

</details>


### [95] [Nonequilibrium Fluctuations and Intrinsic Stability of Nanostructured Solar Cells: Microscopic Derivation and Universal Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08529)
*Rivo Herivola Manjakamanana Ravelonjato,Ravo Tokiniaina Ranaivoson,Philipe Manjakasoa Randriantsoa,Raoelina Andriambololona,Wilfrid Chrysante Solofoarisina*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文从两态主方程推导纳米结构太阳能电池效率的微观随机模型，将效率涨落与熵产生联系起来，并首次将普适涨落定律应用于实际电池参数；模拟表明器件处于强非平衡区，负熵（因而效率）涨落呈指数稀有，从而解释了效率的统计稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究已给出纳米结构太阳能电池的平均效率，但对其效率涨落（efficiency fluctuations）的统计性质与热力学约束缺乏微观层面的刻画，需要建立效率涨落与熵产生（entropy production）之间的显式联系。

Method: 由描述吸收、辐射复合（radiative recombination）与提取过程的两态主方程（two-state master equation）推导效率的微观随机模型，并借助局部细致平衡（local detailed balance）论证热力学亲和的合理性；随后将近期文献中的普适涨落定律（universal fluctuation laws）应用于纳米结构太阳能电池的实际参数，对含偏置的离散随机游走（biased discrete random walk）在近平衡区数值验证精确下确界定律（infimum law），再用于真实构型。

Result: 总熵产生表现为下鞅（submartingale），其负指数为鞅（martingale）；数值上验证了近平衡区的精确下确界定律并应用于实际电池构型；模拟显示器件工作于强非平衡区，负熵涨落（即效率涨落）呈指数稀有，多数构型在全部模拟轨迹中未出现可探测的负涨落，与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 结果解释了该器件的本征统计稳定性，为理解均值附近的效率涨落提供了概念与定量框架；不改变此前得到的平均效率，但明确了其在强非平衡区中的统计稳健性（statistical robustness）。

Abstract: We study nonequilibrium fluctuations of efficiency in nanostructured solar cells. Our contribution is threefold. First, we derive a microscopic stochastic model of efficiency from a two-state master equation describing absorption, radiative recombination, and extraction. This derivation establishes an explicit link between efficiency fluctuations and entropy production, and justifies the thermodynamic affinity through local detailed balance. Second, we review universal fluctuation laws established in the recent literature and apply them for the first time to the real parameters of nanostructured solar cells. We show that the total entropy production behaves as a submartingale, and that its negative exponential is a martingale. For a biased discrete random walk, the exact infimum law is verified numerically in the near-equilibrium regime, then applied to realistic solar cell configurations. Third, our simulations show that nanostructured solar cells operate in a strongly nonequilibrium regime, where negative entropy fluctuations, and hence efficiency fluctuations, are exponentially rare. Over all simulated trajectories, most configurations show no detectable negative fluctuation, consistent with the theoretical prediction. These results explain the intrinsic statistical stability of the devices and provide a conceptual and quantitative framework for understanding efficiency fluctuations around the mean values. They do not modify the mean efficiencies obtained previously, but clarify their statistical robustness in the strongly nonequilibrium regime.

</details>


### [96] [Permutation order governs basin entropy in critical one-input Boolean networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08532)
*Ruodan Liu,Ahana Ghosh,Sean Campbell,Venkata Sai Narayana Bavisetty,Matthew Wheeler,Claus Kadelka*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文刻画了单输入同步布尔网络中反馈环（feedback loops）的置换结构如何决定盆地熵（basin entropy），并证明该决定性在整体误差层面成立，同时给出加性预测的适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盆地熵衡量网络长期行为的不确定性，但反馈环之间的独立振荡是否能简单叠加仍不清楚，需要厘清环结构与熵之间的关系。

Method: 对每个含 m 个相关节点（relevant nodes）的临界单输入同步布尔网络，分析其反馈环诱导的置换结构（permutation structure），并考察相位置换（phase offset）导致的非加性偏差与集合层面的平均行为。

Result: 尽管反馈环独立振荡，其相对相位可被偏移，使非加性偏差可在 m 上线性增长；但在网络集合（ensemble）层面，此前的加性预测（additive prediction）在次领头项仍成立，偏差出现在 ln m·ln ln m 阶。

Conclusion: 反馈环的置换结构决定盆地熵（至整体常数误差）；加性预测的失效被限制在极小的对数高阶修正内，为集合层面使用加性近似提供了依据。

Abstract: Basin entropy measures uncertainty about a network's long-term behavior. For every critical one-input synchronous Boolean network with $m$ relevant nodes, we show that the permutation structure induced by its feedback loops determines basin entropy, up to a universal error. Although the loops oscillate independently, their relative phases can be offset, producing a deviation from additivity that can grow linearly in $m$. Yet at the ensemble level, a previously derived additive prediction is preserved through the first subleading term, with the discrepancy appearing at order $\ln m\ln\ln m$.

</details>


### [97] [Interacting particles crossing barriers: spatial statistics, choice of route taken and role of interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08617)
*Gulzar Ahmad,Andrew J Archer,Sergey Savel'ev,Marco G Mazza*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在含多条路径的势能景观中，研究两相互作用粒子（吸引或排斥）的到达统计：首达粒子到达时间可显著长于或短于单粒子情形，取决于相互作用性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将单粒子在势能景观中的首达问题推广到两相互作用粒子体系，考察粒子间相互作用如何改变到达统计与空间分布。

Method: 考虑从局部极小出发到全局极小目的地存在多路径选择的势能景观，粒子间相互作用以软高斯势（soft Gaussian potential）或Lennard-Jones势描述，分别对应吸引与排斥；统计首达粒子身份、到达时间分布及首达时第二粒子所在位置，并用Jensen–Shannon散度（Jensen–Shannon divergence）分析粒子位置的概率分布。

Result: 首达粒子的典型到达时间可与单粒子情形显著不同：软高斯吸引相互作用使两粒子结合为单个更大粒子，跨越势垒更慢，到达时间可能大得多；排斥性硬核相互作用则因排除面积（excluded-area）与粒子排序（particle-ordering）效应使到达时间缩短。Jensen–Shannon散度揭示了受限势内粒子空间分布对相互作用势参数的依赖。

Conclusion: 粒子间相互作用（吸引或排斥）对首达统计与空间分布具有定性影响，到达时间可呈相反变化方向；Jensen–Shannon散度可作为刻画该依赖关系的有效工具。

Abstract: We consider the statistics of pairs of particles moving in a potential energy landscape exhibiting a choice of different pathways from a local minimum start point to the global energy minimum destination. We examine the impact of both attractive and repulsive interactions between the particles, treating these via either a soft Gaussian or a Lennard-Jones potential. We investigate the statistics of which particle arrives at the destination first, arrival-time distributions and where in the system the second particle is located when the first arrives. We find that the typical arrival time of the first particle can be significantly different to that of a single particle, sometimes being much longer and in other cases being shorter, depending on the nature of the particle interactions. In the case of soft Gaussian attractive interactions, which effectively bind the pair of particles together to form a single larger particle, the arrival time can be much longer, since bigger particles cross barriers more slowly. In contrast, the arrival time is shorter when there is a repulsive core interaction between the particles, due to excluded-area and particle-ordering effects. We investigate the probability distribution of particles' positions through the Jensen--Shannon divergence which unveils the dependence of the spatial distribution of the particles within the confining potential on the parameters of the particle interaction potentials.

</details>


### [98] [$\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge and exact solution of the $\pm J$ Edwards-Anderson model in a transverse field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09097)
*Mikhail Vasin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 利用马约拉纳表示证明横场±J Edwards–Anderson模型可通过精确规范变换消除符号无序，其谱与均匀耦合模型一致，并给出Δ=J√Z处的二阶相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在研究横场下符号无序（±J）的Edwards–Anderson自旋玻璃模型在T=0时的谱与相结构，借助马约拉纳表示寻求对交换积分符号无序的精确处理途径。

Method: 采用自旋-1/2算符的马约拉纳表示（Majorana representation），证明相邻自旋耦合量与该表示下的哈密顿量对易且不受高斯定律约束；由此得到的Z2规范自由度可将符号无序通过精确规范变换（exact gauge transformation）消除，使理论化为马约拉纳场的二次型，并计算局域格林函数（local Green's functions）。

Result: Edwards–Anderson序参量在Δ>J√Z时消失，在Δ<J√Z时等于1/4，表明在Δ=J√Z处发生从顺磁相到自旋玻璃基态的二阶相变（second-order transition）。

Conclusion: 该自旋玻璃基态在自旋构型上简并，且在T=0时满足复本对称（replica symmetric）；Z2规范变换使无序模型的谱与均匀耦合模型完全相同，为符号无序自旋玻璃提供了可解框架。

Abstract: Using the Majorana representation of spin-1/2 operators, we study the $\pm J$ Edwards--Anderson model in a transverse field at $T=0$. We show that, in this representation, the quantity describing the coupling between neighboring spins commutes with the Hamiltonian and, unlike in the Kitaev model, is not subject to any Gauss law. The resulting $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge freedom allows the disorder in the signs of the exchange integrals to be eliminated by an exact gauge transformation, so that the spectrum of the system coincides with that of the model with uniform couplings. The resulting theory is quadratic in the Majorana fields. Computing the local Green's functions, we find that the Edwards--Anderson order parameter vanishes for $Δ>J\sqrt{Z}$ and equals $1/4$ for $Δ<J\sqrt{Z}$, which signals a second-order transition from a paramagnetic phase to a spin-glass ground state that is degenerate over spin configurations and replica symmetric at $T=0$.

</details>


### [99] [Thermalization of coarse-grained variables in the Toda lattice: a numerical study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09287)
*Martina Giachello,Cesare Vianello,Giacomo Gradenigo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 对Toda晶格哈密顿动力学（Toda lattice Hamiltonian dynamics）的数值研究表明，尽管模型可积，多类粗粒化变量（coarse-grained variables）的能量时间相关函数（energy time correlation）仍呈指数或拉伸指数衰减，表现出良好的热化（thermalization）性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou型数值实验将可积性的破缺（breaking of integrability）视为弛豫到热平衡的关键要素；本文意在检验可积性（integrability）是否真的是热化的障碍。

Method: 采用数值方法研究Toda晶格的哈密顿动力学，构造若干在较大系统尺寸下仍具热力学意义（thermodynamic relevance）的粗粒化变量，并计算其能量时间相关函数。

Result: 所考察的粗粒化变量的能量时间相关函数呈指数（exponential）或拉伸指数（stretched exponential）衰减，表明其具备良好的热化性质。

Conclusion: 只要接受一种足够弱的平衡定义，可积性并不构成热化的障碍；该结论与“可积性破缺是弛豫必要前提”的通常观点互为补充。

Abstract: We present here a numerical study of the Toda lattice Hamiltonian dynamics showing how, despite model integrability, it is possible to find many macroscopic observables which display standard equilibrium behavior. In particular, we study numerically the energy time correlation of certain coarse-grained variables, showing that it decays with either exponential or stretched exponential behaviour, thus indicating good thermalization properties. The spirit of our observation is complementary to that of standard Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou-like numerical experiments, in which the breaking of integrability is regarded as the key ingredient for relaxation to thermal equilibrium. The present communication corroborates, on the contrary, the view that integrability is not an obstacle to thermalization, provided that a sufficiently weak definition of the latter is accepted. In the Toda lattice we have introduced several kinds of coarse-grained variables, constructed with the purpose to maintain a thermodynamic relevance also for large system sizes, which all show good thermalization properties.

</details>

### [100] [Resolving non-neutrality across scales in a hyperdiverse tropical forest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09479)
*Pietro Zgaga,Pablo Villegas,Tancredi Caruso,Tommaso Gili*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该论文借鉴统计力学（statistical mechanics）思路，结合随机矩阵滤波（random-matrix filtering）与空间显式中性模型（spatially explicit neutral model），追踪空间粗粒化（spatial coarse-graining）下的种间空间相关，并在巴罗科罗拉多岛（Barro Colorado Island）热带森林数据中识别出由相互作用到有效中性组织的连续渡越尺度（约20–35 m）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同生态过程作用于不同时空尺度，宏观规律可在忽略微观细节时被刻画，但需明确这种有效描述在何种尺度上成立。研究旨在提供一个可操作框架，用以定位中性描述（neutral description）有效性的特征尺度。

Method: 构建受统计力学启发的框架，将随机矩阵滤波与空间显式中性模型相结合，在不同空间粗粒化程度下比较经验与中性的种间相关谱（correlation spectrum），并应用于巴罗科罗拉多岛热带森林的八次群落普查数据。

Result: 在数十米以下（20–35 m）经验相关谱偏离中性预期，显示尺度依赖的物种特异性结构，其中部分可归因于环境异质性（environmental heterogeneity），残余空间关联则可能是生物相互作用或其他未测量的非中性过程之候选信号。随粗粒化增强，经验与中性相关谱逐步收敛，在渡越尺度以上中性模型能准确描述测得的集体组织。

Conclusion: 中性（neutrality）可作为特征尺度以上森林集体组织的有效描述，但这并不意味着个体层面物种等价。该框架提供了定位该特征尺度的操作化途径。

Abstract: Different ecological processes operate at different spatial and temporal scales. Macroscopic regularities can therefore be captured by descriptions that ignore microscopic details, even when those details are mechanistically relevant to ecological processes. Here, we develop a framework inspired by statistical mechanics to track interspecific spatial correlations under spatial coarse-graining. To do so we combine random-matrix filtering with a spatially explicit neutral model. Applying this approach to eight censuses of the Barro Colorado Island tropical forest, we observe a continuous crossover from an interacting to an effectively neutral organization. Below a few tens of metres (20--35~m), the empirical correlation spectrum differs from neutral expectations, revealing scale-dependent species-specific structure. Part of this structure can be attributed to environmental heterogeneity. However, residual spatial associations provide candidate signatures of biotic interactions or other unmeasured ecological, non-neutral processes. With increasing spatial coarse-graining, empirical and neutral correlation spectra progressively converge, and above the crossover scale the neutral model provides an accurate description of the measured collective organization. Neutrality thus emerges as an effective description of the collective organization of the forest above a characteristic scale, without implying that species are equivalent at the individual level. Our framework provides an operational way to locate that scale.

</details>


<div id='nlin.CD'></div>

### [101] [Synchronization transitions in Kuramoto oscillators with mixed interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09564)
*Abhishek Sharma,Ayushi Suman,Mehernaz Anvari,Manish Dev Shrimali,Charo I. del Genio,Sarika Jalan*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 本研究考察具有混合阶相互作用（mixed-order interactions）的网络化 Kuramoto 振子系综中的有限尺寸效应（finite-size effects），通过数值模拟揭示了系统规模、节点平均度（average degree）与相互作用强度如何改变同步转变行为，并与全局耦合无限系统的既有理论结果进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kuramoto 模型（Kuramoto model）是研究涌现同步（emergent synchronization）现象的范式系统，但以往研究几乎都假设振子间为全局耦合（global coupling），而现实系统多具有特定网络结构，该缺口亟待填补。

Method: 采用数值模拟（numerical simulations）方法，研究具有混合阶相互作用的网络化 Kuramoto 振子系综，系统考察有限尺寸条件对同步转变的影响。

Result: 结果表明，系统规模（size of the system）、节点平均度（average degree of the nodes）与相互作用强度（strength of the interactions）等参数会改变向同步转变的行为，且相对全局耦合无限系统（globally coupled infinite systems）的理论预言存在偏离。

Conclusion: 网络结构与有限尺寸效应显著影响 Kuramoto 系综的同步转变，基于全局耦合无限系统假设的理论结果在网络化有限系统中需加以修正。

Abstract: The Kuramoto model is a paradigmatic system for emergent synchronization phenomena, and significant efforts have been made to investigate its transition from incoherent to coherent dynamics. However, previous studies have almost exclusively assumed global coupling between the oscillators, even though many real-world systems are organized in specific network structures. Here, we fill this gap by examining finite-size effects in Kuramoto oscillator ensembles on networks with mixed-order interactions. Using numerical simulations, we show how parameters such as the size of the system, the average degree of the nodes and the strength of the interactions alter the behaviour of the transition to synchronization with respect to the known theoretical results that are derived for globally coupled infinite systems.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Experimental realization of twofold topological phase transition induced by long-range hoppings in one-dimensional chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08605)
*J. A. Franco-Medrano,B. Manjarrez-Montañez,E. Flores-Olmedo,G. Báez,A. M. Martínez-Argüello,Y. Betancur-Ocampo,R. A. Méndez-Sánchez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在一维声子链（phononic chain）中通过次近邻之外的第三近邻耦合（third-nearest-neighbor couplings）实验实现双重拓扑相变（twofold topological phase transition），观测到狄拉克点（Dirac points）、谷赝自旋结构（valley pseudospin structure）与拓扑边缘态（topological edge states）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统紧束缚模型（tight-binding models）难以超越Su-Schrieffer-Heeger范式，长程跃迁（long-range hoppings）被视为工程化奇异拓扑相的有效机制，本文旨在实验验证第三近邻耦合驱动的一维长程相图。

Method: 构建模拟顺式聚乙炔（cis-polyacetylene）的一维声子链，利用高度结构化铝板实现可调跃迁参数（tunable hopping parameters），并以非平衡格林函数（non-equilibrium Green's function）输运计算对实验进行佐证。

Result: 实验观测到狄拉克点的出现、谷赝自旋结构以及拓扑边缘态，结果与理论预测的长程相图（long-range phase diagram）一致，并获得非平衡格林函数输运计算支持。

Conclusion: 声子超材料（phononic metamaterials）可作为探索超越Su-Schrieffer-Heeger范式拓扑现象的多功能平台，长程跃迁驱动的多重拓扑相变得到实验确认。

Abstract: Long-range hoppings offer a powerful mechanism to engineer exotic topological phases beyond conventional tight-binding models. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a twofold topological phase transition driven by third-nearest-neighbor couplings in a one-dimensional phononic chain that emulates cis-polyacetylene. Tunable hopping parameters in highly structured aluminum plates allow us to observe the emergence of Dirac points, a valley pseudospin structure, and topological edge states. The experimental results are supported by non-equilibrium Green's function transport calculations. Our findings confirm the predicted long-range phase diagram and establish phononic metamaterials as a versatile platform for exploring topological phenomena beyond the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger paradigm.

</details>


### [103] [Jackknife-conformal uncertainty for linear ACE potentials: calibrated forces and silicon properties compared with Bayesian and POPS baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08678)
*James R. Kermode*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文指出机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials）存在模型误设（misspecification），并以线性原子团簇展开（ACE）势为例，用留一构型刀切法（delete-one-configuration jackknife）得到对误设稳健的参数协方差，结合保形分位数（conformal quantile）给出力与多性质的可校准不确定度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯后验方差在模型误设下会低估误差，因此需要一种对误设稳健的不确定度量化（uncertainty quantification）方法，并能迁移到力以外的多种材料性质。

Method: 以留一构型刀切法估计参数协方差，用训练集留出部分的单一保形分位数（conformal quantile）进行缩放；通过隐式微分（implicit differentiation）穿过每个弛豫过程，并借助委员会（committees）将同一参数分布传播至硅的21种性质，与密度泛函理论（DFT）结果对照；另以训练标签构造偏差项修正刀切法在热快照缺陷上的失效，并与点态最优参数集（POPS）及单尺度贝叶斯后验比较。

Result: 每原子力不确定度在留出硅数据与未参与拟合的裂纹上接近目标覆盖率（nominal coverage），未参与拟合的螺位错在较小模型下覆盖不足（under-covered）；区间覆盖了大多数性质，较大模型的区间约紧4倍。单尺度贝叶斯后验形状错误，覆盖性质更少且联合证据（joint evidence）远低；POPS在性质上表现相当，但其力区间未校准（名义0.90下为0.95与0.84）。

Conclusion: 误设稳健的刀切法加保形校准可提供接近名义覆盖率的不确定度，且同一参数分布可无调参地传播到多类性质；其对弛豫点缺陷（relaxed point defects）的失效源于所有构型共享的偏差，可由训练标签测得的偏差项修正至接近名义覆盖。

Abstract: Machine-learned interatomic potentials are misspecified, so Bayesian posterior variances understate their errors. For linear atomic cluster expansion (ACE) potentials we derive calibrated uncertainties from one misspecification-robust parameter covariance, the delete-one-configuration jackknife. Scaled by one conformal quantile from a held-out part of the training set, it gives per-atom force uncertainties close to their target coverage on held-out silicon data and on cracks left out of the fit; screw dislocations left out are under-covered by the smaller of the two models. With one scale fitted to the forces of a held-out test split, the same parameter distribution is propagated to 21 properties of silicon, from elastic constants to defect and surface energies, by implicit differentiation through each relaxation and by committees, and is tested against density functional theory (DFT) without tuning on any property. For a smaller and a larger model the intervals cover most properties, and the larger model's are about 4 times tighter. The Bayesian posterior with one calibrated scale has the wrong shape: it covers fewer properties and has far lower joint evidence. Pointwise optimal parameter sets (POPS), once their hypercube is numerically defined, performs comparably on the properties, but its per-atom force regions are not calibrated to a target (0.95 and 0.84 at a nominal 0.90). Where the jackknife fails is relaxed point defects, whose training cells are all hot snapshots: a bias that every configuration shares is invisible to a jackknife, but it can be measured from the training labels, and as a discrepancy term, devised after the property results were seen, it brings the coverage close to nominal.

</details>


### [104] [Ion Redistribution and Lattice-Distortion-Modulated d-Electron Correlation in Layered Multiferroic ABP$_2$S$_6$ Probed by X-ray Photoelectron Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08728)
*Jing Tang,Ruiqi Lu,J. Anibal Boscoboinik,Xi Ling*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作建立温度依赖 X 射线光电子能谱（X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy, XPS）作为铁电相变的定量化学探针，通过在 vdW 层状 ABP2S6 体系中对比 CuCrP2S6（有铁电转变）与 AgCrP2S6（顺电），揭示巨大 XPS 反常源于 Cu+ 离子迁移，并可定量估计离子位移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电相变由离子位移诱导的对称性破缺驱动，但其化学特征很少在原子尺度被直接探测；需发展能灵敏反映离子迁移与局域化学环境变化的定量谱学手段。

Method: 以范德华（van der Waals, vdW）层状 ABP2S6 为模型体系，采用温度依赖 XPS 追踪芯能级（core-level）谱随温度的变化，对比经历铁电相变的 CuCrP2S6（CCPS）与在该温区保持顺电的 AgCrP2S6（ACPS），并结合铁电结构模型分析 Cu 2p 与 Cr 芯能级。

Result: ACPS 仅表现出平滑的常规热展宽与谱峰位移；CCPS 在铁电转变附近出现显著且极大的温度依赖 XPS 反常。Cu 2p 分析表明反常源于与极化翻转（polarization switching）相关的温度驱动 Cu+ 离子迁移，Cr 芯能级变化则反映次级晶格挤压与 d 电子关联调制。

Conclusion: XPS 对铁电序具有内禀敏感性；结合铁电结构模型可将 XPS 结合能位移与 Cu 离子迁移幅度关联，从而由谱学观测量定量估计离子位移，为铁电材料及离子位移驱动相变提供通用化学探针。

Abstract: Ferroelectric phase transitions are driven by ion displacement-induced symmetry breaking, yet their chemical signatures are rarely probed directly at the atomic level. Here, we establish temperature-dependent X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy (XPS) as a powerful and quantitative probe of ferroelectric phase transitions by tracking ion migration-induced changes in local chemical environments. Using van der Waals (vdW) layered ABP$_2$S$_6$ materials as a model system, we systematically compare CuCrP$_2$S$_6$ (CCPS), which undergoes a ferroelectric phase transition, with AgCrP$_2$S$_6$ (ACPS), which remains paraelectric over the same temperature range. While ACPS exhibits only smooth trivial thermal broadening and shifts in core-level spectra, CCPS shows pronounced and enormously large temperature-dependent XPS anomalies across the ferroelectric transition, demonstrating that XPS is intrinsically sensitive to ferroelectric order. Detailed analysis of the Cu 2p spectra reveals that these anomalies originate from temperature-driven Cu$^+$ ion migration associated with polarization switching, whereas concurrent changes in the Cr core levels reflect secondary lattice squeezing and modulation of d-electron correlation induced by Cu displacement. Supported by a ferroelectric structural model, we further correlate XPS binding-energy shifts with the magnitude of Cu-ion migration, enabling a quantitative estimation of ionic displacement directly from spectroscopic observables. This work also highlights the rich information provided by temperature-dependent XPS for detecting and quantifying ferroelectric phase transitions and ion migration, offering a general chemical probe for ferroic materials and ion-displacement-driven phase transitions.

</details>


### [105] [Bichromatic light-induced electronic torques on the Néel vector in Rashba magnetic multipoles from $p$-wave magnets to $i$-wave altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08761)
*Mohsen Yarmohammadi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 双色光$(ω,bω)$可在Rashba耦合磁多极体中产生垂直于面外序参量$\boldsymbol N$的电子力矩，其选择定则由$\gcd(n,b\pm1)=1$决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对具有面外序参量的二维磁体，逆法拉第场（inverse Faraday field）与$\boldsymbol N$共线，无法提供横向力矩，需要寻找新的光驱动机理。

Method: 采用精确Floquet响应（exact Floquet response）结合广义单位占据电子力矩评估，涵盖$n=1$至$n=6$的Rashba耦合磁多极体；以Rashba铁磁体（$n=0$）为对照。

Result: 动量依赖交换项主导费米海力矩并消除动量无关场的盲点；面外$\boldsymbol N$下横向力矩存在条件为反向旋转光束满足$\gcd(n,b+1)=1$或同向旋转满足$\gcd(n,b-1)=1$；面内$\boldsymbol N$下费米海还产生沿$\hat z$的额外力矩分量。

Conclusion: 有限动量占据态物理（finite-momentum occupied-state physics）是光驱动力矩的关键，精确Floquet计算验证并拓展了高频描述，且按频率标度与起源对$n=1,\dots,6$、$b=1,\dots,7$通道进行了分类。
