Abstract: Light can control magnetic order on ultrafast timescales, but for a two-dimensional magnet with an out-of-plane order parameter $\boldsymbol N\parallel\hat z$, the inverse Faraday field is collinear with $\boldsymbol N$ and gives no transverse torque. Here we show that bichromatic $(ω,bω)$ light generates electronic torques perpendicular to $\boldsymbol N$ in Rashba-coupled magnetic multipoles, from $p$-wave ($n=1$) magnets to $i$-wave ($n=6$) altermagnets. Beyond high-frequency effective fields, we evaluate the exact Floquet response and the generalized torque per occupied electron, for prescribed occupations. The momentum-dependent exchange terms dominate this Fermi-sea torque and remove the leading-order zeros (amplitude-ratio blindspots) of the momentum-independent field in the channels tested. For out-of-plane $\boldsymbol N$, rotational symmetry permits a transverse torque only if $\gcd(n,b+1)=1$ for counter-rotating beams or $\gcd(n,b-1)=1$ for co-rotating beams. For in-plane $\boldsymbol N$, the Fermi sea also gives a torque component along $\hat z$ that the momentum-independent field cannot produce. We classify the active channels, with $n=1,\dots,6$ and $b=1,\dots,7$, by frequency scaling and origin (cycle-averaged versus oscillating exchange). As a control, a Rashba ferromagnet ($n=0$) has one symmetry-allowed out-of-plane channel and a weak in-plane response without a selection rule. Finite-momentum occupied-state physics is therefore essential for light-driven torques in unconventional magnets, and the exact Floquet calculation validates and extends the high-frequency description.

</details>


### [106] [Conformationally frustrated epitaxy at heteromolecular interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08851)
*Jakub Planer,Pavel Procházka,Tomáš Krajňák,Veronika Stará,Daniela Hrubá,Zdeněk Endstrasser,Tomáš Skála,Jan Čechal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究以BTB分子在Ag(111)上完全去质子化的分子δ层为模板，考察完整BTB分子的异质外延（heteroepitaxy），结合STM、LEEM/LEED、XPS与DFT，揭示分子柔性与构象能量在决定外延匹配中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子异质外延（molecular heteroepitaxy）可用于构筑界面明确的多层结构，但分子内柔性（intramolecular flexibility）在外延匹配中的作用尚不清楚。

Method: 在Ag(111)上以完全去质子化BTB构成的分子δ层为模板，沉积完整BTB分子，采用扫描隧道显微术（scanning tunneling microscopy）、低能电子显微术与衍射（low-energy electron microscopy and diffraction）、X射线光电子能谱（X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy）及密度泛函理论（density functional theory）联合表征与计算。

Result: 分子模板上分子间键合模式（bonding motifs）得以保持，但晶格参数与分子构象相对银表面发生改变；单个分子在STM中呈现不同形貌，与理论给出的位点依赖构象变化一致。计算表明分子-基底相互作用与构象能的竞争产生不同折中几何，导致外延界面处的构象受阻（conformational frustration）；将BTB约束为平面构型会改变其优选取向吸附位置。

Conclusion: 分子柔性通过以能量有利的构象补偿不利的分子-基底相互作用，同时保持分子间网络，从而参与外延匹配；与更刚性分子覆盖层的比较进一步支持该解释。

Abstract: Precise control of molecular heteroepitaxy offers a route to multilayer architectures with well-defined interfaces. However, the role of intramolecular flexibility in epitaxial matching remains insufficiently understood. Here, we investigate the heteroepitaxy of intact 1,3,5-tris(4-carboxyphenyl)benzene (BTB) on a molecular $δ$-layer composed of fully deprotonated BTB molecules on Ag(111). Combining scanning tunneling microscopy, low-energy electron microscopy and diffraction, X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy, and density functional theory, we identify the key interactions controlling molecular packing and epitaxial growth. We show that intermolecular bonding motifs are preserved on the molecular template, but their lattice parameters and molecular conformations change as compared to a silver surface. Individual molecules exhibit distinct appearances in scanning tunneling microscopy, consistent with site-dependent conformational variations identified by theory. Our calculations reveal that the competition between molecule-substrate interactions and conformational energy produces different compromise geometries, resulting in conformational frustration at the epitaxial interface. Crucially, constraining BTB into a planar geometry changes the preferred lateral adsorption positions, demonstrating that molecular flexibility directly modifies the energetic preferences for epitaxial registry. Comparison with more rigid molecular overlayers further supports this interpretation. Our findings reveal that molecular flexibility contributes to epitaxial matching by allowing unfavorable molecule-substrate interactions to be compensated by energetically favorable conformations while preserving the intermolecular network.

</details>


### [107] [Magnetic-Field-Tuned Critical End Point and High-Field Phases in HoMn6Sn6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08871)
*Joanna Blawat,John Singleton,Junho Seo,Charles Mielke,Tyler J. Slade,Paul C. Canfield,Philip J. W. Moll,Ross D. McDonald,Robert J. McQueeney*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过高达60 T的脉冲磁场磁化测量与应变释放器件输运实验，系统研究了单晶HoMn6Sn6的磁场-温度相图，发现45 T处Δ出现延伸至约90 K临界终点的变磁转变，并观察到60 T下900%的巨磁阻、Shubnikov-de Haas振荡及与非磁化强度成比例的异常霍尔效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑Kagome材料RMn6Sn6具有丰富的磁相，是理解不同自旋织构如何通过外磁场调控拓扑与输运性质的理想平台，但高场相图特征及临界现象此前缺乏细致研究。

Method: 结合最高60 T的脉冲强磁场（pulsed magnetic fields）磁化测量与聚焦离子束（focused-ion-beam）制备的应变释放HoMn6Sn6器件输运测量，并与平均场计算（mean-field calculations）对照。

Result: 在H||c、45 T处发现倾斜相之间的变磁转变（metamagnetic transition），终止于约90 K的临界终点；低温变磁区磁阻达900%（60 T），伴随Shubnikov-de Haas振荡及费米面轻微收缩迹象；临界终点附近磁阻偏离费米液体行为，异常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect）在约40 K处峰值且不与磁化强度成正比。

Conclusion: HoMn6Sn6展现出RMn6Sn6家族中唯一且温度、场强远超同类现象的临界终点，表明临界点附近增强的自旋涨落（spin fluctuations）显著影响输运；高温低场下异常霍尔效应重现，说明Ho与Mn亚晶格磁化独立影响电导，振荡的相位分析提示非平庸能带拓扑，但需进一步能带计算确认。

Abstract: Topological Kagome RMn6Sn6 materials, where R is a rare-earth, exhibit a rich variety of magnetic phases which provide an opportunity to understand how different spin textures affect topological and transport properties through the application of magnetic field H. In this work, we report experiments on single-crystal HoMn6Sn6, made possible by pulsed magnetic fields of up to 60 T, that permit high-field features of the field-temperature phase diagram to be studied in detail using magnetometry. Complementary, sensitive transport experiments over the same field range employ focused-ion-beam fabrication of novel strain-relieved HoMn6Sn6 devices. In agreement with meanfield calculations, we find a metamagnetic transition at 45 T with H||c between canted phases that ends in a critical end point at about 90 K. As far as we are aware, this is the only critical endpoint that is observed within the RMn6Sn6 kagome metals, and its temperature and field far exceed those of analogous phenomena in other materials. In the low-temperature metamagnetic region, HoMn6Sn6 shows large magnetoresistance, reaching 900 % at 60 T, in concert with Shubnikov de Haas oscillations that suggest a slight Fermi-surface contraction as the metamagnetic transition is crossed. A phase analysis of the oscillations suggests non-trivial band topology, although a firmer attribution must await detailed bandstructure calculations. Near the critical end point, we find deviations from Fermi-liquid behavior in the temperature-dependent magnetoresistance. In the metamagnetic region, the anomalous Hall contribution is not proportional to magnetization, and peaks at about 40 K, suggesting the role of enhanced spin fluctuations near the critical point. At higher temperatures, the anomalous Hall effect reappears at low fields, showing that the magnetization provided by the Ho and Mn sublattices independently affect the conductivity.

</details>


### [108] [Rotational Doppler Effect and Helimagnon Frequency Comb in Sliding Magnetic Spirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08873)
*Yang Liu,Jie Liu,Tianci Gong,Zhaozhuo Zeng,Xiaoping Gao,Kai Chang,Peng Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作揭示磁螺旋（magnetic spiral）中螺旋子（helimagnon）与无隙戈德斯通模（Goldstone mode，即螺旋滑移）耦合后产生无阈值旋转多普勒频移（rotational Doppler shift）以及有限驱动窗口内的螺旋子频率梳（frequency comb）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体自旋运动可作为动态磁背景重塑磁振子谱（magnon spectra）；磁螺旋同时承载传播的螺旋子与无隙滑移模，为研究这一效应提供了干净且可控的模型体系。

Method: 在磁螺旋背景下考察螺旋子被激发并伴随螺旋滑移的情形：滑移使局域自旋参考系旋转，从而对螺旋子谱产生旋转多普勒频移；进一步在强激发下分析有限动量相位子（phason）与螺旋子的混合。

Result: 多普勒频移无阈值，线性模呈偶数缠绕（even winding），其二次谐波呈奇数缠绕；更强激发产生一个有限动量相位子，其与螺旋子混合仅在一定驱动窗口内形成频率梳。梳间距由螺旋长度决定的相位子频率给定，而非滑移速率。

Conclusion: 两个特征尺度在微波实验中可分离：多普勒偏移跟踪滑移速度，而频率梳间距与螺旋长度成反比，从而提供可实验检验的判据。

Abstract: Collective spin motion can reshape magnon spectra by providing a dynamical magnetic background. Magnetic spirals offer a particularly clean setting, as they simultaneously host propagating helimagnons and a gapless Goldstone mode, i.e., the sliding of the spiral. Here, we show that once a helimagnon is excited and the spiral slides, the motion, which rotates the local spin frame, produces a thresholdless rotational Doppler shift of the helimagnon spectrum, with even winding for the linear mode and odd winding for its second harmonic. Stronger excitation generates one finite-momentum phason. Mixing of that phason with the helimagnon produces a helimagnon frequency comb only inside a finite driving window, spaced by the phason frequency fixed by the spiral length rather than by the sliding rate. These two scales are separable in a microwave experiment: the Doppler offset tracks the sliding velocity, whereas the comb spacing scales inversely with the length of the spiral.

</details>


### [109] [Limits of Lorenz number estimation from the temperature-dependent Seebeck coefficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08881)
*Naoki Sato,Takao Mori*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文在固定有限谱表示下以线性规划（linear programming）构造与观测相容的洛伦兹数（Lorenz number）最小与最大界，考察温度依赖塞贝克系数（Seebeck coefficient）及电导率对洛伦兹数约束能力的信息含量。结果显示五个温度点的塞贝克数据可将区间宽度收窄约97.8%，但单一能带情形在400 K仍保留约[1.46453, 2.37037]×10⁻⁸ W Ω K⁻²的区间，即使塞贝克系数与电导率完全精确也不能唯一确定电子热导率（electronic thermal conductivity）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分离电子热导率与晶格热导率（lattice thermal conductivity）依赖由塞贝克系数并经假设的能带与散射模型（band and scattering model）估算的洛伦兹数，其可靠性存疑。作者因此追问：洛伦兹数的信息究竟有多少来自温度依赖的塞贝克数据本身，而非来自所假设的模型。

Method: 将相容洛伦兹数的上下界表述为固定有限谱表示（spectral representation）下的线性规划（linear program），假定化学势（chemical potential）已知，且每个候选谱在各观测温度下具有共同的能量依赖关系。参考塞贝克系数由单能带（single-band）与同号双能带（same-sign two-band）模型生成，观测温度数由1增至5，并比较谱幅度自由变化与幅度比值已知两种情形。

Result: 观测温度由1个增至5个时，两类模型的洛伦兹数区间宽度分别收窄约97.6%与97.8%。单能带示例在400 K仍有约[1.46453, 2.37037]×10⁻⁸ W Ω K⁻²的达到区间；当谱幅度可随温度独立变化时，精确的电导率数值不进一步收窄区间。若已知各温度间谱幅度比值，电导率可将全部相容洛伦兹数限制在宽度不足五温度自由幅度区间1%的包络区间内。

Conclusion: 温度依赖塞贝克数据能显著收窄相容洛伦兹数范围，但不足以唯一确定电子热导率；电导率仅在谱幅度的温度依赖受到独立约束时才提供进一步的谱信息。

Abstract: Separating electronic and lattice thermal conductivity often relies on a Lorenz number estimated from the Seebeck coefficient using an assumed band and scattering model. We ask how much information about the Lorenz number comes from temperature-dependent Seebeck coefficients rather than the assumed model. We formulate compatible minimum and maximum Lorenz numbers as linear programs in a fixed finite spectral representation, assuming known chemical potentials and a common energy dependence across observation temperatures for each candidate spectrum. Reference Seebeck coefficients are generated by single-band and same-sign two-band models. Increasing the prescribed observations from one to five temperatures reduces the Lorenz interval widths by approximately 97.6% and 97.8%, respectively. Nevertheless, the single-band example retains an attained interval of approximately $[1.46453,2.37037]\times10^{-8}$ W $Ω$ K$^{-2}$ at 400 K. Exact electrical conductivity values do not narrow these ranges when spectral amplitudes can vary independently with temperature. Thus, even exact Seebeck coefficients and electrical conductivities at all five temperatures need not uniquely determine electronic thermal conductivity at 400 K. For the same single-band example, known ratios of spectral amplitudes across temperatures allow electrical conductivity values to confine all compatible Lorenz numbers to an enclosing interval less than 1% as wide as the five-temperature free-amplitude interval. Temperature-dependent Seebeck data strongly narrow the compatible range, while conductivity provides further spectral constraints when the temperature dependence of spectral amplitudes is independently restricted.

</details>


### [110] [A Real-Space Framework for the Bare and Interacting Longitudinal Susceptibility of Solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08894)
*Rohit Pathak,Abhay Mishra,Mikhail I. Katsnelson,Anna Delin,Olle Eriksson,Vladislav Borisov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作在全势线性缀加平面波（full-potential linear muffin-tin orbital）框架下实现了裸纵向磁化率（bare longitudinal susceptibility）与相互作用磁化率，并应用于Cr、FeSe、LaTe3与2H-NbSe2四种密度波材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在在第一性原理电子结构计算中提供按格位、自旋与轨道分辨的磁化率/电荷响应工具，用以分析密度波（density-wave）材料中涨落与巡游磁性（Stoner不稳定性）的来源。

Method: 裸磁化率由Kohn-Sham格林函数（Green's function）的Matsubara求和在实空间构建，并按格位、自旋、轨道分解；自旋与电荷磁化率通过戴森方程（Dyson equation）结合在位交换关联核（exchange-correlation kernel）获得。求解器先在bcc Fe的Stoner型不稳定性上检验，再用于四种密度波材料。

Result: 裸磁化率在bcc Cr中于H点出现峰值（非磁态接近Stoner不稳定性），在FeSe中于条纹矢量M处出现峰值；LaTe3中Te网络的响应峰靠近观测到的电荷密度波矢量，出现在相邻布里渊区的镜像位置，两个等价矢量的选择由晶胞中格位的相位决定；2H-NbSe2中响应调制较弱，观测矢量位于宽极大值的侧翼，与电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）的重要性一致。反铁磁序使Cr中序矢量的裸纵向响应降低约8%，FeSe中约减半；轨道分解揭示了最近邻键上贡献最大的轨道。

Conclusion: 该实现可对密度波材料中磁化率的结构与轨道特征进行定量分析，并说明磁有序、格位相位及电子-声子耦合对响应的影响。

Abstract: We present an implementation of the bare and interacting longitudinal susceptibility in a full-potential linear muffin-tin orbital code. The bare susceptibility is built in real space from Matsubara sums of Kohn-Sham Green's functions and is resolved by site, spin and orbital degrees of freedom; the spin and charge susceptibilities follow from a Dyson equation, with an on-site exchange-correlation kernel. After testing the solver on the Stoner-like instability of bcc Fe, we apply it to four density-wave materials. The bare susceptibility peaks at H in bcc Cr, where the nonmagnetic state is close to a Stoner instability, and at the stripe vector M in FeSe. In LaTe3 the response of the Te nets peaks close to the observed charge-density-wave vector, at its image in the neighbouring Brillouin zone; which of the two equivalent vectors carries the response is set by the phases of the sites in the cell. In 2H-NbSe2 the response is weakly modulated, and the observed vector lies on the flank of a broad maximum, consistent with the known importance of electron-phonon coupling there. Antiferromagnetic order reduces the bare longitudinal response at the ordering vector by ~8% in Cr and about half in FeSe. The orbital decomposition of the bare susceptibility shows which orbitals contribute the most for the nearest-neighbor bonds.

</details>


### [111] [A stochastic propagation method for large-scale phonon calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08926)
*Wenyingdi He,Weiqing Zhou,Shengjun Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文将随机传播方法（stochastic propagation method, sPM）从电子系统推广到晶格动力学，用于大规模声子计算，实现频率估计、振动态密度构建以及选定声子极化矢量的重构，并以线性标度替代立方标度的完全对角化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构复杂材料的大规模声子计算受限于完全对角化（full diagonalization）的三次方成本，需要更低标度的替代方案。

Method: 在动力学矩阵（dynamical matrix）下传播随机初始向量；对有限程相互作用，在固定采样与传播参数时主导传播成本随原子数线性增长；并给出误差分析。

Result: 误差分析表明，对于谱分辨且频率非零的非简并模式，在小误差区间内延长传播比单纯增加随机初始态数目能更快地渐近降低频率估计误差；与直接对角化的比较验证了谱估计与选定极化矢量，收敛测试支持所预测的误差标度。

Conclusion: sPM可为结构复杂材料提供线性标度的声子频率与态密度估计及极化矢量重构，是替代完全对角化的可行途径。

Abstract: Large-scale phonon calculations for structurally complex materials are limited by the cubic cost of full diagonalization. We extend the stochastic propagation method (sPM) from electronic systems to lattice dynamics for estimating phonon frequencies, constructing vibrational densities of states, and reconstructing selected phonon polarization vectors by propagating random initial vectors under the dynamical matrix. For finite-range interactions, the leading propagation cost scales linearly with atom number at fixed sampling and propagation parameters. Our error analysis shows that, for nondegenerate modes that are spectrally resolved and have nonzero frequency in the small-error regime, extending propagation yields faster asymptotic reduction of frequency-estimation errors than increasing the number of random initial states alone. Comparisons with direct diagonalization validate the spectral estimates and a selected polarization vector. Convergence tests support the predicted scaling of frequency errors for selected resolved modes.

</details>


### [112] [Spontaneous Formation of Lateral Defect-Density Gradient in CVD-Grown MoS2 Monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09103)
*M. Kostka,H. -S. Chen,C. -Y. Hsiao,Y. -R. Chu,J. Mach,M. Bartošík,J. Šťastný,T. Šikola,W. -C. Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 二硫化钼（MoS₂）薄片在化学气相沉积（CVD）生长过程中自发形成缺陷密度梯度，可在一个样品内连续表征缺陷态的光学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料缺陷工程（defect engineering）可调控电子、光学与催化性质，但缺陷态MoS₂因可控缺陷制备复杂且不一致而研究不足。

Method: 利用CVD生长中自发形成的缺陷密度梯度，结合光致发光（photoluminescence）与拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）面扫描成像，并对激子发光进行解卷积分析。

Result: 梯度在单个薄片内呈横向分布；激子发光解卷积表明电子掺杂（electron doping）是光学响应空间变化的主因，其来源很可能是生长中形成的硫空位（sulfur vacancies），并由拉曼模式位移佐证。

Conclusion: 梯度被归因于生长中成核中心附近的前驱体耗尽（precursor depletion）导致缺陷密度渐变；该结构为在单一样品内系统表征缺陷态MoS₂提供便捷平台，硫空位驱动的表面活化（surface activation）对催化应用具有潜在意义。

Abstract: Defect engineering in two-dimensional materials is a powerful strategy for tuning electronic, optical, and catalytic properties. While pristine molybdenum disulfide (MoS2) has been thoroughly studied, defective MoS2 remains poorly understood due to the complexity and inconsistency of controlled defect creation. Here, we report a defect-density gradient that forms spontaneously in MoS2 flakes during chemical vapor deposition (CVD) growth. Photoluminescence and Raman spectroscopy mapping reveal the lateral arrangement of this gradient across a single flake. Deconvolution of the excitonic photoluminescence emission identifies electron doping as the key factor governing the spatial variation in optical response. This doping likely originates from sulfur vacancies formed during CVD growth, as further supported by the observed Raman mode shifts. We propose that this gradient arises from precursor depletion near the nucleation center during growth, resulting in a gradual variation in defect density. This lateral defect structure provides a convenient platform for systematically characterizing defective MoS2 across a continuous range of defect densities within a single sample. The sulfur-vacancy-driven surface activation identified here can also be important for future catalytic applications.

</details>


### [113] [A Monte Carlo study of thermal conductivity in black phosphorene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09149)
*L. O. Vilyaev,D. V. Zavyalov,V. I. Konchenkov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用蒙特卡洛方法结合 Green-Kubo 关系，对黑磷烯中电子在声学与光学声子上的运动与散射进行统计建模，估算其电子热导率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在研究黑磷烯（black phosphorene）的电子热导率，并检验其各向异性特征，同时为既有理论结果提供更严格的依据。

Method: 使用蒙特卡洛方法（Monte Carlo method）与 Green-Kubo 关系（Green-Kubo relations），对电子在声学声子（acoustic phonons）和光学声子（optical phonons）上的运动与散射进行统计建模，散射概率以解析形式表达。

Result: 结果显示热导率具有显著各向异性（anisotropy）：扶手椅方向（armchair direction）的热导率数值比锯齿方向（zigzag direction）高一个数量级，与相关材料的实验数据一致。

Conclusion: 该建模为以往关于黑磷烯各向异性热导率的若干理论工作结果提供了更严格的论证依据。

Abstract: In this work the electronic thermal conductivity of black phosphorene is studied. Using the Monte Carlo method together with the Green-Kubo relations, a statistical modelling of the motion and scattering of electrons on acoustic and optical phonons is performed to estimate the electronic thermal conductivity. The scattering probability is expressed analytically. The obtained results show a pronounced anisotropy of the thermal conductivity: the thermal conductivity values along the armchair direction are an order of magnitude larger than those along the zigzag direction, in agreement with the experimental data for related materials. In addition, the modelling allowed a more rigorous justification of some results of previous theoretical works on the anisotropic thermal conductivity of black phosphorene.

</details>


### [114] [From local learning to global electronic properties in large-scale systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09153)
*Yu Cheng,Zhichang Fu,Yechen Chen,Yonggang Li,Weiqing Zhou,Shengjun Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将实空间电荷密度学习与基于预测密度构建Kohn-Sham哈密顿量的随机传播相结合，在无需目标体系自洽迭代与显式对角化的情况下，实现了百万级硅原子的电子结构计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从局域原子环境出发的机器学习能高效获得电子结构，但在大体系中评估全局电子性质（如态密度、光学与输运性质）仍然计算代价高昂，亟需将机器学习方法的实用尺度扩展到真实材料与器件的长度尺度。

Method: 方法由三部分组成：学习实空间电荷密度（real space charge density）；用预测密度构造Kohn-Sham哈密顿量（Kohn-Sham Hamiltonian）并进行随机传播（stochastic propagation）；借助分层分块（hierarchical blocking）在有限的GPU显存内完成稠密网格推断。该框架规避了目标体系的自洽迭代（self-consistent iterations）与显式对角化（explicit diagonalization）。

Result: 在硅体系中演示了百万原子（one million Si atoms）规模的态密度（density of states）计算，以及十万原子以上规模的电子光学与输运计算，且无需为每种性质单独训练模型。

Conclusion: 该框架拓展了Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论（Kohn-Sham DFT）下电子结构计算的实际可及尺度，为在真实材料与器件相关的长度尺度上研究原子结构如何决定电子、光学与输运性质提供了可能。

Abstract: Machine learning from local atomic environments offers an efficient route to electronic structure, but evaluating global electronic properties in large systems remains computationally demanding. Here we combine real space charge density learning with stochastic propagation using Kohn-Sham Hamiltonians constructed from the predicted densities. The framework avoids target system self-consistent iterations and explicit diagonalization, while hierarchical blocking enables dense grid inference within limited GPU memory. We demonstrate density of states calculations for one million Si atoms and optical and transport calculations for over one hundred thousand atoms, without training separate models for each property. This framework extends the practical scale of electronic structure calculations within Kohn-Sham DFT, opening opportunities to investigate how atomic structure governs electronic, optical and transport properties at length scales relevant to real materials and devices.

</details>


### [115] [Pressure-induced symmetry breaking and polarity in the van der Waals magnet CrSBr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09202)
*Amit Pawbake,Sitaram Ramakrishnan,Kseniia Mosina,Aljoscha Soll,Zdenek Sofer,Pierre Rodiere,Rolf Heid,Gaston Garbarino,Pierre Bouvier,Clement Faugeras*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过低温单晶X射线衍射、拉曼散射和光致发光测量，结合第一性原理计算，揭示CrSBr在压力与温度下的晶体对称性及电子态演化。发现约1.5 GPa和6.5 GPa处发生两次压力诱导结构相变，并伴随声子异常与直接-间接带隙转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华磁体（van der Waals magnet）CrSBr是研究结构、磁性与电子耦合现象的重要平台，但其晶体对称性与电子态在压力和温度下的演化尚未明确。

Method: 采用低温单晶X射线衍射（single-crystal X-ray diffraction）、拉曼散射（Raman scattering）与光致发光（photoluminescence）测量，在最高12 GPa的静水压（hydrostatic pressure）下开展实验，并辅以第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）。

Result: 观察到级联压力诱导结构相变：约1.5 GPa时由正交Pmmn相转变为单斜P2₁/m相，约6.5 GPa时出现极性正交Pmm2相；对称性变化伴随声子异常（phonon anomalies）及直接-间接带隙交叉（direct-to-indirect bandgap crossover）。

Conclusion: 结果表明压力可作为调控手段（knob），以耦合方式（coupled manner）调控CrSBr的结构、对称性与电子性质。

Abstract: The van der Waals magnet CrSBr has emerged as a fascinating platform for exploring coupled structural, magnetic, and electronic phenomena. Yet, the evolution of its crystal symmetry and electronic states under pressure and temperature remains unresolved. To elucidate this we employ low-temperature single-crystal X-ray diffraction, Raman scattering and photoluminescence measurements under hydrostatic pressures of up to 12 GPa, complemented by first-principles calculations.We observe a cascade of pressure-induced structural transitions from the orthorhombic $Pmmn$ to monoclinic $P2_1/m$ phase at $\approx$ 1.5 GPa, followed by the emergence of a polar orthorhombic $Pmm$2 phase $\approx$ 6.5 GPa. The change in symmetry is accompanied by phonon anomalies and a direct-to-indirect bandgap crossover. Our results demonstrate pressure as a knob to engineer structure, symmetry and electronic properties in CrSBr in a coupled manner.

</details>


### [116] [A Compact High-Repetition-Rate Intense THz Source for Atomic-Scale Materials Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09255)
*Léo Guiramand,Florant Exertier,Alexandre Fahy,Anna Martinez,Matteo de Tullio,Saïd Idlahcen,Jonathan Houard,Gerald da Costa,Thomas Godin,Xavier Ropagnol,Domenico Paparo,François Blanchard,Angela Vella,Ammar Hideur*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作报告了一种100 kHz台式高强度单周期太赫兹（THz）源，基于铌酸锂（lithium niobate）中的光整流（optical rectification）与阶梯式倾斜脉冲前沿（tilted-pulse-front）方案，由工业级镱（ytterbium）激光驱动，并集成到原子探针层析（atom probe tomography, APT）中验证其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代超快光子学需要同时具备高重频、强场幅值与长期稳定性的紧凑实用THz源，但三者兼顾仍是挑战；此前THz辅助APT仅限低重频源，限制了分析通量与可及体积。

Method: 采用优化的阶梯式倾斜脉冲前沿光整流方案在铌酸锂中产生单周期THz脉冲，由工业级镱激光在100 kHz下驱动；并以铝纳米针尖（nanotip）为对象，将源集成至APT仪器，与常规电压辅助和激光辅助模式进行对比基准测试。

Result: 室温下光- THz转换效率超过0.13%，峰值电场达数百kV/cm，且长期稳定性优异；100 kHz THz辅助APT实现快速大体积采集，同时提升质量分辨能力（mass resolving power）、降低热拖尾（thermal tailing）并保持高质量三维重构。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了紧凑、可扩展的高重频强场THz平台，可应用于强场物理、非线性THz光子学、超快光谱与纳米尺度材料表征。

Abstract: High-intensity terahertz (THz) sources combining high repetition rates, strong field amplitudes, and long-term stability are key enabling technologies for next-generation ultrafast photonics. However, simultaneously achieving these characteristics in a compact and practical architecture remains a major challenge. Here, we report a high-performance tabletop THz source delivering intense single-cycle pulses at 100 kHz, generated by optical rectification in lithium niobate using an optimized echelon-based tilted-pulse-front scheme and driven by an industrial-grade ytterbium laser. The source achieves optical-to-THz conversion efficiencies exceeding 0.13% at room temperature, peak electric fields of several hundred kV/cm, and excellent long-term stability, establishing a robust platform for high-average-power strong-field THz applications. As a proof of concept, the source was integrated into an atom probe tomography (APT) instrument. APT reconstructs the three-dimensional chemical composition of materials through field-induced ion emission from a sharp nanotip. While THz-assisted ion emission has been shown to improve analytical performance by reducing thermal effects, previous demonstrations were limited to low-repetition-rate THz sources, restricting throughput and accessible analysis volumes. Using an aluminum nanotip, we demonstrate efficient THz-assisted APT at 100 kHz and benchmark its performance against conventional voltage- and laser-assisted operation. The high repetition rate enables rapid large-volume acquisition while improving mass resolving power, reducing thermal tailing, and preserving high-quality three-dimensional reconstructions. Beyond APT, this work establishes a compact and scalable high-repetition-rate THz platform for strong-field physics, nonlinear THz photonics, ultrafast spectroscopy, and material characterization at the nanoscale.

</details>


### [117] [Non-trivial topological spin-polarized surface states induced nonreciprocal transport in the hourglass semimetal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09258)
*Zahir Muhammad,Ghulam Hussain,Zia ur Rehaman,Rajibul Islam,Igor Antoniazzi,Muhammad Ismail Khan,Carmine Autieri,Maciej R. Molas,Xiaoguang Li,Xiaoyang Lina Azizur Rahmana,Weisheng Zhaoa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文以典型沙漏型狄拉克半金属（hourglass Dirac semimetal）Ta₃GeTe₆为对象，结合ARPES、霍尔测量、拉曼光谱与DFT计算，揭示其非平庸电子拓扑结构，并指出表面反演对称性破缺（inversion-symmetry breaking）是驱动非互易输运（nonreciprocal transport）行为的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑半金属因奇异的能带交叉（band crossings）与受保护的表面态而受到广泛关注，Ta₃GeTe₆已被实验与理论共同确认为沙漏型狄拉克半金属的原型材料；本文旨在系统验证其拓扑能带特征并厘清非互易输运的物理起源。

Method: 采用ARPES确认沙漏型能带色散与Rashba型能带，霍尔测量获取空穴载流子浓度与非互易输运行为，拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）表征声子各向异性对称性，DFT计算获得体能带交叉与表面自旋极化态，并辅以唯象玩具模型（phenomenological toy model）预测非互易电阻。

Result: 霍尔测量显示高空穴载流子浓度与非互易输运行为，与唯象模型预测的非互易电阻及观测到的方向依赖磁阻（magnetoresistance）一致；拉曼光谱揭示两重与四重声子对称性拖曳，建立对称性依赖的电阻；DFT显示费米能级附近的狄拉克型与沙漏型狄拉克能带交叉，以及表面反演对称性破缺诱导的Rashba型自旋劈裂与自旋极化表面态。

Conclusion: 实验与理论结果表明Ta₃GeTe₆具有非平庸电子拓扑（non-trivial electronic topology），且表面反演对称性破缺是驱动该材料非互易输运行为的核心机制。

Abstract: Quantum materials such as topological semimetals have gained consummate interest due to their exotic band crossings and protected surface states. In particular, Ta$_3$GeTe$_6$ has emerged as a prototypical hourglass Dirac semimetal, as confirmed by both experimental and theoretical analysis. The ARPES confirms the topological hourglass-type band dispersions and Rashba-type band, while Hall measurements reveal a high hole carrier concentration and nonreciprocal transport behavior. These findings are further supported by a phenomenological toy model, which predicts a nonreciprocal resistance, consistent with the observed direction-dependent magnetoresistance. Furthermore, the Raman spectroscopy uncovers two-fold and four-fold phonon symmetries drag of highly anisotropic phonons, which establishes symmetry-dependent resistance. DFT calculations reveal Dirac-like and hourglass Dirac band crossings near the Fermi level for the bulk, with Rashba-type spin splitting induced by the surface inversion-symmetry breaking, leading to spin-polarized surface states. The combined experimental and theoretical results demonstrate that Ta$_3$GeTe$_6$ hosts a non-trivial electronic topology, with the inversion-symmetry breaking at the surface as the driver of this nonreciprocal transport behavior.

</details>


### [118] [Physics Informed Priors for Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials with Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09325)
*George I. Simmons,Thomas D. Swinburne,Thomas Hudson,James R. Kermode*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种在 POPS-Regression 基础上引入物理先验、并在模型系综上强制执行的不确定性方案，可同时获得对模型误设（model misspecification）敏感的不确定性估计与物理合理性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势（machine learning interatomic potentials）的标准不确定性量化方法难以刻画模型误设的影响，而误设是显著误差来源；已有的误设感知方案（如 POPS-Regression）未保证后验分布的物理性，且不确定性估计相对真实误差偏保守。

Method: 构建模型误设感知方案：在 POPS-Regression 基础上允许用户指定物理先验（physical priors），并在所得模型系综上强制施加；适用于可由凸参数集（convex set）表示的物理量。借助线性与消息传递神经网络（message-passing neural network）架构中的线性性，将神经网络读出层近似为基于固定非线性特征的线性模型以应用该方案。

Result: 在声子振动（phononic vibrations）与弹性性质（elastic properties）上演示了该方案，并分别应用于线性势与消息传递神经网络势两类模型。

Conclusion: 该方法通用性强，可作为手工构造或主动学习（active learning）数据集构建的替代途径，通过补充数据纠正物理失效，从而降低面向特定应用设计势函数的计算开销。

Abstract: Standard uncertainty quantification methods for machine learning interatomic potentials often fail to quantify the effects of model misspecification despite its prominence as a source of error. To combat this, misspecification-aware uncertainty schemes have been gaining attention, pointwise optimal parameter set regression (POPS-Regression) among them. However, no guarantees on the physicality of the posterior distribution are given and uncertainty estimates can be conservative compared to the true error. To combat this, we present a model misspecification aware scheme that builds on POPS-Regression by allowing users to specify physical priors that are enforced across the obtained ensemble of models. This allows the user to incorporate misspecification-aware uncertainties without compromising on physical properties. The scheme is applicable to any physical quantity that can be represented by a convex set of parameters and so is robust for a wide variety of static properties which can help inform dynamical simulations. Here, we demonstrate the scheme for phononic vibrations and elastic properties. The scheme is also applied to both linear and message-passing neural network potentials by exploiting the linearity available in both architectures; for neural network potentials we approximate the readout layer as a linear model based on fixed non-linear features. The versatility of the method provides an alternative to hand-crafted or active learning approaches to data-set construction to correct physical failures by adding data thus reducing the computational overhead for designing potentials for specific applications.

</details>


### [119] [Graph representations of dislocation networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09534)
*Gaurav Lathiya,Markus Stricker*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究将离散位错动力学（discrete dislocation dynamics, DDD）模拟得到的位错网络（dislocation network）表示为图（graph），以少量拓扑性质刻画网络状态，并用随机森林（random forest）模型从这些图性质与应变率预测屈服应力（yield stress），在多盒尺寸下取得 $R^2\approx0.86-0.90$（大盒尺寸 $R^2\approx0.91-0.94$）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体（crystal）的力学响应由位错网络演化决定，但定量地将“网络状态”与屈服应力联系起来仍具挑战性，作者因此寻求一种紧凑、可量化的微观结构描述方式。

Method: 把 DDD 模拟获得的位错网络构建为图，采用五种不同的图表示（graph representation），以一组紧凑的拓扑性质（topological properties）描述每个网络；再用标准随机森林（random forest）模型结合施加的应变率（strain rate）预测屈服应力，并采用按应变率分层的交叉验证（strain rate stratified cross-validation）评估。

Result: 模型在所有盒尺寸下预测屈服应力的 $R^2\approx0.86-0.90$，在较大盒尺寸下为 $R^2\approx0.91-0.94$；且该精度在五种图表示下保持一致，说明拓扑-应力关系是图性质的稳健特征，而非某一种表示的伪影（artefact）。

Conclusion: 作者提出图拓扑（graph topology）可作为连接位错网络微观结构状态与力学响应的紧凑且稳健的途径，用以补充已有的变量体系。

Abstract: The mechanical response of a crystal is governed by the evolving network of dislocations it contains, yet quantitatively linking the `state of that network' to its yield stress remains challenging. Here we represent the dislocation networks obtained from discrete dislocation dynamic simulation as graphs, using five distinct graph representations, characterize each network through a compact set of topological properties, and predict the yield stress from these graph properties and the applied strain rate using a standard random forest model evaluated with strain rate stratified cross-validation. The model predicts the yield stress with $R^2\approx0.86-0.90$ across all box sizes and $R^2\approx0.91-0.94$ for larger box sizes, and this accuracy is consistent across all five representations, indicating that the topology-stress relationship is a robust feature of the graph properties rather than an artefact of any single graph representation. We propose graph topology as a compact and robust route for connecting the microstructural state of a dislocation network to its mechanical response in addition to established variables.

</details>


### [120] [Inverse design of superconductors and semiconductors with differentiable foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09572)
*Cristóbal Méndez,Justin Tahmassebpur,Tomás A. Arias*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出可微分 Equiformer 快速有效原子理论（DEFEAT），使预训练等变基础模型无需在分数数据上重训即可接受连续的元素权重与占位率，从而对成分、原子数、原子位置和晶格矢量进行统一梯度优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体设计需同时选定元素、化学计量比与原子结构，但梯度仅存在于连续结构变量上，成分与化学计量比等离散变量缺乏可微路径。

Method: 基于预训练 EquiformerV3 基础模型构建 DEFEAT，引入连续物种权重与占位率；利用微调性质模型的梯度及机器学习能量惩罚项，在单次优化中同时更新成分、原子数、原子位置与晶格矢量。

Result: 在声子介导超导体搜索中，45 个结构新颖设计中 39 个的 Allen-Dynes 各向同性临界温度超过 10 K，最高为 ZrTiNb2CN3 的 24.0 K，H4LiPd3（23.2 K）位于静态 DFT 凸包上；在光伏吸收层搜索中，SCAN 确认十个新结构的带隙落在 1.34 eV 目标 20% 以内且距凸包 70 meV/atom 以内，并显示训练泛函的系统性带隙误差会传播至设计结果。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了跨离散与连续变量的端到端可微材料优化；同时表明需对目标函数中的泛函系统误差进行校正，且有限温度下与固相的竞争可相对静态凸包重排候选材料。

Abstract: Designing a crystal for a target property requires choosing its elements, stoichiometry, and atomic structure together, yet gradients exist only for the continuous structural variables. We introduce differentiable Equiformer fast effective-atom theory (DEFEAT), which lets a pretrained EquiformerV3 foundation model accept continuous species weights and site occupancies without retraining on fractional data, thereby enabling gradient-based materials optimization. Gradients from fine-tuned property models and a machine-learned energy penalty then guide updates to composition, atom count, atomic positions, and lattice vectors in a single optimization. In a search for phonon-mediated superconductors, density-functional perturbation theory on 45 structurally novel designs gives isotropic Allen-Dynes critical temperatures above 10 K for 39; the highest is 24.0 K for ZrTiNb2CN3, and H4LiPd3 (23.2 K) lies on the static DFT hull. In searches for photovoltaic absorbers, SCAN calculations confirm ten novel crystal structures with band gaps within 20% of a 1.34 eV target on their respective raw or empirically corrected scales, all within 70 meV/atom of the DFT hull, and show that the systematic gap error of the training functional propagates into the designs unless the objective corrects for it. Quasiharmonic free energies from machine-learned potentials further show that finite-temperature competition with solid phases can reorder candidates relative to the static hull.

</details>


### [121] [Inversion symmetry breaking by interlayer hybridization in a tetralayer van der Waals heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09669)
*Juan C. Arias-Muñoz,Cuiju Yu,Nianze Shang,Christopher P. Edwards,Zixuan Ning,Farzaneh Bouzari,Rodolfo Canet-Albiach,Seyed Hossein Hosseini-Shokouh,Ruihuan Fang,Hanlin Fang,Sanshui Xiao,Jose L. Lado,Zhipei Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过垂直堆叠两层本征中心对称的双层薄片构建四层范德华异质结，在原本恢复反演对称的偶数层体系中实现了反演对称破缺，二次谐波强度可与单层相比，并伴随极化各向异性（强度比2.36）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层过渡金属硫族化合物天然缺乏反演对称性，是谷物理、强激子效应与非线性光学的重要平台；但其六方堆叠相在偶数层与体态中反演对称性恢复，抑制上述效应并限制应用，因此需要工程化手段在多层体系中重新引入反演对称破缺。

Method: 将两层本征中心对称的双层薄片垂直堆叠为四层异质结，并以二次谐波产生（second harmonic generation）测量作为反演对称破缺的判据，结合光谱测量与理论估算分析层间杂化（interlayer hybridization）的作用。

Result: 四层异质结出现二次谐波信号，强度与组成该结构的单层相当；光谱与理论估算表明有效对称破缺由层间杂化驱动，产生显著的电子与声子特征，并进一步诱导极化各向异性（polar anisotropy），强度比达2.36；作者提出激子对二次谐波光学偶极的不对称贡献可能影响该行为。

Conclusion: 层间耦合（interlayer coupling）被视为引入反演对称破缺、调控范德华异质结光学响应的关键机制，为二维材料光子学与光电子学提供了可行策略。

Abstract: Inversion symmetry breaking unlocks a broad range of physical phenomena fundamental for modern technologies, motivating efforts to engineer noncentrosymmetric materials. In this context, monolayer transition metal dichalcogenides, which naturally lack inversion symmetry, have emerged as a versatile platform for valley physics, strong excitonic effects, and nonlinear optics. However, in their hexagonal stacking phase, inversion symmetry is restored in even-layer stacks and bulk form, suppressing these effects and limiting applications. In this work, inversion symmetry breaking is demonstrated in a vertically stacked tetralayer heterostructure assembled from two intrinsically centrosymmetric bilayer flakes, as evidenced by second harmonic generation measurements with an intensity comparable to that of their constituent monolayer. Spectroscopic measurements and theoretical estimations indicate that effective symmetry breaking in the tetralayer heterostructure is driven by interlayer hybridization, giving rise to pronounced electronic and phononic signatures. This symmetry breaking further induces polar anisotropy, with an intensity ratio reaching 2.36. We propose that asymmetric contributions from excitons to the second harmonic optical dipole may influence this behavior. These findings establish interlayer coupling as a critical mechanism for introducing inversion symmetry breaking and tuning the optical response of van der Waals heterostructures, providing a powerful strategy for photonics and optoelectronics in two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [122] [Experimental Evaluation of High-Throughput Structure Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09671)
*Gabriele D. Di Gianluca,James Grossmann,Jackson Niedel,Juan Esquivel,Molly Crossman,Tyler Miniati,Melchior Jean,Zhongwei Li,Pawan Prakash,Andrew Valentini,Jung Soo Kim,Benjamin Geisler,Richard G. Hennig,Peter J. Hirschfeld,Gregory R. Stewart,James J. Hamlin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过电弧熔炼（arc melting）实验检验34种计算预测的三元化合物，发现多数未形成预测结构，并据此提出可用于筛选虚拟材料的简单判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量计算数据库（如 Materials Project、Open Quantum Materials Database、Alexandria）各自列出约10^5量级的预测稳定化合物，但这些预测在实际可合成性（synthesizability）方面的整体可靠性仍缺乏认识。

Method: 采用电弧熔炼（arc melting）对34种预测的三元化合物（ternary compounds）进行实际合成尝试，并对比实验结果与预测结构。

Result: 多数材料未形成预测结构，而是得到无序变体（disordered variants）或多相混合物（multiphase mixtures）；有四种形成了此前未报道的有序结构（ordered structures），其中一种的化学计量比此前已有报道但未给出结构。

Conclusion: 论文讨论了失败与成功案例，并提出简单筛选判据（filtering criteria），帮助研究者规避那些不太可能形成的假想材料（hypothetical materials）。

Abstract: High throughput computational databases such as Materials Project, the Open Quantum Materials Database, and Alexandria now each list on the order of 10^5 predicted stable compounds. However, the overall reliability of these predictions in terms of actual experimental synthesizability remains poorly understood. In this work we attempt to synthesize, via arc melting, 34 different predicted ternary compounds. The majority of these materials did not form in the predicted structure, instead resulting in disordered variants or multiphase mixtures. Four formed ordered structures not reported before, one of them at a composition reported earlier without a structure. We discuss both the failures and the successes, and identify simple filtering criteria that can help researchers avoid hypothetical materials that are unlikely to form.

</details>


### [123] [Theory of Optical and Magneto-Optical Properties of [111]-Oriented Site-Controlled Quantum Dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09704)
*N. D. O'Connor,G. Juska,E. Pelucchi,S. Schulz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出以受控对称性降低微扰（controlled symmetry-reducing perturbation）将量子点对称性降至 $C_s$，从而统一解释零磁场下 $C_{2v}$ 样与法拉第几何下 $C_{3v}$ 样的光学谱，而无需假设结构对称性发生变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位置可控的 [111] 取向量子点（quantum dots, QDs）是潜在的非经典光源，但其光谱呈现看似矛盾的对称性特征：零磁场激子谱常被解读为 $C_{2v}$ 样，而法拉第几何（Faraday geometry）测量却显示 $C_{3v}$ 样选择定则。

Method: 结合 8 带 $\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}$ 理论、自洽 Hartree 修正的组态相互作用（configuration interaction）方案与群论（group theory）分析，引入受控的对称性降低微扰，将量子点对称性降至 $C_s$。

Result: 零磁场下，低对称性附加跃迁振子强度（oscillator strength）较弱，光学响应仍以 $C_{2v}$ 样为主；法拉第几何下，同一低对称结构产生四个主导的、近圆偏振的三子（trion）跃迁，再现 $C_{3v}$ 样实验响应；而仅由零场谱推断的严格 $C_{2v}$ 描述只允许两个此类跃迁，在有限磁场下失效。

Conclusion: 该研究在无需恢复或增强结构对称性的前提下，统一描述了零磁场与有限磁场的光学响应；并表明高对称性样光谱不能唯一确定量子点的确切对称性，对更广泛的量子点及纳米结构体系具有指导意义。

Abstract: Site-controlled [111]-oriented quantum dots (QDs) are promising sources of non-classical light, yet their optical spectra exhibit apparently conflicting symmetry signatures. At zero magnetic field, excitonic spectra are commonly interpreted as $C_{2v}$-like, whereas measurements in Faraday geometry display selection rules characteristic of $C_{3v}$-like symmetry. Here, we show that both regimes can be described consistently without invoking any change in the underlying structural symmetry. Combining 8-band $\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}$ theory, a self-consistent Hartree-informed configuration interaction scheme, and group theoretical analysis, we introduce a controlled symmetry reducing perturbation that lowers the QD symmetry to $C_s$. At zero field, the optical response remains predominantly $C_{2v}$-like because the additional low-symmetry transitions carry only weak oscillator strengths. In Faraday geometry, the same reduced-symmetry structure produces four dominant, nearly circularly polarised trion transitions, reproducing the experimentally observed $C_{3v}$-like response. By contrast, an exact $C_{2v}$ description inferred from the zero-field spectrum permits only two such trion transitions and therefore fails at finite field. Our results thus provide a unified description of the zero and finite magnetic field optical response without any restoration or enhancement of the underlying structural symmetry. More generally, we demonstrate that a higher-symmetry-like optical spectrum does not uniquely identify the exact symmetry of a QD, therefore also providing useful guidance for other QD and nanostructure systems that lie beyond the ones studied here.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [The Kähler Stability Wall in Perturbative Brane--Antibrane Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08491)
*Swapnil Kumar Singh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文指出微扰型膜–反膜暴胀（brane–antibrane inflation）中“Kähler 体积稳定”与“反 D3 张力提供暴胀能”两项要求之间存在精确解析边界，并给出闭式解与可检验的磁通算术筛选条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微扰膜–反膜暴胀需要物理 Kähler 体积（Kähler volume）保持稳定，同时由反 D3（anti-D3）扇区提供暴胀能量；作者旨在刻画这一要求对允许的反 D3 张力施加的精确约束及其对有效场论描述的影响。

Method: 在领头阶幂零有效理论（nilpotent effective theory）中，以条件 U_σ=U_σσ=0 描述 Kähler 极小与去紧致化势垒（decompactification barrier）的鞍结退化（saddle-node degeneration）；对边界的正张力分支求闭式解并用单实参数一致化，进而对体积模量（volume modulus）积分得到 Schur 补奇异（Schur-complement singularity），并将结果转译为涉及翘曲喉（warped throat）、D3 单极（D3 tadpole）等的磁通算术筛选条件。

Result: 正张力分支获得闭式解，并由同一解确定 Kähler 质量、势垒高度与到端点正则距离（canonical distance）的局域标度；积分掉体积模量后出现 Schur 补奇异，表明除非暴胀子–体积混合（inflaton–volume mixing）被压低，绝热单场描述在同一边界处失效。数值检验验证了临界轨迹、鞍结标度律与由此得到的稀疏喉部磁通筛选。

Conclusion: 这些条件构成紧致实现（compact realization）的必要条件；在缺乏相应整体性与 Wilsonian 控制检验时，不能断言存在紧致真空。

Abstract: Perturbative brane--antibrane inflation requires the physical Kähler volume to remain stabilized while the anti-D3 sector supplies the inflationary energy. This paper shows that this requirement imposes an exact analytic boundary on the allowed anti-D3 tension. In the leading nilpotent effective theory, the Kähler minimum and the decompactification barrier disappear through a saddle-node degeneration determined by \(U_σ=U_{σσ}=0\). The positive-tension branch of this boundary is solved in closed form and uniformized by a single real parameter. The same solution fixes the local scaling of the Kähler mass, barrier height, and canonical distance to the endpoint. Integrating out the volume modulus reveals a Schur-complement singularity, showing that the adiabatic single-field description fails at the same boundary unless the inflaton--volume mixing is suppressed. The result is then translated into necessary flux-arithmetic filters involving the warped throat, D3 tadpole, conifold control, scalar normalization, and the full Kähler-sector mass matrix. Numerical tests verify the critical locus, the saddle-node scaling laws, and the resulting sparse throat-flux filtration. These conditions are necessary for compact realizations; no compact vacuum is asserted without the corresponding global and Wilsonian control checks.

</details>


### [125] [Explicit Symmetry Breaking in Non-Abelian Scalar Theories and One-Loop Renormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08493)
*Lucas Ducastelo de C. C. Lima*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将SU(2)复标量模型映射为SO(2N)实表示并推广至SU(N)，在标量部门显式破缺规范对称性后，用背景场方法结合Schwinger-DeWitt技术计算一圈发散并证明可重整性，进而导出重整化群方程，发现该显式破缺在渐近区使理论失稳并不可避免地导致朗道极点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔规范场（non-Abelian gauge theory）与标量场耦合是粒子物理的基础结构，但常面临高能不稳定性问题；本文旨在系统考察标量部门显式破缺规范对称性对理论高能行为的影响。

Method: 先分析经典SU(2)复标量模型，将标量多重态映射为实SO(2N)表示以形式化推广至SU(N)；在经典层面通过拆分质量参数与四次自耦合常数实现仅作用于标量部门的显式规范对称性破缺（gauge symmetry breaking）；量子层面采用背景场方法（background field method）与Schwinger-DeWitt技术计算精确的一圈发散（one-loop divergences）。

Result: 计算给出精确的一圈发散结果，显式证明了破缺理论的可重整性（renormalizability）；由此导出的重整化群方程（Renormalization Group equations）及其流的不动点分析表明，该显式对称性破缺在渐近区使理论失稳，驱动标量耦合远离原点。

Conclusion: 标量部门的显式规范对称性破缺虽保持可重整性，但在渐近能量区破坏理论稳定性，并不可避免地导致朗道极点（Landau pole），构成该类模型的高能一致性限制。

Abstract: Non-Abelian gauge theories coupled to scalar fields are fundamental in particle physics, yet they often face high-energy instabilities. In this work, we start by analyzing the classical $SU(2)$ complex scalar model, mapping the scalar multiplet into a real $SO(2N)$ representation to formalize its generalization to $SU(N)$. Within this classical setup, we introduce an explicit gauge symmetry breaking exclusively in the scalar sector by splitting the mass parameters and quartic self-coupling constants. Moving to the quantum level, we employ the background field method and the Schwinger-DeWitt technique to compute the exact one-loop divergences, explicitly demonstrating the renormalizability of the broken theory. Finally, we derive the Renormalization Group equations and analyze the fixed points of the flow. It is shown that this explicit symmetry breaking asymptotically destabilizes the theory, driving the scalar couplings away from the origin and inevitably leading to a Landau pole.

</details>


### [126] [Andreev reflections and optimal truncation in the unitary Fermi droplet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08580)
*Silas R. Beane,Adele Le Borgne,Domenico Orlando,Susanne Reffert,Maximilian Schwick*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在双标度极限（大味数 N、大电荷 Q、固定 Q/N）下研究外势阱中的幺正费米气体（unitary Fermi gas），发现谐振阱中体相（bulk）梯度展开在首三项后严格截断，该截断源于 Euler beta 函数零点且与低能常数无关；非谐振阱的体相渐近级数则因子性发散，其大阶行为由 BdG 问题的复周期轨道（复化的 Andreev 反射）决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的在于刻画大 N、大 Q 双标度极限下阱中幺正费米气体的普适热力学结构，特别是区分体相与边缘（edge）对巨势（grand potential）梯度展开的贡献，并厘清谐振与非谐振阱中展开收敛性质的差异。

Method: 问题在领头阶约化为非均匀配对背景下 BdG 算符的谱 zeta 函数；在 Son-Wingate 有效场论中计算得出 Euler beta 函数零点，用于证明体相展开截断；对非谐振阱采用热核 WKB 分析（heat-kernel WKB）确定渐近级数的大阶增长与 Borel 奇点。

Result: 谐振阱中体相展开在首三项后终止——所有更高阶体相贡献积分均为零，且该抵消是普适的、不依赖低能常数；截断在任意奇数维的各向异性阱中依然成立，故谐波势的高阶依赖全部由液滴边缘承载。非谐振阱的体相渐近级数为因子性发散，其大阶增长、符号振荡与最优截断由 BdG 问题的复周期轨道固定，并已对四次与六次阱数值验证预测的 Borel 奇点。

Conclusion: 谐振阱中高阶体相效应发生完全抵消，热力学的高阶信息集中于液滴边缘；非谐振阱则表现为可由复周期轨道（Andreev 反射的解析延拓）刻画的发散渐近级数，其 Borel 结构得到数值支持，揭示了体相梯度展开与 BdG 复轨道之间的定量联系。

Abstract: We study the unitary Fermi gas in an external trap in the double-scaling limit of large flavor number N and large charge Q at fixed Q/N. At leading order, the problem reduces to the spectral zeta function of a BdG operator in an inhomogeneous pairing background. The grand potential admits a gradient expansion with distinct bulk and edge sectors. We show that when the trap is harmonic, the bulk sector expansion terminates: beyond the first three terms every bulk contribution integrates to zero. This cancellation is general and follows from zeros of the Euler beta function computed in the Son-Wingate effective field theory, independent of the low-energy constants. The truncation persists for anisotropic traps in any odd dimension. All higher-order dependence for harmonic potentials is therefore carried by the droplet edge. For anharmonic traps, the asymptotic bulk series is factorially divergent. Using a heat-kernel WKB analysis, we show that its large-order growth, sign oscillations, and optimal truncation are fixed by complex periodic orbits of the BdG problem, which are analytic continuations of Andreev reflections. We numerically confirm the predicted Borel singularities for quartic and sextic traps.

</details>


### [127] [Discreetly Conformal: Higher-Order Chiral Scalars and the Discrete Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08581)
*Calvin Y. -R. Chen,Kurt Hinterbichler,Samanta Saha,Thomas Yan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 无质量标量场（massless scalar）在将更大一组平移对称性（shift symmetries）规范化（gauge）后，可分解为一对广义手征与反手征标量（generalized chiral and anti-chiral scalars），其作用量含高阶空间导数；该机制解释了隐藏共形对称性（hidden conformal symmetry），并可推广至德西特空间（de Sitter space）中的离散系列标量（discrete series scalars）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果指出，二维平坦空间上无质量标量在规范化平移对称性后，其经典相空间等价于手征与反手征 Floreanini–Jackiw 标量所描述的共形场论（conformal field theory）；本文旨在检验并推广这一图像。

Method: 通过规范化无质量标量场更大的一组平移对称性，将其分解为一对由含高阶空间导数（higher-order spatial derivatives）作用量描述的广义手征与反手征标量，并阐明其物理对称性与规范对称性。

Result: 这些对称性给出了带规范平移对称性标量的隐藏共形对称性；该结构可提升至德西特空间，其中离散系列标量同样分解为上述广义手征标量，从而为其隐藏共形对称性提供解释。

Conclusion: 平移对称性规范化导致的广义手征标量分解，是隐藏共形对称性的统一来源，且在平坦空间与德西特空间中均成立。

Abstract: With its constant shift symmetry gauged, a massless scalar on two-dimensional flat space defines a conformal field theory whose classical phase space is equivalent to that of a chiral and an anti-chiral Floreanini--Jackiw scalar. We show that this picture generalizes: After gauging a larger set of the shift symmetries of the massless scalar field, it decomposes into a pair of generalized chiral and anti-chiral scalars, described by actions with higher-order spatial derivatives. We spell out their physical and gauge symmetries, and show how these give rise to the hidden conformal symmetry of the scalar with gauged shift symmetries. This lifts to de Sitter space, where we find that the discrete series scalars also decompose into these generalized chiral scalars, providing an explanation for their hidden conformal symmetry.

</details>


### [128] [Analytic Bounds on Conformal Defect Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08582)
*Yuma Furuta,Wataru Harada,Yuya Kusuki,Yin Tang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于缺陷改变算符四点函数的共形自举（conformal bootstrap），仅用两个缺陷熵与一个缺陷改变算符的标度维数，导出缺陷上最低非真空标度维数的解析上界，并应用于 Ising/Potts 模型、三维 O(N) 模型与 AdS/BCFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形缺陷（conformal defects）的稳定性取决于局域在缺陷上的算符标度维数，但这些维数通常难以确定，缺乏不依赖数值求解的普适判据。

Method: 利用缺陷改变算符（defect-changing operators）四点函数的共形自举（conformal bootstrap）推导最低非真空标度维数的解析上界，输入仅需两个缺陷熵（defect entropies）与一个缺陷改变算符的维数；适用于幺正共形线缺陷及二维 CFT 的共形边界，并结合模糊球（fuzzy-sphere）计算与 AdS/BCFT 分析。

Result: 上界给出缺陷熵较大时相关算符必然出现的判据；Ising 与 Potts 模型提供精确检验；三维 O(N) 模型中，单个钉扎场缺陷（pinning field defect）的数据排除了 O(2)、O(3) 中三种及以上取向、O(4) 中四种及以上取向的稳定杂质序，并要求 O(3) 中相对角至 120°、O(4) 中至 109.47° 的缺陷间存在相关畴壁算符；模糊球计算与界一致。

Conclusion: 该类杂质中仅两态序在 O(3) 与 O(4) 中仍可能稳定，O(2) 中无稳定序留存；AdS/BCFT 中固定不等膜张力时，轻的边界条件改变算符要求边界上存在额外的轻原初算符，且对应更大边界熵。

Abstract: Conformal defects describe impurities and boundaries at criticality. Their stability depends on the scaling dimensions of operators localized on the defects, but these dimensions are often difficult to determine. We derive analytic upper bounds on the lowest nonvacuum scaling dimension using only two defect entropies and the dimension of a defect-changing operator. The bounds identify when relevant operators must appear on the defect with larger defect entropy. They follow from the conformal bootstrap of four-point functions of defect-changing operators and apply to unitary conformal line defects and to conformal boundaries in two-dimensional CFTs. The Ising and Potts models provide exact checks. In three-dimensional $O(N)$ models, we apply the bounds to direct sums of pinning field defects with symmetry-related orientations. Data from a single such defect exclude stable impurity order with three or more orientations in $O(2)$ and $O(3)$, or four or more in $O(4)$, for any geometry of the orientations, and require a relevant domain-wall operator between individually stable pinning field defects at every nonzero relative angle up to $120^\circ$ in $O(3)$ and up to $109.47^\circ$ in $O(4)$. A direct fuzzy-sphere computation of the lowest domain-wall dimension at different relative angles for $N=2,3,4$ is consistent with these bounds and indicates that, within this class of impurities, only two-state order remains a candidate for stable order in $O(3)$ and $O(4)$, while none survives in $O(2)$. A separate application to AdS/BCFT shows that, at fixed unequal brane tensions, a light boundary-condition-changing operator requires additional light primaries on the boundary with larger boundary entropy.

</details>


### [129] [Fermionic Kaluza-Klein Reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08584)
*Francesco Bertucci*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作针对任意维度的自由自旋-1/2与自旋-3/2场，在一般乘积时空（product spacetime）上完成Kaluza–Klein（KK）紧致化，并在作用量层面给出低维理论中质量与内部空间Dirac、Rarita–Schwinger算子本征值相关联的无穷质量塔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有KK紧致化研究多集中于玻色场或特定内部空间，缺乏对任意维度一般乘积时空上自旋-1/2与自旋-3/2自由场的系统处理，尤其是自旋-3/2场的KK拟设与规范不变性去向尚待澄清。

Method: 在作用量（action）层面进行分析，对自旋-3/2场引入类Hodge分解（Hodge-like decomposition）以构造KK拟设（KK ansatz）；推导得到的低维理论由无穷多质量态构成，其质量谱由内部空间上Dirac算子与Rarita–Schwinger算子的本征值决定，并讨论规范不变性（gauge invariance）的命运。

Result: 低维有效理论表现为无穷质量塔（infinite towers of massive particles），质量与内部空间Dirac、Rarita–Schwinger算子本征值直接关联；自旋-3/2场的类Hodge分解给出统一的KK拟设框架，并据此考察规范不变性的保持或破缺情况。

Conclusion: 该分析为任意维乘积时空上高自旋自由场的KK紧致化提供了作用量层面的统一表述，将低维质量谱归结为内部空间旋量算子谱问题，并为自旋-3/2场规范不变性在紧致化下的行为给出了讨论基础。

Abstract: We work out the Kaluza--Klein (KK) compactification on a general product spacetime for free spin-1/2 and spin-3/2 fields in arbitrary dimension. The analysis is performed at the level of the action, and the resulting lower-dimensional theory describes infinite towers of massive particles whose masses are related to the eigenvalues of the Dirac and Rarita--Schwinger operators on the internal space. A Hodge-like decomposition is used to write down a KK ansatz for spin-3/2 fields, and the fate of gauge invariance is discussed.

</details>


### [130] [AdS to dS Flows Dual to Pseudo-Hermitian Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08587)
*José M. Begines,Antón F. Faedo,Carlos Hoyos,Daniele Musso*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了高维“半人马（centaur）”几何的推广，作为爱因斯坦引力耦合鬼标量场的解；这些解在渐近反德西特（AdS）与渐近德西特（dS）区域之间插值，并具有全息对偶于伪厄米（pseudo-Hermitian）理论的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将原有“半人马”几何推广至二维以上维度，并借助全息（holography）框架刻画由实耦合与实算子延拓到纯虚值所得到的伪厄米理论。

Method: 以爱因斯坦引力（Einstein gravity）耦合鬼标量（ghost scalar）构造解；其中一族解来自超引力（supergravity）理论，该理论是一致截断（consistent truncation）且在弦论（string theory）中有已知嵌入；随后分析热力学与线性稳定性。

Result: 所得解插值于渐近 AdS 与渐近 dS 时空之间，两个渐近区域由视界（horizon）分隔；并确定了超引力解到十一维（eleven dimensions）的升阶（uplift）。

Conclusion: 高维“半人马”几何可作为伪厄米理论的全息对偶候选，并且部分解可嵌入弦论/十一维框架，具备进一步研究热力学与稳定性的基础。

Abstract: We construct generalizations of ''centaur" geometries in more than two dimensions as solutions of Einstein gravity coupled to a ghost scalar. The solutions interpolate between asymptotically anti-de Sitter and asymptotically de Sitter spacetimes, with the two asymptotic regions separated by a horizon. A holographic interpretation is that they are dual to pseudo-Hermitian theories obtained from the continuation of real couplings and operators to purely imaginary values. One family of solutions is obtained from a supergravity theory that is a consistent truncation with a known embedding in string theory. We study the thermodynamic and linear stability of the solutions, and determine the uplift of the supergravity solutions to eleven dimensions.

</details>


### [131] [Density kernel and temporal quantum information probes in double-scaled SYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08592)
*Johanna Erdmenger,Maximilian Gaschler,Jonathan Karl,Arnab Kundu,Georg Setzer*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在双标度 Sachdev–Ye–Kitaev（DSSYK）模型中，利用精确的弦希尔伯特空间（chord Hilbert space）构造由时间关联函数生成的非厄米密度核（non-Hermitian density kernel），并以此研究多时刻（multi-time）纠缠与时间信息诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠缠定义依赖固定时间切片上的希尔伯特空间二分，无法刻画跨越多时刻的量子关联；近期提出的非厄米密度核虽可编码因果响应、时间非经典性与量子动力学，但缺乏可控的引力对偶模型中进行定量研究。

Method: 基于 DSSYK 的精确弦表述构造时间密度核；弦空间天然的直和结构引出粗粒化（coarse-graining），去除不同弦扇区之间的相干性；结合 DSSYK 的 Lanczos 系数，用 Krylov 空间中位置依赖跳跃参数的波前（wavefront）图像刻画传播时间。

Result: 对应的核度量可量化信息在弦希尔伯特空间不同子区域间的动力学转移；减小 λ 会增强时间关联跨弦扇区的传播，同时加剧固定扇区内的记忆损失，且这一加速与小 λ 极限下半经典引力描述的出现相一致。依弦空间划分不同，探针表现出饱和、先增长后衰减或暂时性记忆损失三种行为，传播时间可由 Krylov 波前图像较好描述。

Conclusion: 借助 DSSYK 的量子正弦-膨胀子引力（quantum sine-dilaton gravity）描述及其与 de Sitter 全息（de Sitter holography）的联系，该工作为在量子与半经典引力区间探测时间量子关联提供了可控框架，并推动了密度核的几何解释。

Abstract: Entanglement is conventionally defined through a Hilbert space bipartition on a fixed time slice. Recent work has generalized this notion to multiple times through a non-Hermitian density kernel constructed from temporal correlation functions, encoding causal response, temporal non-classicality and quantum dynamics. We construct this kernel in the double-scaled Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (DSSYK) model using its exact chord Hilbert space formulation and study the resulting temporal information diagnostics. The direct sum structure of chord space naturally introduces a coarse-graining under which coherence between different chord sectors is removed. We show that the corresponding kernel measures quantify how information is dynamically transferred across subregions in the chord Hilbert space. We find that decreasing $λ$ enhances the propagation of temporal correlation across chord sectors, as well as the loss of memory within a fixed sector. This accelerated temporal dynamics in the small $λ$ limit coincides with the emergence of a semiclassical gravity description. Depending on the chord space partition, the probes exhibit either saturation, a growth followed by a decay, or temporary memory loss. The characteristic propagation time is nicely captured by a wavefront picture in Krylov space, with position-dependent hopping parameters that are determined by the DSSYK Lanczos coefficients. Finally, given the quantum sine-dilaton gravity description of DSSYK and its connections to de Sitter holography, our results provide a controlled setting for probing temporal quantum correlations across quantum and semiclassical gravitational regimes, and motivate a geometric interpretation of the density kernel.

</details>


### [132] [Reflecting Baths and Black Hole Interior Islands in JT Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08597)
*Dmitry S. Ageev,Anastasia N. Zueva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在有限反射墙浴（reflecting walls）耦合的Jackiw-Teitelboim引力中，借助岛公式（island prescription）研究纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）演化，揭示反射边界诱导的相关性会使外部量子极值面（quantum extremal surface）暂时消失，而黑洞内部岛构型在此期间主导辐射熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准黑洞信息悖论（information paradox）源于无岛熵无界增长并超过热力学黑洞熵；本文关注在有限非引力浴（non-gravitating baths）与反射边界条件下，这一图像如何被修正，以及外部描述失效时熵的去向。

Method: 针对自由无质量狄拉克物质（free massless Dirac matter），在Jackiw-Teitelboim引力加有限反射墙浴的设定下计算纠缠熵演化，显式计入反射边界诱导的相关性；并使用正则Kruskal坐标（regular Kruskal coordinates）将极值化（extremization）延拓至视界之外，在黑洞内部寻找真实的量子极值面。

Result: 浴足够短时，无岛熵（no-island entropy）在低于热力学黑洞熵处饱和，避免信息悖论；浴较大时，反射相关性驱使外部量子极值面趋向未来视界，使近视界外部岛分支暂时消失，该分支先以类似扰乱尺度（scrambling-like scale）出现，并于与浴尺度成正比的时间重现。内部岛构型在外部分支消失期间对辐射熵给出主导贡献。

Conclusion: 在所研究参数区间内，黑洞内部岛构型解决了仅用外部描述时信息悖论的重现问题，表明反射边界与内部量子极值面在纠缠熵演化中不可忽略。

Abstract: We study the evolution of entanglement entropy within the island prescription in Jackiw-Teitelboim gravity coupled to finite non-gravitating baths bounded by reflecting walls. For free massless Dirac matter, we study the evolution of entanglement entropy, explicitly accounting for correlations induced by the reflecting boundaries. For sufficiently short baths, the no-island entropy saturates below the thermodynamic black hole entropy, avoiding the usual information paradox associated with its unbounded growth. For larger baths, reflected correlations drive the exterior quantum extremal surface toward the future horizon, leading to a temporary disappearance of the the near-horizon exterior island branch. We find that this branch first appears at a scrambling-like scale, and it reappears at a time that is proportional to the size of the bath. We extend the extremization across the horizon using regular Kruskal coordinates and identify real quantum extremal surfaces in the black hole interior. We show that, in the parameter regime studied, interior island configurations provide the dominant contribution to the radiation entropy during the exterior disappearance time interval, resolving the reappearance of the information paradox in an exterior-only description.

</details>


### [133] [A Song of Spice and Ice: A diagonal dual to dS$_3$ & its Galilean flat space limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08600)
*Arjun Bagchi,Sharang Rajesh Iyer,K. Narayan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个对三维渐近de Sitter时空的对角共形场论（CFT）对偶，由未来边界CFT的全纯部分与过去边界的反全纯部分构造，可复现dS₃熵及一类复Kerr dS₃时空熵；其平空间极限则诱导出伽利略收缩（Galilean contraction）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受两个CFT拷贝间对跖映射（antipodal map）启发，论文旨在为三维渐近de Sitter时空构造一个对角CFT对偶，以复现其熵，并考察该构造在平空间极限下的行为。

Method: 将对偶构造为未来边界CFT全纯部分与过去边界CFT反全纯部分的对角组合，使两扇区具有等值反号的虚中心荷；由未来边界的波函数（Wavefunction）及其共轭构造全息能量-动量张量期望值；再取平空间极限，分析其对偶二维CFT的收缩类型。

Result: 利用Cardy公式复现了dS₃熵，以及一类具两个实视界的复Kerr dS₃时空熵；平空间极限自然诱导对角二维CFT上的伽利略收缩（与从AdS₃趋于渐近平直时空时的Carrollian极限形成对比），复Kerr族的内视界在极限下保持完整，得到与平空间宇宙学密切相关的平空间复黑洞，并由BMS-Cardy公式复现其熵。

Conclusion: 该对角CFT构造为dS₃全息提供了以Cardy公式计算熵的框架，并揭示其平空间极限对应伽利略型收缩而非Carrollian收缩，从而将复黑洞与由AdS₃中BTZ黑洞导出的平空间宇宙学联系起来。

Abstract: We propose a diagonal CFT dual to asymptotically de Sitter spacetimes in three dimensions (3d), constructed from the holomorphic part of the CFT dual at the future boundary and the antiholomorphic part of the CFT dual at the past boundary, in part inspired by an antipodal map between the two CFT copies. This gives equal and opposite imaginary central charges for the holomorphic and antiholomorphic sectors. The corresponding diagonal holographic energy-momentum tensor expectation values are constructed from the Wavefunction at the future boundary and its conjugate. This enables a recovery of the entropy of $dS_3$, as well as a family of complex Kerr $dS_3$ spacetimes with two real horizons, by a Cardy formula.
  We then explore the flatspace limit of our dS construction. With the central charges as above, the flatspace limit naturally induces a Galilean contraction on the dual diagonal 2d CFT, in contrast to the more familiar Carrollian limit when 3d Asymptotically Flat Spacetime (AFS) is approached from $AdS_3$. The corresponding limit for the complex Kerr family above leaves the inner horizon intact, resulting in flat space complex black holes ($\mathbb{C}$BH) that are closely related to flat space cosmologies which arise from BTZ black holes in AdS$_3$. We recover the entropy of these $\mathbb{C}$BHs by the BMS-Cardy formula.

</details>


### [134] [Dressed AdS$_4$ black holes in M-theory from the 3d superconformal index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08618)
*Evan Deddo,Seppe Geukens,Leopoldo A. Pando Zayas,Christoph F. Uhlemann,Wenjie Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将4d $\mathcal N=4$ SYM超共形指数（superconformal index）的相结构分析推广至3d CFT，聚焦ABJM理论与$\mathcal N=4$ ADHM理论；在有限$N$下计算含两个独立电荷的微正则超共形指数，得到大电荷范围内精确的有符号态简并度（signed state degeneracies）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4d $\mathcal N=4$ SYM的超共形指数已显示丰富相结构，其AdS$_5$全息对偶涉及（双）修饰黑洞（(dual-)dressed black holes）、灰色星系（gray galaxies）与引力子气体（graviton gas）贡献；作者希望检验此类黑洞物理是否同样出现在3d CFT中。

Method: 研究对象为ABJM理论与$\mathcal N=4$ ADHM理论；在有限$N$下构造含两个独立电荷的微正则超共形指数（microcanonical superconformal index），并据此计算大电荷区域的精确有符号态简并度。

Result: 所得简并度呈现出大$N$极限下预期的黑洞物理特征，包括孤立黑洞（isolated black holes）与被额外物质修饰的黑洞（black holes dressed by additional matter）。

Conclusion: 3d CFT的超共形指数中同样可识别出4d情形下的黑洞相结构，表明该全息图像在维度降低后依然成立，为3d CFT的引力对偶提供了态计数层面的证据。

Abstract: The superconformal index of 4d $\mathcal N\,{=}\,4$ SYM has been shown to exhibit a rich phase structure, whose holographic interpretation in AdS$_5$ involves (dual-)dressed black holes, gray galaxies, and graviton gas contributions. Here we extend the analysis to 3d CFTs, focusing on ABJM theory and the $\mathcal N=4$ ADHM theory. We study the microcanonical superconformal index with two independent charges at finite $N$ and obtain exact signed state degeneracies up to large charges. The results exhibit the black hole physics expected to emerge at large-$N$, including isolated black holes and black holes dressed by additional matter.

</details>


### [135] [NLO corrections to charged-current correlators near extremality: non-Abelian hydrodynamics meets AdS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08628)
*Matti Järvinen,Elias Kiritsis,Francesco Nitti,Andrea Olzi,Edwan Préau*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在近极端（near-extremal）全息等离子体中，解析计算了同时带有夸克化学势与同位旋化学势时的纵向带电流关联函数，并在流体力学展开的次领头阶捕捉了流体极点与AdS$_2$喉部红外模塔之间的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近极端带电黑洞几何中的AdS$_2$喉部会产生一整套红外模式（infrared modes），与常规流体力学极点相互耦合，标准流体力学描述在$\omega,k,\mu_3\ll T$之外失效，因此需要一个能同时刻画两者交织的解析框架。

Method: 在低温AdS$_5$ Reissner-Nordström黑洞（同时带夸克数与同位旋荷）背景上研究规范场涨落，采用$\omega,k,T,\mu_3\ll\mu_q$的流体力学展开并计算至次领头阶（next-to-leading order）的纵向带电流关联函数。

Result: 得到的准正规模（quasinormal-mode）公式在零同位旋化学势下重现扩散极点与红外谱的碰撞，而有限同位旋化学势会解除该碰撞；解析谱与完整数值结果精确符合，次领头阶关联函数亦显著改进谱函数，使与数值结果的吻合扩展到更高频率与动量。

Conclusion: 该解析方法可统一描述流体力学极点与AdS$_2$红外模塔的相互作用，并超越标准流体力学窗口，为近极端全息等离子体的关联函数与谱函数提供可靠的解析刻画。

Abstract: We analytically compute the longitudinal charged-current correlator in a near-extremal holographic plasma with quark and isospin chemical potentials, $μ_q$ and $μ_3$ respectively. To this end, we study fluctuations of gauge fields around a low-temperature AdS$_5$ Reissner-Nordström black hole charged both under the quark number and the isospin. The result is obtained at next-to-leading order in a hydrodynamic expansion where apart from the temperature and isospin chemical potential, the frequency and momenta are small compared to the quark chemical potential, $ω,k,T,μ_3\llμ_q$. This approach captures the interplay between the hydrodynamic-like pole and the tower of infrared modes produced by the AdS$_2$ throat controlling the geometry of the black hole near extremality. At zero isospin chemical potential, the resulting quasinormal-mode formula reproduces the collision of the diffusive pole with the infrared spectrum, while the finite isospin chemical potential lifts this collision. The analytic spectrum agrees precisely with the full numerical results. The next-to-leading-order correlator also substantially improves the spectral function beyond the standard hydrodynamic regime $ω,k,μ_3\ll T$, extending the agreement with numerics to larger frequencies and momenta.

</details>


### [136] [Type IIB Forms and Scalars from an $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{R})$ Topological Field Theory with Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08841)
*Euihun Joung,J. A. Rosabal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将IIB型超引力的标量与形式场扇区表述为带边界的十一维拓扑场论，IIB场作为边缘模出现，从而获得具有显式整体SL(2,R)对称性的真正作用量原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IIB超引力（type IIB supergravity）仅能以赝作用量（pseudo-action）描述，无法保持显式的整体SL(2,R)对称性，且F5自对偶等对偶关系需额外附加。

Method: 构建十一维带边界拓扑场论（topological field theory with boundary）；边界项将SL(2,R)规范对称性破缺为SO(2)，使轴子-伸缩子（axion-dilaton）参数化SL(2,R)/SO(2)陪集，并由边界场方程导出对偶关系。

Result: IIB场被识别为边缘模（edge modes）；边界场方程给出包括F5自对偶（self-duality）在内的对偶关系；由于作用量不直接涉及轴子-伸缩子，S对偶单值性（monodromy）表现为平坦联络的和乐（holonomy）。

Conclusion: 该构造提供了具有显式整体SL(2,R)对称性的真实作用量原理，并以几何化方式统一刻画IIB对偶结构与S对偶单值性。

Abstract: We formulate the scalar and form-field sector of type IIB supergravity as an eleven-dimensional topological field theory with boundary. Unlike the pseudo-action, it is a genuine action principle with manifest global $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{R})$ symmetry. The IIB fields arise as edge modes: a boundary term breaks the $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{R})$ gauge symmetry to $\mathrm{SO}(2)$, so the axion-dilaton parametrizes $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{R})/\mathrm{SO}(2)$, and the boundary field equations give the duality relations, including $F_5$ self-duality. As the action never refers to the axion-dilaton directly, S-duality monodromies become holonomies of a flat connection.

</details>


### [137] [Simplicity of the three-point stress-tensor form factor beyond the planar limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08878)
*Song He*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将 N=4 超对称杨–米尔斯理论中三点应力张量多重态形状因子的三环次领头色有限余项，表示为单一 A_2 型函数的置换求和，并通过 bootstrap 确定其符号与完整函数，进而联系 QCD 中 Higgs 加三胶子振幅的相应扇区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标在于给出该三环次领头色有限余项的紧凑解析表示，并厘清其与平面结果及更高圈数结构之间的关系。

Method: 采用基于独立计算的共线数据、分支割约束与经验性末项关系（final-entry relations）的 bootstrap 方法，确定符号（symbol）及完整函数；剩余函数层面歧义由单一末项关系固定。

Result: 种子（seed）的非乘积部分由 35 个六权广义多重对数（generalized polylogarithms）组合而成，低权乘积与 zeta 值项属于同一五字母代数；剩余歧义正比于 ζ_2 乘以两环平面余项。权重三左侧余积（coproduct）因子在模乘积意义下与平面结果张成同一空间，权重四时次领头色结果含四个额外非经典方向。

Conclusion: 通过最大超越性（maximal transcendentality）对应，该紧凑表示同时描述 QCD 中 Higgs 加三胶子振幅的六权次领头色扇区；并在三环约束的猜想性推广下构造了四环候选符号空间。

Abstract: We express the complete three-loop subleading-color finite remainder of the three-point stress-tensor-multiplet form factor in $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang--Mills theory as a permutation sum of a single $A_2$-type function. The non-product part of the seed is a combination of 35 weight-six generalized polylogarithms, while all lower-weight products and zeta-value terms belong to the same five-letter algebra. A bootstrap based on independently computed collinear data, branch-cut constraints and empirical final-entry relations determines both the symbol and the full function. The remaining function-level ambiguity is proportional to $ζ_2$ times the two-loop planar remainder and is fixed by a single final-entry relation. The weight-three left coproduct factors of the planar and subleading-color results span the same space modulo products; at weight four, the subleading-color result contains four additional nonclassical directions. Through the established maximal-transcendentality correspondence, this compact representation also describes the weight-six subleading-color sector of Higgs-plus-three-gluon amplitudes in QCD. We also construct a four-loop candidate symbol space under a conjectural extension of the three-loop constraints.

</details>


### [138] [AdS${}_3$/BCFT${}_2$ correspondence and 't Hooft anomaly of U(1) symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08934)
*Hikaru Toya,Satoshi Yamaguchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文从 AdS₃/BCFT₂ 对偶视角研究 U(1) 't Hooft 反常（'t Hooft anomaly）如何阻碍保持对称性的边界条件（symmetry-preserving boundary conditions），并给出 AdS₂ 切片下规范场的一般解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 't Hooft 反常（'t Hooft anomaly）在边界共形场论（boundary conformal field theory, BCFT）框架下对边界条件施加的限制机制。

Method: 采用 AdS₃/BCFT₂ 对应（AdS₃/BCFT₂ correspondence），在体时空中考虑 U(1) Maxwell–Chern–Simons 系统，并推导 AdS₂ 切片（AdS₂ slicing）下规范场（gauge field）的一般解。

Result: 结果表明 BCFT 流（BCFT current）在边界处不守恒；同时得到了 AdS₂ 切片下规范场的一般解。

Conclusion: U(1) 't Hooft 反常会阻碍保持对称性的边界条件，其全息（holographic）表现为边界流的非守恒性。

Abstract: We investigate how a U(1) 't Hooft anomaly obstructs symmetry-preserving boundary conditions from the perspective of the AdS${}_3$/BCFT${}_2$ correspondence. By considering a U(1) Maxwell--Chern--Simons system in the bulk spacetime, we show that the BCFT current is not conserved at the boundary. We also derive the general solution for the gauge field in AdS${}_2$ slicing.

</details>


### [139] [Induction instead of Feynman diagrams, L-functionals instead of Hilbert space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09032)
*Albert Schwarz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种可替代费曼图（Feynman diagrams）的归纳构造过程（inductive process），并结合L泛函（L-functional）的概念，给出一种不受红外发散（infrared divergences）困扰的量子电动力学（QED）表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于费曼图的QED表述面临红外发散问题，作者希望寻找一种能规避该困难的替代性理论表达框架。

Method: 构造一个归纳过程以替代费曼图，并将其与L泛函（L-functional）概念相结合，用于QED的表述。

Result: 所得表述不出现红外发散（infrared divergences），即在无需处理该类发散的情况下完成对QED的阐述。

Conclusion: 归纳过程与L泛函的结合可为QED提供一种无红外发散的替代性表述，费曼图并非不可或缺的分析工具。

Abstract: The main result of this paper is a construction of an inductive process that can replace Feynman diagrams. Combining this result with the notion of the L-functional, one can give an exposition of QED that does not suffer from
  infrared divergences

</details>


### [140] [Analogue Schwinger Pair Production in Acoustic Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09062)
*Ruo-Han Wang,Yu-Ye Cheng,Jia-Rui Sun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文提出在合成的规范势（synthetic vector potential）下，利用玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate）构建类 Reissner-Nordström 声学黑洞（acoustic black hole），实现类 Schweinger 对产生（Schwinger pair production），并给出极值与近极值情形下声子对的平均产生数及其实验可行性估算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于将施温格对产生这一量子电动力学（quantum electrodynamics）现象推广到声学模拟（analogue gravity）体系，利用维度约化引入有效电荷，从而在可实验实现的凝聚体平台中探索强场对产生及相关热效应。

Method: 对五维声学几何（acoustic geometry）作 Kaluza-Klein 约化（Kaluza-Klein reduction），使高维声子模在四维时空中表现为有效带电声子（charged phonons）；其感受的电场来自合成矢量势沿紧致维的分量。通过选取背景流速与合成矢量势，使约化后的声学度规（acoustic metric）呈现类 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞形式，并在极值（extremal）与近极值情形下计算自发产生的声子对平均数目。

Result: 在极值极限下，声子对平均产生数呈施温格公式（Schwinger formula）形式；带电声子对产生不仅具有与真实带电粒子施温格对产生相同的有效热解释（effective thermal interpretation），还可从五维视角解释为类霍金辐射（Hawking radiation）与超辐射（superradiance）。

Conclusion: 基于玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体的实验参数对声子对产生进行了估算，为未来实验室探测提供了理论指导，表明该声学模拟方案具备实验可检验性。

Abstract: We show that an analogue Schwinger pair production can be realized in Reissner-Nordstr{ö}m-like acoustic black holes constructed from a Bose-Einstein condensate subject to a synthetic vector potential. Upon Kaluza-Klein reduction of the five-dimensional acoustic geometry, the higher-dimensional phonon modes appear as effectively charged phonons in the resulting four-dimensional spacetime, in which the electric field experienced by the charged phonons arises from the component of the synthetic vector potential along the compactified direction. Then by appropriately choosing the background flow velocity and the synthetic vector potential, the reduced acoustic metric can take the form of a Reissner-Nordstr{ö}m-like black hole. From this metric we obtain the mean number of spontaneously produced phonon pairs in both the extremal and near extremal cases, and show that in the extremal limit this mean number takes the form of the Schwinger formula. Moreover, charged-phonon pair production not only admits the same effective thermal interpretation as Schwinger pair production of real charged particles, but can also be interpreted as the analogue Hawking radiation and superradiance from the five-dimensional perspective. Finally, we make estimates of pair production using experimental parameters in Bose-Einstein condensate, providing theoretical guidance for future laboratory detection.

</details>


### [141] [Carrollian minimal massive gravity and the fate of third-way consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09209)
*Büşra Dedeoğlu,Özgür Sarıoğlu,Utku Zorba*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了最小大质量引力（minimal massive gravity）的卡罗尔极限（Carrollian limit），发现代数层面奇异而 Chern-Simons 型数据层面正则，最终该耦合可被重整为卡罗尔拓扑大质量引力，并给出黑洞热力学与渐近对称性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究最小大质量引力在超相对论性（卡罗尔）极限下的行为，以厘清其代数结构、约束系统与物理自由度在极限中的去向。

Method: 取卡罗尔极限（代数奇异、Chern-Simons 型数据正则），分析括号、收缩曲率的三次闭合条件及 Dirac 约束，并通过辅助场与 boost 连接的可逆重定义化简拉格朗日量。

Result: 该 third-way 耦合可移除：一般参数下可化为卡罗尔拓扑大质量引力（至多差一边界项）；求得反德西特真空与卡罗尔黑洞，其旋转参数仅存于 boost 连接；退化矢量味度规使标准电荷公式失效，改用正则公式后边界电荷不含形变痕迹；Dirac 分析给出九个第一类约束、无局域自由度，大质量引力子被移除；渐近对称代数为 bms_3，中心荷来自 Einstein 与 Lorentz-Chern-Simons 项。

Conclusion: 卡罗尔极限下最小大质量引力的 third-way 耦合本质上是可移除的，极限理论无局域自由度且大质量引力子消失；黑洞具有两套各自满足第一定律的热力学数据，分别对应 Bekenstein-Hawking 面积律与 Chern-Simons 熵。

Abstract: We construct the Carrollian limit of minimal massive gravity. The limit is singular for the algebra and regular for the Chern-Simons-like data. The one divergent bracket points along the null direction of the contracted bilinear form. The Lagrangian is therefore finite while the brackets are not. The contracted curvature still obeys a cubic closure condition, forced on shell rather than identically. The third-way coupling itself, however, is removable. For generic parameters an invertible redefinition of the auxiliary field and the boost connection takes it to Carrollian topologically massive gravity up to a boundary term. The same redefinition fails before the limit. We solve for an anti-de Sitter vacuum and a Carrollian black hole, whose rotation parameter leaves the frame and survives only in the boost connection. The degenerate vector flavor metric obstructs the standard charge formula, which we replace by a canonical one. The boundary charges, obtained in two independent ways, carry no trace of the deformation. A Dirac analysis gives nine first-class constraints and no local degrees of freedom on any background. The massive graviton is removed. The asymptotic symmetry algebra is bms_3, with central charges carried by the Einstein and Lorentz-Chern-Simons terms. The black hole has two sets of thermodynamic data, each with its own first law. One obeys the Bekenstein-Hawking area law, the other keeps the Chern-Simons entropy.

</details>


### [142] [The instanton sum in two-dimensional self-dual models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09648)
*Mendel Nguyen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将二维自对偶 $CP^{N-1}$ 模型的路径积分限制在全纯构型上，发现瞬子测度在一圈阶精确，且与普通模型的一圈瞬子测度一致；$N=2$ 时瞬子气体可由自由大质量狄拉克费米子描述，但模型仍然无能隙，并讨论了 $θ$ 依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察将普通二维 $CP^{N-1}$ 模型的路径积分限制为全纯构型（holomorphic configurations）后所得的自对偶模型的性质，特别是其瞬子测度与谱性质是否不同于普通模型。

Method: 方法上，定义自对偶 $CP^{N-1}$ 模型为普通二维 $CP^{N-1}$ 模型路径积分在全纯构型上的限制，并计算其瞬子测度（instanton measure），与普通模型的一圈结果比较；对 $N=2$ 情形用自由大质量狄拉克费米子（free massive Dirac fermions）描述瞬子气体（instanton gas）。

Result: 主要结果为：瞬子测度是一圈精确（one-loop exact）的，并与普通模型的一圈瞬子测度一致；$N=2$ 时瞬子气体可由自由大质量狄拉克费米子描述，但自对偶模型仍为无能隙（gapless）；文中还对 $θ$ 依赖性作了评论。

Conclusion: 结论是自对偶 $CP^{N-1}$ 模型虽具有与普通模型相同的一圈瞬子测度、且在 $N=2$ 时可用自由大质量狄拉克费米子刻画瞬子气体，但并未因此产生能隙，仍保持无能隙性质；$θ$ 依赖性的讨论进一步补充了该模型的刻画。
