Abstract: This paper considers the self-dual $CP^{N-1}$ model in two dimensions, which is defined by restricting the path integral of the ordinary two-dimensional $CP^{N-1}$ model to holomorphic configurations. We find that the instanton measure is one-loop exact and agrees with the one-loop instanton measure in the ordinary model. For the $N=2$ case, the instanton gas admits a description in terms of free massive Dirac fermions. Nevertheless, we find that the self-dual model is gapless. We also comment on the $θ$ dependence.

</details>


### [143] [Remark on residual solutions in the $KBc$ algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09761)
*Ilmar Gahramanov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在含辅助恒等式 $(\partial^mc)^2=0$ 的 $KBc$ 代数框架下研究开玻色弦场论（open bosonic string field theory）的剩余解（residual solutions），证明控制上同调（cohomology）的算子仅依赖 $s=\gamma_1+\gamma_2$，并在正能级 $n$ 上说明上同调只能出现在直线 $s=\pm n$ 上，进而给出至四能级及六能级的部分精确结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于定义关系（defining relations）的分类无法覆盖满足附加鬼场（ghost field）辅助恒等式的剩余解族，因此需要针对该子族单独分析其上同调结构与形变可能性。

Method: 在 $KBc$ 代数中引入辅助恒等式 $(\partial^mc)^2=0$，证明控制上同调的算子只依赖 $s=\gamma_1+\gamma_2$；据此在正能级 $n$ 上将上同调定位到 $s=\pm n$ 的直线上，并逐能级计算至四能级与六能级，同时考察标准生成元重定义（generator redefinition）对辅助恒等式的保持失败情况与显式剩余解的正则化（regularization）。

Result: 至四能级，在 $s=-3$ 与 $s=-4$ 两条直线的每一点上各得到一个上同调类，给出剩余解族的精确多项式形变（polynomial deformations）；六能级上 $s=-6$ 处上同调一般消失，仅在 $(-1,-5)$ 与 $(-5,-1)$ 处为二维。

Conclusion: 剩余解族的上同调受 $s=\gamma_1+\gamma_2$ 的直线结构强烈约束，低能级上出现可精确表述的多项式形变，而高能级行为发生定性变化；标准生成元重定义不保持辅助恒等式，显式剩余解的正则化问题亦需专门处理。

Abstract: In this paper we study residual solutions of open bosonic string field theory in the $KBc$ algebra with the auxiliary identities $(\partial^mc)^2=0$ of the ghost field. These solutions are not covered by the classification based on the defining relations alone. We show that the operator which controls the cohomology on the residual family depends only on $s=γ_1+γ_2$. At positive level $n$, cohomology can appear only on the lines $s=\pm n$. We compute it through level four and find one class at every point of each line $s=-3$ and $s=-4$. These classes give exact polynomial deformations of the residual family. At level six on $s=-6$, the cohomology instead vanishes generically and has dimension two at $(-1,-5)$ and $(-5,-1)$. We also compute the failure of the standard generator redefinition to preserve the auxiliary identities, and discuss the regularization of an explicit residual solution.

</details>


### [144] [Edge dynamics of covariant higher-rank Chern-Simons theory of fractons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09786)
*Erica Bertolini,Matteo Carrega,Nicola Maggiore,Daniel Sacco Shaikh,Maria Florencia Semino*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究2+1维分形子（fracton）协变高阶Chern-Simons理论在平面边界存在时的边界性质，通过规范对称性的差分生成元导出两类积分边界恒等式：零阶矩给出服从双散度守恒律的无迹二阶流及高阶Kac-Moody代数，一阶矩给出普通守恒矢量流与标准U(1) Kac-Moody代数，并进一步分析混合体-边对易子、微观因果性（microcausality）与诱导边界作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察分形子（fracton）高阶张量规范理论在有边界情形下的对称性结构与边界自由度，特别是标量规范对称性如何通过Ward恒等式（Ward identity）的矩组织边界流与代数。

Method: 采用协变高阶Chern-Simons作用量框架，利用与标量规范对称性相关的差分生成元（differential generator），提取泛函场方程的单标量Ward组合，并对其取零阶与一阶法向矩；进一步通过泛函微分计算等时体-边对易子，并求解局部边界约束以构造诱导作用量。

Result: 零阶矩给出无迹（traceless）二阶流，满足分形子双散度守恒律（double-divergence conservation law）并实现高阶Kac-Moody代数；一阶矩显式给出法向-切向场w_i=h_{2i}并给出普通守恒矢量流与标准U(1) Kac-Moody代数。w_1与h_{11}的混合等时体-边对易子在有限法向距离非零、在内在边界极限下消失；边界流与规范不变场强空间分量的等时对易子为零，微观因果性成立；两组边界扇区解耦时推迟响应恒为零。

Conclusion: 局部边界约束解为w_i=∂_iχ与h_{ij}=∂_i∂_jξ+η_{ij}φ；两扇区解耦时诱导作用量为w_i扇区的手征玻色子（chiral boson）与h_{ij}扇区（φ为辅助场、ψ=∂_1ξ为传播场）的直和，后者在正能量支上具有非手征Luttinger描述并可做手征约化。边界条件确定w_i模的速度与正性支，h_{ij}扇区的相容性分析选出正能量支；在正能量支上两个手征模以与κ符号相反的取向同向传播。

Abstract: We study the covariant higher-rank Chern-Simons theory of fractons in 2+1 dimensions in the presence of a planar boundary. The differential generator associated with the scalar gauge symmetry singles out a single scalar Ward combination of the functional field equations. Its zeroth and first normal moments give two integrated boundary identities. The zeroth moment yields a traceless rank-two current obeying a fractonic double-divergence conservation law and a higher-rank Kac-Moody algebra. The first moment makes the normal-tangential field $w_i=h_{2i}$ explicit and yields an ordinarily conserved vector current with a standard $U(1)$ Kac-Moody algebra. Functional differentiation also produces a mixed equal-time bulk-boundary commutator between $w_1$ and $h_{11}$: it is non-vanishing at finite normal distance and vanishes in the intrinsic boundary limit. The equal-time commutators of the boundary current with the spatial components of the gauge-invariant field strength vanish, so microcausality is satisfied; when the boundary parameters are such that the two boundary sectors decouple, its retarded response vanishes at all times. Locally, the boundary constraints are solved by $w_i=\partial_iχ$ and $h_{ij}=\partial_i\partial_jξ+η_{ij}φ$. When the boundary parameters are such that the two sectors decouple, the induced action is the direct sum of a chiral boson in the $w_i$ sector and a higher-rank sector in which $φ$ is auxiliary and $ψ=\partial_1ξ$ is the propagating field. The latter has a non-chiral Luttinger description on its positive-energy branch and admits a chiral reduction. The boundary conditions determine the velocity and positivity branch of the $w_i$ mode, while the compatibility analysis in the $h_{ij}$ sector selects the positive-energy branch. On the positive-energy branch the two chiral modes co-propagate with orientation opposite to the sign of $κ$.

</details>


### [145] [5d $\mathbb{Z}_2$ S-folds and Seiberg--Witten curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09804)
*Sung-Soo Kim,Song Liu,Mudassar Sabir,Futoshi Yagi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了五维 $\mathcal{N}=1$、秩为 1 的超共形场论（superconformal field theories, SCFTs）在 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ S-折叠（S-fold）下的 Seiberg–Witten 曲线，并通过两种互补构造的相互印证完成了自洽性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象为五维 $\mathcal{N}=1$、秩为 1 超共形场论的 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ S-折叠，其 Seiberg–Witten 曲线此前缺乏系统计算；同时，受 arXiv:2112.14550 启发，需要检验质量参数调节与 $D_4$ 7-膜（7-brane）插入之间的对应关系。

Method: 论文采用两种互补构造：其一，直接在原始曲线上实现 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 作用，将成对的点等同并映为 S-折叠曲线上的单点；其二，将质量参数（mass parameters）调节至特殊值，对应插入 $D_4$ 7-膜。

Result: 将两种构造所得的 Seiberg–Witten 曲线表示为 Weierstrass 正规形式（Weierstrass normal form）后，二者相互一致，构成了非平凡的一致性检验。

Conclusion: 两种构造给出相同的 S-折叠 Seiberg–Witten 曲线，验证了 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 商构造与 $D_4$ 7-膜质量调节方法之间的等价性。

Abstract: We compute the Seiberg--Witten curves for $\mathbb{Z}_2$ S-folds of five-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ rank-1 superconformal field theories. Two complementary constructions are presented. First, we implement the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ action by identifying pairs of points on the original curve, thereby mapping them to a single point on the S-folded curve. Second, motivated by arXiv:2112.14550, we tune mass parameters to special values, corresponding to the insertion of $D_4$ 7-branes. As a nontrivial consistency check, we show that the two Seiberg--Witten curves agree with each other when expressed in the Weierstrass normal form.

</details>


### [146] [The Double Copy of the Doubly Spinning Black Ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09812)
*Damien A. Easson*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为双自旋 Pomeransky–Sen'kov 黑洞环（black ring）建立了精确的弯曲背景外尔双拷贝（Weyl double copy），其完整外尔张量可写成两个无源麦克斯韦场（Maxwell fields）的二次型，并揭示出不可通过基底变换消除的混合麦克斯韦双线性项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有外尔双拷贝研究多集中于单自旋或较简单背景，双自旋黑洞环的复杂外尔结构能否精确分解为麦克斯韦场二次型、以及混合项是否可约化，尚不清楚，故需检验双拷贝在更一般旋转背景下的适用性。

Method: 直接在弯曲背景上构造精确外尔双拷贝：一个麦克斯韦场由稳态基灵矢量（stationary Killing vector）生成，另一个为将偶极环规范势（dipole-ring gauge potential）在其真空极限附近线性化所得的带通量磁场；并考察外尔系数矩阵的逆 S_{IJ} 作为矩阵零拷贝（matrix zeroth copy）。

Result: 完整外尔张量为两个无源麦克斯韦场的二次型，其中混合麦克斯韦双线性项（mixed Maxwell bilinear）不可或缺，任何与时空无关的实或复场基底变换都无法将其消除。矩阵 S_{IJ} 可独立地由六维 Ricci 平坦母空间（Ricci-flat parent）中两个基灵矢量内积的加权极限得到，并满足 Box_g S_{IJ} = -½ F_{I ab} F_J^{ab}。

Conclusion: 双自旋黑洞环具有精确的弯曲背景外尔双拷贝，且其结构本质上需要非对角（混合）麦克斯韦项；单自旋极限可退化为 Emparan–Reall 环的 diagonal 两场外尔双拷贝，表明该构造是既有结果的推广。

Abstract: We establish an exact curved-background Weyl double copy for the doubly spinning Pomeransky--Sen'kov black ring. Its full Weyl tensor is a quadratic form in two source-free Maxwell fields: one generated by the stationary Killing vector, the other a flux-carrying magnetic field obtained by linearizing the dipole-ring gauge potential about its vacuum limit. The mixed Maxwell bilinear is essential: no spacetime-independent real or complex change of field basis removes it. We identify the inverse $S_{IJ}$ of the Weyl coefficient matrix as a matrix zeroth copy. The same matrix arises independently from a weighted limit of the inner products of two Killing vectors in a six-dimensional Ricci-flat parent and satisfies $\Box_g S_{IJ}=-\tfrac12 F_{I\,ab}F_J{}^{ab}$. The singly spinning limit recovers the diagonal two-field Weyl double copy of the Emparan--Reall ring.

</details>


### [147] [On Superconformal Index Degenerations and Half-Indices in S-Confining Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09820)
*Melisa Doğan,Ilmar Gahramanov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维 $\mathcal N=1$ 超共形指数（superconformal index）的 $p\to 0$ 极限及其与三维 $\mathcal N=2$ 理论半指数（half-index）的对应关系，并借此给出若干边界禁闭对偶（boundary confining duality）半指数恒等式的解析证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立四维超共形指数极限与三维半指数之间的系统联系，并为 Okazaki–Smith 提出的半指数恒等式提供解析证明。

Method: 在特殊函数层面将椭圆超几何积分（elliptic hypergeometric integral）退化为基本超几何积分（basic hypergeometric integral）；由味（flavor）幂零逸度（fugacity）的标度行为确定手征多重态（chiral multiplet）的 Neumann 与 Dirichlet 边界条件，规范多重态（gauge multiplet）取 Neumann 边界条件；并将该方案应用于 $SU(2)$、$USp(2N)$、$G_2$、$SU(N)$ 等 s-禁闭理论（s-confining theory）。

Result: 成功重现多个边界禁闭对偶的半指数恒等式，以 Nassrallah–Rahman 积分与 Gustafson 积分表达；对含秩二反对称手征、四个基本表示与 $N$ 个反基本表示的 $SU(N)$ 情形，给出对所有 $N\geq 5$ 成立的半指数恒等式解析证明。

Conclusion: $p\to 0$ 极限提供了连接四维超共形指数与三维半指数的可行 prescription（prescription），并可作为证明边界对偶半指数恒等式的有效工具。

Abstract: We study the $p \rightarrow 0$ limit of four-dimensional $\mathcal N=1$ superconformal indices and its relation to half-indices of three-dimensional $\mathcal N=2$ theories. At the level of special functions, this limit reduces elliptic hypergeometric integrals to basic hypergeometric integrals. The scaling of the flavor fugacities determines the Neumann and Dirichlet boundary conditions of the chiral multiplets. The gauge multiplet has Neumann boundary conditions. We apply this prescription to s-confining theories with gauge groups $SU(2)$, $USp(2N)$, $G_2$, and $SU(N)$, and recover the half-index identities of several boundary confining dualities, expressed through the Nassrallah-Rahman and Gustafson integrals. For $SU(N)$ with a rank-two antisymmetric chiral, four fundamentals and $N$ antifundamentals, we obtain an analytic proof of the half-index identities proposed by Okazaki and Smith for all $N \geq 5$.

</details>


### [148] [99.9...% of the SU(2) BFSS ground state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09837)
*Alexander Michel,Jaroslav Scheinpflug,Xi Yin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过 Rayleigh-Ritz 方法求解 SU(2) BFSS 矩阵模型（BFSS matrix model）的基态波函数，借助辅助算符（auxiliary operator）构造高效变分基，得到能量与基态重叠的严格界，并高精度复现了三引力子振幅（three-graviton amplitude）预言的渐近径向概率密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解 BFSS 矩阵模型的基态波函数是研究该模型非微扰性质的核心问题，而自由度数目庞大带来巨大计算困难，需要构造严格且高效的变分方法。

Method: 引入辅助算符 H_aux，证明其与哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）在基态子空间上一致且具有谱隙（spectral gap）；利用 SU(2) 单态扇区（singlet sector）的表示论（representation theory）大幅削减自由度，构造变分基并精确计算内积。

Result: 所得态满足能量界 E g_YM^{-2/3} ≤ 4.18×10^{-8} 与基态子空间重叠 ‖P_{ker H}ψ‖² ≥ 0.999997；高精度基态矩（ground-state moments）与矩阵 bootstrap 给出的界一致，并准确复现三引力子振幅预言的渐近径向概率密度的指数与系数。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，结合辅助算符与表示论约化的变分框架可在可控计算量下严格求解 SU(2) BFSS 基态，并为更一般规范群情形提供可行途径。

Abstract: We solve for the ground-state wavefunction of the SU(2) BFSS matrix model by applying the Rayleigh-Ritz method to an auxiliary operator $H_{\mathrm{aux}}$. We show that this operator agrees with the Hamiltonian on the ground-state subspace and has a spectral gap. For SU(2) specifically, the representation theory of the singlet sector gives an astronomical reduction in the number of degrees of freedom. This allows us to construct an efficient variational basis whose inner products we compute exactly. We demonstrate the method by computing a state that satisfies rigorous bounds on its energy $E\,g_{\mathrm{YM}}^{-2/3}\leq 4.18\times10^{-8}$ and ground-subspace overlap $\|P_{\ker H}ψ\|^2\geq 0.999997$. We compute ground-state moments with high precision, finding values consistent with the bounds from our matrix bootstrap. Additionally, the state accurately reproduces both the exponent and coefficient of the asymptotic radial probability density predicted by the three-graviton amplitude.

</details>


### [149] [Existence of extremal solutions in the 1d conformal bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09867)
*Filip Herček,Nat Levine*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文证明在一维共形场论（1d CFT）中，OPE 极大化问题存在极值解（extremal solutions），且对偶间隙为零（zero duality gap）。方法是将共形 bootstrap（conformal bootstrap）表述为 Hilbert 空间中的凸优化问题并应用 Fenchel-Rockafellar 定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极值解与极值泛函在共形 bootstrap 中具有基础性理论意义，但其存在性尚未被严格建立；在零数值截断的全无限维 bootstrap 极限下，这一问题尤为微妙。

Method: 将 bootstrap 重新表述为 Hilbert 空间中的凸优化（convex optimisation）问题，并应用 Fenchel-Rockafellar 定理进行证明。

Result: 证明了一维 CFT 中 OPE 极大化问题（OPE maximisation problems）存在极值解，这类解是满足交叉方程（crossing equation）的一大类一般相互作用、幺正（unitary）的精确解，并使某个 OPE 系数达到最大；同时证明这些问题具有零对偶间隙，即存在任意接近极值性的泛函。

Conclusion: 在全无限维 bootstrap 框架下，极值解的存在性与零对偶间隙在一维 CFT 的 OPE 极大化问题中得到严格确立，为共形 bootstrap 的极值结构提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Extremal solutions and functionals play an important role in the conformal bootstrap. These are the solutions that saturate unitarity bounds, and the dual functionals that encode the optimal bounds. While they have fundamental theoretical implications, their existence has not been rigorously established. This is particularly subtle in the setting of the full infinite-dimensional bootstrap, i.e. the limit of zero numerical truncation. In this article, we prove that extremal solutions exist for OPE maximisation problems in 1d CFT. These are a large class of generically interacting, unitary, exact solutions of the crossing equation that maximise an OPE coefficient. We also prove that these problems have 'zero duality gap', meaning there exist functionals arbitrarily close to extremality. Our approach is to formulate the bootstrap as a convex optimisation problem in a Hilbert space and apply the Fenchel-Rockafellar theorem.

</details>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Mode interaction in inclined-layer convection: a physical interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09545)
*Zheng Zheng,Laurette S. Tuckerman,Tobias M. Schneider*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 对倾斜逆重力流体层中的Oberbeck–Boussinesq方程开展数值研究，计算含不同数量同向旋转对流卷的平衡态，发现连接四个与六个相同卷分支的混合模态分支呈现卷数增减的两条对比路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究倾斜流体层中由Oberbeck–Boussinesq方程描述的对流平衡态结构，特别是含不同数量同向旋转卷（co-rotating rolls）的态之间如何相互转换，即卷数增加或减少的机制。

Method: 采用数值方法（numerical investigation）求解倾斜逆重力流体层中的Oberbeck–Boussinesq方程，计算不同卷数平衡态分支，并追踪连接四卷与六卷状态的混合模态分支（mixed-mode branches）。

Result: 混合模态分支连接了含四个与六个相同同向旋转卷的分支，并显示出两条对比性的卷数变化路径：一条路径中卷消失（或出现），另一条路径中卷合并（或分裂）。结果在(2,3)模态相互作用（(2,3) mode interaction）情景下得到解释。

Conclusion: 含不同卷数的平衡态通过混合模态分支相互连接，卷数变化存在“消失/出现”与“合并/分裂”两种不同路径，该现象与(2,3)模态相互作用情景相符。

Abstract: We carry out a numerical investigation of the Oberbeck--Boussinesq equations in a fluid layer inclined against gravity. Equilibria containing different numbers of co-rotating rolls are computed. Mixed-mode branches connect branches containing states with four and six identical co-rotating rolls. These branches display two contrasting routes to reducing (or increasing) the number of rolls, one in which rolls disappear (or appear) and another route in which rolls merge (or split). These results are interpreted in light of the $(2,3)$ mode interaction scenario.

</details>


### [151] [A Quantum Mechanical Description of Active Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09861)
*Ghansham Chandel,Siddhartha Das*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 论文提出以能量消耗率（energy expenditure rate）和动量产生率（momentum generation rate）作为描述游动活性物质（active matter）演化的基本动力学量，并据此构造拉格朗日量与哈密顿量；在傅里叶空间分析后引入复场，得到类狄拉克方程（Dirac equation）与类薛定谔方程，并用多极展开刻画游动体与流体的双向耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生命宜居的自然环境离不开耗散力与熵力（dissipative and entropic forces），活性物质需依赖能量与动量的持续通量才能生存和传播；而基本粒子以能量和动量刻画，作者据此假设活性物质的演化应由能量消耗率与动量产生率驱动。此外，生命系统广泛采用周期性、非重复的运动，其周期中储存有意义的信息，促使作者在傅里叶空间（Fourier space）中分析这些动力学。

Method: 基于能量消耗率与动量产生率构造活性物质的拉格朗日量与哈密顿量；在傅里叶空间引入复场（complex field）描述单个单元的演化概率分布及资源稀缺约束下互不相互作用活性粒子的种群密度；并将活性游动体与周围流体的双向耦合（bidirectional coupling）通过活性力的多极展开（multipole expansion）形式化，重点讨论力单极子与偶极子。

Result: 所得单单元波函数（wavefunction）的演化类似于最小耦合（minimal coupling）下的狄拉克方程，其中规范对称性（gauge symmetry）反映控制方程在任意资源景观下的普适性；同时给出类薛定谔方程（Schrödinger-like equation），其有效势项对应环境所要求的非运动基础代谢率（base metabolic rate）。多极展开中每个极对应一种不同的活性运动模式（mode of active motility）。

Conclusion: 以能量消耗率和动量产生率为基本动力学量，可将游动活性物质的演化纳入拉格朗日/哈密顿框架，并通过傅里叶空间分析自然过渡到算符形式的量子力学工具，得到类狄拉克与类薛定谔描述；结合多极展开可形式化活性游动体与流体的耦合，每个极对应一种活性运动模式。

Abstract: The natural environments habitable to life are inseparable from dissipative and entropic forces, which mandate active matter to operate on constant fluxes of energy and momentum to survive and propagate through spacetime. In contrast to fundamental particles which are characterized by energy and momentum, we postulate that energy expenditure rate and momentum generation rate are the fundamental dynamical quantities driving this evolution of swimming active matter and accordingly construct the corresponding Lagrangian and Hamiltonian of motion of the active matter. Furthermore, living systems broadly employ periodic, non-repeating motion for both locomotion and various biological functions, with meaningful information stored in the periods of these functions. Consequently, analyzing these dynamics in the Fourier space lands us at the familiar tools of the operator based, quantum mechanics. This is accomplished by introducing a complex field to describe the evolving probability distribution of a single unit, as well as the population density of mutually non-interacting active particles under the constraint of scarce resources. The resulting single-unit wavefunction evolves analogously to the Dirac equation with minimal coupling, wherein gauge symmetry reflects the generality of the governing equations across arbitrary resource landscapes. Additionally, we present a Schrödinger-like equation where the effective potential term represents the environmentally mandated, non-motile base metabolic rate. Finally, we formalize the bidirectional coupling between active swimmers and the surrounding fluid via a multipole expansion of the active force with an emphasis on force monopoles and dipoles, where each pole corresponds to a distinct mode of active motility.

</details>


### [152] [Electrohydrodynamic interactions of two Quincke rotors: asymptotic theory and boundary element simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08807)
*Debasish Das*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 悬浮于漏电介质（leaky dielectric fluid）中的弱导电球在电场超过临界值时发生Quincke旋转（Quincke rotation）；本文通过远场渐近理论（far-field asymptotic theory）与边界元法（BEM）模拟，研究电相互作用（electric interaction）与水动力相互作用（hydrodynamic interaction）如何修正该不稳定性及双球的集体旋转模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有描述（如Das与Saintillan的偶极-旋子模型）未系统处理双球间电与水动力相互作用对Quincke不稳定性及后续旋转的影响，尤其缺乏对长时间行为与近阈值集体模式的解析刻画，故需建立系统的远场渐近理论并与数值模拟对照。

Method: 从电偶极（electric dipole）与水动力旋子（rotlet）相互作用出发构建远场渐近理论；远离阈值时采用多尺度展开（multiple-scale expansion）得到慢相位方程，近阈值时采用失谐多尺度展开（detuned multiple-scale expansion）得到横向偶极振幅的耦合方程并进行线性化；同时以边界元法（BEM）模拟确定起始区间与旋转的成长与弛豫过程。

Result: 线性化识别出四种集体旋转模式（collective rotation modes），并给出其临界场与单模稳态分支及稳定性；近阈值主导阶下，电相互作用同等移动四个模态阈值，而水动力相互作用使配对夹角低于cos⁻¹(1/√3)≈54.7°时先出现反向旋转（counter-rotation）、高于该角时先出现同向旋转（co-rotation）。远离阈值时，分隔稳定反向与同向旋转的角度随场强增大；BEM模拟将起始限定于各构型的场强区间内，稳态速度对比显示配对取向改变电效应与水动力效应的平衡。

Conclusion: 研究给出了一套可系统预测双球Quincke旋转集体模式、临界场与稳定性的渐近框架，并以BEM模拟加以验证，同时与Das与Saintillan未展开的偶极-旋子模型进行了比较，阐明配对取向对电与水动力效应平衡的调控作用。

Abstract: Weakly conducting spheres suspended in a leaky dielectric fluid can undergo spontaneous Quincke rotation when the applied electric field exceeds a critical value. We investigate how electric and hydrodynamic interactions modify this instability and the subsequent rotation of two spheres at fixed separation and orientation. Starting from electric dipole and hydrodynamic rotlet interactions, we develop a systematic far-field asymptotic theory. Away from onset, slow drift of the rotation axes makes a regular expansion fail at long times. A multiple-scale expansion gives slow phase equations that select the axes according to pair orientation and field strength. Near onset, weak interactions produce corrections comparable to the isolated rotation magnitude. A detuned multiple-scale expansion gives coupled equations for the transverse dipole amplitudes. Their linearization identifies four collective rotation modes, whose critical fields and steady branches with one active mode are determined together with their stability. At leading order near onset, electric interaction shifts all four modal thresholds equally, whereas hydrodynamic interaction makes counter-rotation appear first for pair angles below $\cos^{-1}(1/\sqrt3)\simeq54.7^\circ$ and co-rotation above it. Away from onset, the angle separating stable counter-rotation from stable co-rotation increases with field strength. Boundary element method (BEM) simulations place onset within a field interval for each configuration and resolve the growth and relaxation of rotation. Steady-speed comparisons show how pair orientation changes the balance between electric and hydrodynamic effects. We compare the unexpanded dipole-rotlet model of Das and Saintillan [Phys. Rev. E 87, 043014 (2013)] with our asymptotic theory and BEM simulations.

</details>


<div id='physics.app-ph'></div>

# physics.app-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [A State-Variable Theory of Evolving Material Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09314)
*Pengfei Zhu,Leiming Du,Guoqi Zhang*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出以单一母不变自由能（parent-invariant free energy）与对称无迹内变量张量 Q 统一描述材料结构状态与力学对称性的耦合，证明结构旋转对称与切线旋转对称在任意非零耦合下相等，并给出声学张量对残余对称性的可观测含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料在加载过程中结构可演化，其力学表达的对称性同时取决于结构状态与本构耦合，二者缺乏统一描述框架；同时需避免为对称群标签赋予独立动力学。

Method: 构建含对称无迹内张量 Q 的母不变自由能，用 Q 的稳定子（stabilizer）区分各向同性、单轴与双轴状态，并以投影梯度动力学满足结构耗散不等式；针对显式双耦合弹性势推导 Q 的刚度收缩重构，并建立声学张量、声学迹重构及具校准与干扰感知的逆问题条件。

Result: 证明对任意非零耦合对，结构旋转对称与切线旋转对称相等；声学张量将残余对称性与受保护的横向简并（transverse degeneracies）相联系；小单轴状态下主导平方速度间隙为 |γ₂s|sin²ϑ/(2ρ₀)，且耦合选择性与残余保护可抑制该项。

Conclusion: 该框架在不为群标签赋予独立动力学的前提下，将状态演化、力学表达的对称性与条件可观测性联系起来，并由量纲结构场、声学、瞬态有限元及合成反演示例加以说明。

Abstract: Material architecture can evolve during loading, yet its mechanically expressed symmetry depends on both the structural state and the constitutive coupling. We connect these quantities through one parent-invariant free energy with a symmetric traceless internal tensor $\mathbf Q$. Its stabilizer distinguishes isotropic, uniaxial, and biaxial states, and projected gradient kinetics satisfies the structural dissipation inequality. Parent invariance generally guarantees only symmetry inheritance. For an explicit two-coupling elastic potential, we derive a stiffness-contraction reconstruction of $\mathbf Q$ and prove equality of the structural and tangent rotational symmetries for every nonzero coupling pair. The associated acoustic tensor links residual symmetry to protected transverse degeneracies. For a small uniaxial state, the leading squared-speed gap is $|γ_2s|\sin^2\vartheta/(2ρ_0)$; coupling selectivity and residual protection can suppress this term. We also derive a directional acoustic-trace reconstruction and state calibrated and nuisance-aware inverse conditions. Dimensionless structural-field, acoustic, transient finite-element, and synthetic inverse examples illustrate the predictions. The framework connects state evolution, mechanically expressed symmetry, and conditional observability without assigning independent dynamics to a group label.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Quantum-walk measurements with sequential imaging of the same atomic sample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09618)
*Ryuta Yamamoto,Takeshi Fukuhara*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作实现了对同一超冷原子样品的连续单原子分辨成像，并结合机器学习优化的拉曼边带冷却，用于光晶格中单原子量子行走的测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子气体显微镜（quantum gas microscope）的荧光成像虽能提供原子分布的微观信息，但光子散射会引起加热，使同一样品难以用于后续量子动力学研究。

Method: 采用针对成像后冷却优化的拉曼边带冷却（Raman sideband cooling），并借助机器学习优化冷却参数，实现多次成像循环下的高保真连续单原子分辨测量；随后测量一维光晶格中单原子的量子行走（quantum walk）。

Result: 从荧光图像确定初始原子位置，无需确定性制备即可提取量子行走跃迁概率；观测到的动力学与单原子量子行走的数值模拟一致。

Conclusion: 该方法为需要重复获取空间分辨关联数据的量子气体显微镜研究提供了降低实验开销的途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate sequential single-atom-resolved imaging of the same ultracold atomic sample and apply this technique to quantum-walk measurements in an optical lattice. In quantum gas microscope experiments, fluorescence imaging provides microscopic information on atomic distributions but induces motional heating through photon scattering, making it difficult to reuse the same sample for subsequent quantum dynamics. To overcome this limitation, we apply Raman sideband cooling with parameters optimized for post-imaging cooling, enabling sequential single-atom-resolved measurements of the same sample. By optimizing the cooling parameters with machine learning, we achieve high fidelity over multiple imaging cycles. To demonstrate the capability of this technique, we measure one-dimensional quantum walks of single atoms in an optical lattice. By determining the initial atomic positions from fluorescence images, we extract quantum-walk transition probabilities without requiring deterministic preparation of the initial atomic positions. The observed dynamics agree with numerical simulations of quantum walks of single atoms. Our method provides a route toward reducing experimental overhead in quantum gas microscope studies requiring repeated acquisition of spatially resolved correlation data.

</details>


### [155] [Raman anyon-Hubbard model in a 1D Bose lattice gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09855)
*Josep Cabedo,Claudio Iacovelli,Leticia Tarruell,Alessio Celi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文提出并命名了“拉曼任意子-Hubbard模型（Raman anyon-Hubbard model）”，证明将拉曼耦合（Raman-coupled）玻色气体装载入一维光晶格可实现对手征BF理论（chiral BF theory）的格点模拟，并数值验证其展现一维任意子（1D anyons）的核心特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意子-Hubbard模型（anyon-Hubbard model）是描述一维任意子的典型格点模型，其连续极限对应拉曼耦合玻色气体的低能有效理论；但此前尚缺乏将该理论映射到格点并可直接实验实现的方案。作者旨在填补这一空白，并突破少粒子（few-particle）实验限制。

Method: 将拉曼耦合玻色气体装载到一维光晶格（1D optical lattice）中，使玻色子跳跃穿过已占据格点时获得密度依赖的Peierls相位（density-dependent Peierls phases）；在小统计相位极限下回归任意子-Hubbard模型，在更大范围内则定义具有无界（unbounded）而非周期性统计相互作用的新格点任意子模型，并进行数值计算。

Result: 数值结果表明该修饰气体呈现一维任意子的标志性性质：基态动量分布（ground-state momentum distribution）的密度依赖不对称性、相互作用诱导的非对称输运（interaction-induced asymmetric transport）以及手征束缚态（chiral bound states）。这些特征在拉曼耦合的$^{39}$K原子体系中 readily accessible。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了拉曼修饰玻色气体与格点任意子模型之间的对应关系，为在一维光晶格中以$^{39}$K原子开展超出少粒子范畴的一维任意子实验探索提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The anyon-Hubbard model is the paradigmatic lattice model of one-dimensional (1D) anyons, in which chiral interactions make bosons equivalent to particles with fractional statistics. Its continuum limit, the chiral BF theory, emerges as the low-energy description of a Raman-coupled Bose gas with imbalanced interactions. Here, we show that loading such a gas into a 1D optical lattice realizes the lattice counterpart of this theory, which we name the Raman anyon-Hubbard model. The Raman-dressed bosons acquire density-dependent Peierls phases when tunneling through occupied sites. For a small statistical phase, the model reduces to the anyon-Hubbard model, while beyond this limit it defines a distinct lattice anyon model with unbounded rather than periodic statistical interactions. Numerically, we show that the dressed gas displays the defining properties of 1D anyons, namely a density-dependent asymmetry of the ground-state momentum distribution, interaction-induced asymmetric transport and chiral bound states. Remarkably, these features are readily accessible with Raman-coupled $^{39}$K atoms, opening up the experimental exploration of 1D anyons beyond the few-particle regime.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Structure is in the zoom: probing neural symmetry through dimensionality scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08506)
*Adil Amin*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该文指出，神经群体测量到的维度（dimensionality）中有一部分并非编码（code）本身，而是测量过程的采样下限（sampling floor）；将参与率（participation ratio）对子样本量的斜率（slope）拆分为沿声明轴（declared axis）获得的偏移与采样下限后，维度标度（dimensionality scaling）成为可解析到特定对称性扇区（sector）的测量工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献常把参与率随子样本量变化的斜率当作系统固有属性，但作者质疑其中多大比例源于测量行为本身而非神经编码，因此需要区分"编码结构"与"采样伪迹"。

Method: 对同一块约1.1万个小鼠初级视皮层（V1）神经元数据，分别沿随机刺激子集、漂移方向、神经元数量三条轴估计斜率；将每个指数拆分为采样下限与沿声明轴获得的偏移，并用两种零模型（标签置换、仅在共享混淆变量（nuisance）的样本内置换）检验偏移是否显著，同时在语言模型（language model）各层复用同一拆分。

Result: V1 数据在三条轴上给出 0.25、0.31、0.35 三个不同斜率，仅方向轴（0.31）含结构；方向对齐偏移在 8/8 光栅记录中通过标签零模型、在第二实验室 32/32 只动物中为正，并解析出编码 O(2) 对称性的偶扇区（even sector）与奇扇区（odd sector）：偶扇区决定类别添加顺序的爬升快慢、在等变网络（equivariant network）中预测相反顺序，并给出粗粒化（coarse-graining）下的闭式缩放因子；奇扇区由方向选择性神经元承载，使阶梯越过半数类别后继续爬升。语言模型上同一拆分在五种架构中区分来自 token 的内容与随深度构建的结构，且第二种零模型显示约 20 个标准差的语法性（grammaticality）信号完全来自载体构成（carrier composition）。

Conclusion: 引入采样下限与声明轴后，维度标度是可解析到轴的测量；当轴对应已知对称性时，可分辨编码的扇区内容。所提工具无需训练探针（probe），可逐层读取任意数组或 Hugging Face 模型层。

Abstract: How much of a neural population's measured dimensionality is the code, and how much is the act of measuring it? The slope of the participation ratio against subsample size is reported for cortical populations and for the layers of language models as a number that belongs to the system. It does not. One patch of eleven thousand mouse V1 neurons returns three slopes, 0.25 along random stimulus subsets, 0.31 along drift direction, 0.35 along neuron count, and only the middle one carries structure; the other two are the sampling floor.
  We split every exponent into that floor and a shift earned along a declared axis, and read the shift against two nulls: permuting the labels, and permuting them only among samples that share a nuisance. On V1 the direction-aligned shift clears the label null in eight of eight grating recordings, is positive in 32 of 32 animals in a second laboratory, and reads both sectors of the code's O(2) symmetry. The even sector sets how the ladder starts: it decides which order of adding classes climbs faster, predicts the opposite order in an equivariant network, and flows under coarse-graining by a closed-form factor that says what orientation columns do to mesoscale signals. The odd sector, carried by the direction-selective neurons, keeps the ladder climbing past half the classes. On language models the same split separates content inherited from the tokens from construction built with depth, on five architectures, and the second null shows that a grammaticality signal of twenty standard deviations is entirely carrier composition. With a floor and a declared axis, dimensionality scaling is an axis-resolved measurement, and where the axis follows a known symmetry it resolves the sector content of the code. The instrument, rung, trains no probe and reads any array or any Hugging Face model layer by layer (https://github.com/adilamin89/structure-in-the-zoom).

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Elastic Wave Imaging via Kirchhoff Migration: Resolution Analysis and Inclusion Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09137)
*Zetao Fei,Josselin Garnier*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文研究均匀介质中小弹性夹杂（elastic inclusion）的逆问题，基于小体积展开（small-volume expansion）导出PP、PS、SP、SS四种Kirchhoff偏移成像泛函的显式主阶表达式，其中SS模式分辨率最高。对椭圆夹杂，PP模式可唯一确定弹性矩张量（elastic moment tensor, EMT）并重建材料与几何参数；对一般散射体，重建结果可解释为有效椭圆表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是同时确定小弹性夹杂的位置、取向、形状与材料参数，并刻画不同P波/S波组合成像模式的分辨能力及参数可辨识性。

Method: 采用小体积展开推导四种Kirchhoff偏移成像泛函（PP、PS、SP、SS）的主阶显式表达式，得到点扩散函数（point-spread function）以量化各模式分辨率；并分析椭圆与圆盘情形下的EMT结构及参数可辨识性，另考察测量构型中心距离不足时忽略交叉模式（cross-mode）贡献引起的模型误差。

Result: SS模式空间分辨率最高；椭圆夹杂情形下PP模式在主阶渐近模型内唯一确定EMT，从而可重建材料与几何参数；一般小散射体的重建可解读为有效椭圆表示；圆盘情形参数辨识欠定，但给出体积的显式上下界；并量化了交叉模式截断导致的模型误差。

Conclusion: 理论预测与数值实验一致，验证了所提成像与重建方法的有效性，并明确了各成像模式的分辨率与参数可辨识边界。

Abstract: We consider the inverse problem of determining the location, orientation, shape, and material parameters of a small elastic inclusion embedded in a homogeneous medium from full-aperture measurements of the scattered field. Using a small-volume expansion, we derive explicit leading-order expressions for four Kirchhoff migration imaging functionals, denoted by PP, PS, SP, and SS, corresponding to the four combinations of pressure (P) and shear (S) waves used for receiver- and source-side backpropagation. The resulting point-spread functions quantitatively characterize the resolution of each mode and show that the SS mode achieves the highest spatial resolution. For an elliptic inclusion, we show that the PP mode uniquely determines the elastic moment tensor (EMT) within the leading-order asymptotic model, thereby enabling the reconstruction of the material and geometric parameters of the inclusion. For a general small scatterer, the reconstruction may instead be interpreted as an effective elliptic representation determined by its leading-order EMT. For a disk, the parameter-identification problem is underdetermined; nevertheless, explicit upper and lower bounds on its volume are derived. We further analyze the model error arising from neglected cross-mode contributions when the distance from the center of the measurement configuration is not sufficiently large relative to the elastic wavelengths. Numerical experiments support the theoretical predictions and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed imaging and reconstruction methods.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Erratum to: Monotonicity of Ursell functions in the Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09151)
*Federico Camia,Jianping Jiang,Charles M. Newman*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文是一则勘误（erratum），针对《Comm. Math. Phys.》401 (2023) 2459–2482 中定理1（Theorem 1）证明中的一处错误予以更正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原论文定理1的证明存在错误，需予纠正以保证结论的可靠性。

Method: 以简注（note）形式给出勘误，修正原证明中的错误之处；摘要未披露具体技术手段。

Result: 摘要未说明修正的具体内容与更正后的证明细节。

Conclusion: 该勘误旨在修正原定理1证明中的错误；摘要未说明定理1的陈述本身是否受影响。

Abstract: In this note, we correct an error in the proof Theorem 1 of Comm. Math. Phys. 401 (2023), 2459-2482.

</details>


### [159] [Singular Vortex in the Large Deviation of Empirical Current for Symmetric Simple Exclusion Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09526)
*Chenjiayue Qi*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文证明二维对称简单排斥过程（symmetric simple exclusion process, SSEP）奇异流可观测量的一个大型偏差（large deviation）估计，其标度因奇异涡旋（singular vortices）而呈非标准的 $N^2(\log N)^{-1}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于奇异流可观测量的非标准大型偏差标度：突破常规 $N^2$ 标度的原因尚需刻画，即奇异涡旋带来的增强贡献（由轨迹绕原点附近转动导致）。

Method: 证明方法综合三类工具：Bodineau–Derrida–Lebowitz 2008 的启发式论证、Jara–Menezes 2018 的平均化方法（averaging method），以及本文新发展的抵消（cancellation）与多尺度重整化（multi-scale renormalization）论证。

Result: 主要结果为二维 SSEP 奇异流可观测量的强大型偏差原理，观测量呈现非标准标度 $N^2(\log N)^{-1}$；此标度源于奇异涡旋使绕原点附近旋转的轨迹对流观测量产生增强贡献。

Conclusion: 研究表明奇异涡旋结构会改变大型偏差标度，本文的抵消与多尺度重整化方法为处理此类奇异可观测量的非标准标度提供了技术路径。

Abstract: We prove a large deviation estimate for singular current observables of the two-dimensional symmetric simple exclusion process. In particular, the observable has a non-standard large deviation scaling $N^2(\log N)^{-1}$ due to the presence of singular vortices, whereby trajectories rotating near the origin point yield an enhanced contribution to the current observable. The proof combines the heuristics of Bodineau-Derrida-Lebowitz 2008, the averaging method of Jara-Menezes 2018, and novel cancellation and multi-scale renormalization arguments developed by this paper.

</details>


### [160] [The Localization Scale of the Block Anderson Model in One Dimension at Arbitrary Coupling and Volume](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09776)
*Jun Yin*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究一维分块安德森模型（one-dimensional block Anderson model），在独立高斯酉系综（Gaussian unitary ensemble, GUE）势与确定性近邻跃迁下，将体态局域化长度（bulk localization length）精确刻画为 min{N, max{W, W²g²}}，并在概率意义下给出高概率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局域化长度估计多针对纯安德森模型，缺少对分块结构（块宽 W 与跃迁-无序比 g）共同作用下局域化长度随系统规模变化的统一刻画；本文旨在填补该空白。

Method: 考虑 L 个分块、总维数 N=WL 的一维模型，块内势为 W×W 独立 GUE 随机矩阵，块间为确定性近邻跃迁，跃迁-无序比为 g；在 W→∞ 极限下，对确定性耦合 g≥0 与环长 L≥3 一致地分析本征向量的质量分布，并以“最短循环区间包含本征向量至少一半质量”定义局域化长度。

Result: 在大 W 极限下，体态局域化长度（至多相差 W 的任意小幂次）等于 min{N, max{W, W²g²}}，对所有 g≥0 与 L≥3 一致成立；对任意固定 D>0 及充分大的 W，所选能量区间内满足该界的本征向量期望占比至少为 1−W^{−D}，且在多项式体积下该界同时对所有本征向量以至少 1−W^{−D} 的概率成立。

Conclusion: 分块结构与跃迁强度共同决定局域化长度的三种标度区制：弱跃迁时由块宽 W 主导、中等跃迁时由 W²g² 主导、强跃迁时受总维数 N 上限约束；结果为分块安德森模型的局域化提供了高概率的定量刻画。

Abstract: We study the one-dimensional block Anderson model with independent $W\times W$ Gaussian unitary ensemble potentials and deterministic nearest-neighbor hopping, with hopping-to-disorder ratio $g$. The system consists of $L$ blocks, with total dimension $N=WL$. As $W\to\infty$, we identify the bulk localization length, up to an arbitrarily small power of $W$, with $\min\{N,\max\{W,W^2g^2\}\}$, uniformly for deterministic couplings $g\ge0$ and cycle lengths $L\ge3$. For every fixed $D>0$ and all sufficiently large $W$, the expected fraction of eigenvectors in the selected energy interval satisfying these bounds is at least $1-W^{-D}$. In polynomial volume the bounds hold simultaneously for all eigenvectors in that interval with probability at least $1-W^{-D}$. The localization length is the number of coordinates in the shortest cyclic interval containing at least half of an eigenvector's mass.

</details>

### [161] [Layer-Peeling Inversion of Temporal Refractive Index Profiles from Noisy Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09630)
*Ohad Silbiger,Yakir Hadad*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种从电磁场测量中直接重建介质时间演化的方法：时间折射率变化序列产生有限空间支撑的响应，结合因果性可借助层剥离的时域扩展依次重构各时间界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏从电磁场测量直接反演介质随时间演化（时变界面序列）的通用方法；论文旨在利用时变介质响应的空间有限性与因果性建立可直接执行的重建框架。

Method: 将时间折射率（refractive index）变化序列视为把入射波分裂为前向与后向传播分量的时间界面（temporal interfaces），利用其有限空间支撑（finite support）与因果性（causality），通过层剥离（layer peeling）的时域扩展（time-domain extension）进行逐层序贯重建；对连续变化介质引入时间调整的 Goupillaud 介质，并借助时空对偶（space-time duality）确立相应的动量守恒（momentum conservation）与可容许性条件（admissibility conditions）。

Result: 该有限支撑加因果性的结构支持对时间界面的序贯重建；所引入的时间调整 Goupillaud 介质与时空对偶分析给出了对应的动量守恒与可容许性条件；方法在测量不完整且含噪声（incomplete and noisy measurements）的情况下仍保持稳健。

Conclusion: 该方法为时变电磁系统（time-varying electromagnetic systems）的计量（metrology）、表征与设计提供了通用框架，并适用于不完整与含噪测量条件。

Abstract: We introduce a direct method for reconstructing the temporal evolution of a medium from electromagnetic field measurements. A sequence of temporal refractive-index changes splits an incident wave into forward- and backward-propagating components, producing a \emph{spatial} response of finite extent. We show that this finite support, together with causality, enables sequential reconstruction of the temporal interfaces through a time-domain extension of layer peeling. For continuously varying media, we introduce a time-adjusted Goupillaud medium and, through space-time duality, establish the corresponding momentum-conservation and admissibility conditions. The method remains robust under incomplete and noisy measurements, providing a general framework for the metrology, characterization, and design of time-varying electromagnetic systems.

</details>


### [162] [Experimental demonstration of dual-polarization nonreciprocal thermal emission at moderate magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07564)
*Mason Christiansen,Jae S. Hwang,Yi Jie Lin,Aryan Zaveri,Tianjun Li,Daniel Cui,Baolai Liang,Aaswath P. Raman*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作通过锗米氏共振超表面与掺杂InAs层结合，在单一结构中实现了同一波长、同一角度下TE与TM双偏振的非互易吸收，在0.48 T永磁体磁场下于16.6 μm处得到±B吸收率差约0.035。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热辐射器受基尔霍夫定律（Kirchhoff's law）约束，发射率与吸收率必须相等，限制了辐射能量技术与热传递。磁光半导体（magneto-optic semiconductors）可在外加磁场下打破洛伦兹互易性（Lorentz reciprocity），但既往实验中的非互易性多局限于TM偏振，因TE偏振光的电场平行于外加磁场而难以响应。

Method: 采用掺杂InAs层上方的锗米氏共振超表面（Mie-resonant metasurface）结构，将TE偏振光散射为含垂直于磁场分量的近场，使InAs的磁光响应得以对其起作用；测试在Halbach永磁阵列提供的0.48 T磁场下进行。

Result: 在16.6 μm波长处，结构在TE与TM偏振下于+B与−B之间均表现出0.035的吸收率差异，即在两种偏振、相同波长与角度上同时实现非互易吸收（nonreciprocal absorption）。

Conclusion: 平面半导体的磁光响应与介质超表面共振可协同设计，为在永磁体可提供磁场水平下实现双偏振非互易性（dual-polarization nonreciprocity）提供了可行路径，有望服务于新兴能量与热传递技术。

Abstract: Conventional thermal emitters obey Kirchhoff's law of thermal radiation, which requires emissivity and absorptivity to be equal at each wavelength, angle, and polarization. This in turn places a fundamental limit on a range of radiative energy technologies as well as heat transfer. This constraint can however be overcome using magneto-optic semiconductors, which break Lorentz reciprocity under an applied magnetic field. However, in most prior experimental demonstrations, nonreciprocity has been restricted to TM polarization, since the electric field of TE-polarized light is parallel to the applied magnetic field. Here, we experimentally demonstrate nonreciprocal absorption in both polarizations at the same wavelengths and angles from a single structure, wherein a germanium Mie-resonant metasurface on a doped InAs layer scatters TE-polarized light into near fields with components perpendicular to the magnetic field, allowing the magneto-optic response of the InAs to act on them. Under a 0.48 T field from a permanent Halbach array, this structure exhibits a difference in absorptivity between $+B$ and $-B$ of 0.035 in both TE and TM polarization at 16.6 $μ$m. Our approach shows that the magneto-optic response of a planar semiconductor and the resonances of a dielectric metasurface can be designed together, offering a potential route to dual-polarization nonreciprocity at fields available from permanent magnets for emerging energy and heat transfer technologies.

</details>


### [163] [Deep-UV All-dielectric qBIC Metasurface for Refractive Index Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08733)
*Beáta Idesová,Alexander A. Antonov,Lina Rohrer,Leonardo de Souza Menezes,Andreas Tittl,Tomáš Šikola,Filip Ligmajer*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作实验演示了基于二氧化铪（HfO₂）的全介质超表面（all-dielectric metasurface），在深紫外（deep-ultraviolet, DUV）225–340 nm波段支持准连续域束缚态（quasi-bound states in the continuum, qBICs），品质因子（quality factor）达500，并实现约60 nm/RIU的折射率灵敏度与40 RIU⁻¹的品质因数（figure of merit）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有支持qBICs的全介质超表面多局限于可见与近红外波段，将其拓展至DUV可探测生物分子的本征电子吸收，但面临材料与纳米加工（nanofabrication）方面的显著挑战。

Method: 采用HfO₂构建全介质超表面以支持DUV qBICs，并与微流控池（microfluidic cell）集成，通过反射模式（reflection mode）进行光学表征与折射率传感测试。

Result: 在225–340 nm范围内实现品质因子达500的qBIC共振；引入水相缓冲液（aqueous buffer）后品质因子进一步提升；灵敏度最高约60 nm/RIU，工作品质因数为40 RIU⁻¹。

Conclusion: 该低损耗（low-loss）HfO₂超表面为无标记（label-free）DUV生物传感提供了稳健基础。

Abstract: All-dielectric metasurfaces supporting quasi-bound states in the continuum (qBICs) have emerged as powerful platforms for biosensing, yet their operation remains largely concentrated in the visible and near-infrared regimes. Extending these resonances into the deep-ultraviolet (DUV) to probe the intrinsic electronic absorption of biomolecules presents substantial material and nanofabrication challenges. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a hafnium dioxide (HfO$_{2}$) metasurface supporting DUV qBICs in the wavelength range from 225 nm to 340 nm with quality factors reaching 500. We further demonstrate its refractive index sensing capabilities through integration with a microfluidic cell and optical characterization in reflection mode. We show that the introduction of an aqueous buffer leads to a further quality factor enhancement. Achieving a sensitivity of up to approximately 60 nm/RIU and an operational figure of merit of 40 $\mathrm{RIU}^{-1}$, this low-loss HfO$_{2}$ metasurface provides a robust foundation for label-free DUV biosensing.

</details>

### [164] [Suppression of gel formation by spontaneous fragmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09537)
*Sergey Matveev*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 研究带乘性核（multiplicative kernel）与恒定速率自发碎裂项的离散 Smoluchowski 方程，证明 λ≥4 时碎裂抑制凝胶化（gelation），并给出二阶矩与单体浓度的解析表达式及稳态分布的机器精度递推算法，经 FFT + 自适应二阶 Runge–Kutta 数值模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘性核下的 Smoluchowski 方程通常会出现凝胶化（gelation）这一非守恒奇异性，而实际颗粒体系中常伴随碎裂过程；因此需明确碎裂项对凝胶化现象的抑制条件及其定量刻画方式。

Method: 在离散 Smoluchowski 方程中引入速率为 λ 的自发碎裂项（每个粒子以恒定速率碎裂为单体），推导二阶矩与单体浓度 n₁(t) 的解析表达式，并据此建立可计算稳态粒度分布的递推关系；数值上采用 FFT 计算右端项并结合自适应步长二阶 Runge–Kutta 方法进行验证。

Result: 当 λ≥4 时，碎裂的引入抑制了凝胶化；所得解析与半解析结果（含稳态粒度分布的机器精度计算）通过数值模拟得到验证。

Conclusion: 恒定速率自发碎裂足以消除乘性核 Smoluchowski 方程的凝胶化转变，且该体系的二阶矩、单体浓度与稳态分布均可用解析或高精度递推方式刻画，为相关工作提供了可验证的定量框架。

Abstract: In this work we study the discrete Smoluchowski equation with a multiplicative kernel and an additional term describing the spontaneous shattering of each particle into monomers at a constant rate $λ$. We show that the introduction of fragmentation suppresses gelation for $λ\ge 4$. We derive analytical expressions for the second moment and the monomer concentration $n_1(t)$. These expressions lead to a recurrence relation allowing the steady-state particle size distribution for $λ\ge 4$ to be calculated with machine precision. We validate our analytical and semi-analytical results via numerical simulations using a solver that employs the Fast Fourier Transform for computation of the right-hand side and a second-order Runge-Kutta method with adaptive time steps.

</details>
